# Exvius Specification

> Generated by openlore v1.2.0 on 2026-11-07 15:24

## Purpose

Prepares and packages an offline-enabled Android build by modifying application code and
configuration, assembling runtime assets, and updating the APK.
The native iOS launcher also dispatches explicit connection, campaign and
preparation commands through the dedicated Python runtime.

## Requirements

### Requirement: DispatchNativePreparationThroughIsolatedRuntime

The native iOS launcher SHALL reuse the prepared .venv-ios interpreter for
summon/home artwork and sound preparation or verification, and plan-data,
fetch-data and verify-packs, including from a different Python installation. It SHALL forward arguments and
delegate command handling to the iOS CLI without installing dependencies or
initializing a campaign. CLI commands SHALL emit one JSON result document.

- **Implementation**: `main::source/ios_server/cli.py`

#### Scenario: Prepare sound from the base interpreter

- Given the dedicated iOS runtime and a separate invoking Python interpreter
- When macos_ios_server.py receives prepare-sounds or verify-sounds
- Then it runs the selected command through the dedicated runtime
- And preserves the supplied private root and shared data paths.

#### Scenario: Install packs from the base interpreter

- Given the dedicated runtime and a local manifest with cached ZIPs
- When the base interpreter invokes plan-data, fetch-data or verify-packs
- Then the dedicated runtime handles the command and emits one JSON result
- And supplied manifest, archive, data and private runtime paths are preserved.

> `source/exvius/android_build.py`

### Requirement: BuildAndroid

The system SHALL the build process shall prepare offline code and assets, assemble the android package, and apply the required manifest and configuration changes. it must report errors when required inputs are invalid or build steps fail.

#### Scenario: BuildWithValidInputs
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The required project files, build tools, and inputs are available
- **WHEN** main is invoked with valid arguments
- **THEN** The offline application code and assets are prepared and the Android build artifacts are updated

#### Scenario: InvalidOrMissingBuildInput
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A required input is missing, invalid, or fails validation
- **WHEN** main is invoked
- **THEN** The build reports an error and does not complete successfully

#### Scenario: BuildToolFailure
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A required external build command fails
- **WHEN** main runs the build process
- **THEN** The failure is reported and the build does not report success


## Sub-components

> `AndroidBuild` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: ValueError

> Implements: `ValueError`

Represents an invalid value detected during Android build preparation.

#### Requirement: ReportInvalidValue

The system SHALL the build process shall raise a valueerror when it encounters a value that violates an expected constraint.

#### Scenario: InvalidValue
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A build input violates an expected constraint
- **WHEN** The invalid value is handled
- **THEN** A ValueError is raised

### Sub-component: PrepareOfflineCode

> Implements: `prepare_offline_code`

Prepares application source code for offline operation and records the resulting changes.

#### Requirement: PrepareOfflineSource

The system SHALL the build process shall apply the required offline code changes to the provided client and report the changes made.

#### Scenario: OfflineChangesApplied
- **GIVEN** The client source contains supported methods requiring offline changes
- **WHEN** Offline code preparation runs
- **THEN** The applicable changes are applied and reported

### Sub-component: Path

> Implements: `Path`

Represents filesystem paths used during build preparation.

#### Requirement: RepresentFilesystemPath

The system SHALL the build process shall use a path value to identify a filesystem location.

#### Scenario: PathProvided
- **GIVEN** A filesystem path value is available
- **WHEN** A Path is constructed
- **THEN** A path object represents that location

### Sub-component: FileNotFoundError

> Implements: `FileNotFoundError`

Represents a required file or directory that cannot be found.

#### Requirement: ReportMissingFile

The system SHALL the build process shall report a filenotfounderror when a required filesystem entry is missing.

#### Scenario: RequiredEntryMissing
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A required filesystem entry does not exist
- **WHEN** The build process accesses it
- **THEN** A FileNotFoundError is raised

### Sub-component: FileExistsError

> Implements: `FileExistsError`

Represents a filesystem conflict caused by an existing entry.

#### Requirement: ReportExistingFile

The system SHALL the build process shall report a fileexistserror when an operation requires a destination that must not already exist.

#### Scenario: DestinationAlreadyExists
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A destination that must be new already exists
- **WHEN** The build process creates it
- **THEN** A FileExistsError is raised

### Sub-component: Any

Determines whether at least one item in an iterable satisfies a condition.

#### Requirement: CheckAnyCondition

The system SHALL the build process shall treat the result as true when at least one item satisfies the supplied condition.

#### Scenario: OneItemMatches
- **GIVEN** At least one item satisfies the condition
- **WHEN** The iterable is checked
- **THEN** The result is true

> Implements: `activity.findall`

Finds activity descendants matching an XML path expression.

#### Requirement: FindActivityDescendants

The system SHALL the build process shall return activity descendants matching the requested xml path expression.

#### Scenario: MatchingActivityDescendants
- **GIVEN** Activity descendants match the expression
- **WHEN** They are searched
- **THEN** The matching elements are returned

### Sub-component: GetCategoryAttribute

> Implements: `category.get`

Retrieves a requested attribute from a category element.

#### Requirement: ReadCategoryAttribute

The system SHALL the build process shall retrieve the requested attribute value from the category element.

#### Scenario: CategoryAttributePresent
- **GIVEN** The category contains the requested attribute
- **WHEN** The attribute is retrieved
- **THEN** Its value is returned

### Sub-component: FindIntentElements

> Implements: `intent.findall`

Finds intent descendants matching an XML path expression.

#### Requirement: FindIntentDescendants

The system SHALL the build process shall return intent descendants matching the requested xml path expression.

#### Scenario: MatchingIntentDescendants
- **GIVEN** Intent descendants match the expression
- **WHEN** They are searched
- **THEN** The matching elements are returned

### Sub-component: RemoveActivityElement

> Implements: `activity.remove`

Removes a child element from an activity element.

#### Requirement: RemoveActivityChild

The system SHALL the build process shall remove the specified child element from the activity element.

#### Scenario: ActivityChildPresent
- **GIVEN** The specified child belongs to the activity element
- **WHEN** It is removed
- **THEN** The activity element no longer contains that child

### Sub-component: WriteManifestTree

> Implements: `tree.write`

Writes the updated XML tree to its output destination.

#### Requirement: PersistManifestTree

The system SHALL the build process shall write the xml tree to the requested destination.

#### Scenario: TreeReadyForOutput
- **GIVEN** The manifest tree has been updated
- **WHEN** It is written
- **THEN** The destination contains the XML document

### Sub-component: ReadConfigText

> Implements: `config.read_text`

Reads configuration file contents as text.

#### Requirement: ReadConfiguration

The system SHALL the build process shall read the configuration file as text.

#### Scenario: ConfigurationAvailable
- **GIVEN** The configuration file exists
- **WHEN** Its text is read
- **THEN** The configuration contents are returned

### Sub-component: SubstituteConfigPattern

> Implements: `re.sub`

Replaces matches of a regular expression in configuration text.

#### Requirement: ReplaceConfigurationPattern

The system SHALL the build process shall replace text matching the specified pattern with the requested replacement.

#### Scenario: PatternMatches
- **GIVEN** The configuration text contains a pattern match
- **WHEN** The substitution is applied
- **THEN** The matching text is replaced

### Sub-component: ReplaceText

> Implements: `text.replace`

Replaces occurrences of specified text in a string.

#### Requirement: ReplaceStringContent

The system SHALL the build process shall replace occurrences of the requested substring with its replacement.

#### Scenario: SubstringPresent
- **GIVEN** The text contains the requested substring
- **WHEN** It is replaced
- **THEN** The updated text contains the replacement

### Sub-component: WriteConfigText

> Implements: `config.write_text`

Writes configuration text to its file.

#### Requirement: PersistConfiguration

The system SHALL the build process shall write the supplied text to the configuration file.

#### Scenario: ConfigurationUpdated
- **GIVEN** Updated configuration text is available
- **WHEN** It is written
- **THEN** The configuration file contains the updated text

### Sub-component: Read

> Implements: `read`

Reads data identified by a name from the enclosing data source.

#### Requirement: ReadNamedData

The system SHALL the build process shall return the data associated with the supplied name.

#### Scenario: NamedDataAvailable
- **GIVEN** Data is available under the supplied name
- **WHEN** The data is read
- **THEN** The associated data is returned

> `source/exvius/android_display.js`

### Requirement: ChooseResolution

The system SHALL the display module shall select a valid resolution policy and dimensions that fit the device frame while preserving the source aspect ratio.

- **Implementation**: `chooseResolution::source/exvius/android_display.js`

#### Scenario: ValidFrameDimensions
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Source and frame dimensions are finite and positive
- **WHEN** chooseResolution is called
- **THEN** A resolution policy, fitted dimensions, and scale are returned

#### Scenario: InvalidDimensions
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** At least one dimension is non-finite or not positive
- **WHEN** chooseResolution is called
- **THEN** Null is returned

### Requirement: ClampAnimationFrame

The system SHALL the display module shall cap an animation frame at the last available frame when the frame count is positive.

- **Implementation**: `clampAnimationFrame::source/exvius/android_display.js`

#### Scenario: FrameExceedsAvailableRange
- **GIVEN** The frame count is positive and the frame is at least the count
- **WHEN** clampAnimationFrame is called
- **THEN** The last available frame index is returned

#### Scenario: FrameIsWithinRange
- **GIVEN** The frame is below the count or the count is not positive
- **WHEN** clampAnimationFrame is called
- **THEN** The input frame is returned unchanged

### Requirement: IsTallList

The system SHALL the display module shall identify scrollable or list layouts that should expand on taller displays, excluding dialogs and configured exceptions.

- **Implementation**: `isTallList::source/exvius/android_display.js`

#### Scenario: EligibleTallList
- **GIVEN** A non-excluded list-like layout overlaps the configured lower screen region and is sufficiently tall
- **WHEN** isTallList is called
- **THEN** True is returned

#### Scenario: ExcludedOrShortLayout
- **GIVEN** A layout is excluded by name or does not meet the size and position criteria
- **WHEN** isTallList is called
- **THEN** False is returned

### Requirement: CollapseCampaignBanner

The system SHALL the display module shall move eligible layouts upward by the campaign banner height and extend tall lists to preserve usable space.

- **Implementation**: `collapseCampaignBanner::source/exvius/android_display.js`

#### Scenario: BannerCanBeCollapsed
- **GIVEN** A banner exists and the layout is eligible to move
- **WHEN** collapseCampaignBanner is called
- **THEN** The layout is shifted upward by the banner height and tall lists are extended by that height

#### Scenario: LayoutIsIneligible
- **GIVEN** No banner exists or the layout is excluded by its position or name
- **WHEN** collapseCampaignBanner is called
- **THEN** The original layout bounds are returned

### Requirement: ResponsiveDelta

The system SHALL the display module shall calculate horizontal centering and vertical expansion offsets between reference and canvas dimensions.

- **Implementation**: `responsiveDelta::source/exvius/android_display.js`

#### Scenario: CanvasDiffersFromReference
- **GIVEN** Reference and canvas dimensions are provided
- **WHEN** responsiveDelta is called
- **THEN** Horizontal centering and vertical height differences are returned

### Requirement: AnchorLayout

The system SHALL the display module shall adjust layout bounds according to the layout's role so interface elements remain positioned or stretched appropriately on the canvas.

- **Implementation**: `anchorLayout::source/exvius/android_display.js`

#### Scenario: ResponsiveLayoutRole
<!-- openlore-test: priority=high (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A layout and reference and canvas dimensions are provided
- **WHEN** anchorLayout is called
- **THEN** Bounds are adjusted according to the layout role and responsive size differences

#### Scenario: IntrinsicLayout
- **GIVEN** The layout has no special anchoring or stretching role
- **WHEN** anchorLayout is called
- **THEN** The original bounds are returned

### Requirement: ClampMapScroll

The system SHALL the display module shall constrain map scroll coordinates to the valid area determined by map and viewport dimensions.

- **Implementation**: `clampMapScroll::source/exvius/android_display.js`

#### Scenario: ScrollExceedsMapBounds
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Requested scroll coordinates extend beyond the map viewport limits
- **WHEN** clampMapScroll is called
- **THEN** Coordinates are limited to valid map bounds

#### Scenario: ScrollIsWithinMapBounds
- **GIVEN** Requested scroll coordinates are within the valid map area
- **WHEN** clampMapScroll is called
- **THEN** The coordinates remain unchanged

### Requirement: InstallAdaptiveDisplay

The system SHALL the display module shall install runtime hooks that apply adaptive resolution, layout, banner, animation, and map display behavior to the game.

- **Implementation**: `installAdaptiveDisplay::source/exvius/android_display.js`

#### Scenario: HooksInstalled
- **GIVEN** The runtime module and required callbacks are available
- **WHEN** installAdaptiveDisplay is called
- **THEN** Adaptive display hooks are attached and retained, and relevant adjustments are reported

#### Scenario: HookEncountersAnError
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A runtime hook encounters an error while adapting display behavior
- **WHEN** The hook handles the failure
- **THEN** The error is caught and the module can report it through the provided reporting callback


## Sub-components

> `AdaptiveDisplay` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: Native

> Implements: `module.getExportByName`

Resolves a named native export from the module.

#### Requirement: ResolveExport

The system SHALL the block shall resolve the requested export by name from the module.

#### Scenario: ExportFound
- **GIVEN** The module exposes the requested export
- **WHEN** The block resolves the export
- **THEN** The matching native export is returned

### Sub-component: RetainedPush

> Implements: `retained.push`

Retains a value for later use.

#### Requirement: RetainValue

The system SHALL the block shall add the supplied value to the retained collection.

#### Scenario: ValueRetained
- **GIVEN** A value and retained collection are available
- **WHEN** The block retains the value
- **THEN** The value is present in the collection

### Sub-component: InterceptorAttach

> Implements: `Interceptor.attach`

Installs an interceptor for a target function.

#### Requirement: AttachInterceptor

The system SHALL the block shall attach the configured callbacks to the target function.

#### Scenario: TargetIntercepted
- **GIVEN** A target function and callbacks are configured
- **WHEN** The block attaches the interceptor
- **THEN** Calls to the target are observed by the callbacks

### Sub-component: GetExportByName

> Implements: `module.getExportByName`

Resolves a named native export from the module.

#### Requirement: ResolveExport

The system SHALL the block shall resolve the requested export by name from the module.

#### Scenario: ExportFound
- **GIVEN** The module exposes the requested export
- **WHEN** The block resolves the export
- **THEN** The matching native export is returned

### Sub-component: EmptyEmblemBanner

> Implements: `emptyEmblemBanner`

Processes emblem banner layout data and applies the appropriate display layout.

#### Requirement: ProcessEmblemBanner

The system SHALL the block shall inspect available scene and layout data and apply the matching emblem banner layout when data is usable.

#### Scenario: UsableLayoutFound
- **GIVEN** The scene contains valid banner data and a matching layout
- **WHEN** The block processes the emblem banner
- **THEN** The matching layout is applied

### Sub-component: Note

> Implements: `note`

Records a diagnostic note about display processing.

#### Requirement: RecordNote

The system SHALL the block should record the supplied diagnostic note.

#### Scenario: NoteRecorded
- **GIVEN** A diagnostic note is supplied
- **WHEN** The block records the note
- **THEN** The note is available for diagnostics

### Sub-component: OriginalEmblemBanner

> Implements: `originalEmblemBanner`

Preserves or invokes the original emblem banner behavior.

#### Requirement: PreserveOriginalBanner

The system SHALL the block shall preserve the original emblem banner behavior for invocation.

#### Scenario: OriginalBehaviorInvoked
- **GIVEN** Banner arguments are available
- **WHEN** The original behavior is invoked
- **THEN** Its result is returned

### Sub-component: ReplaceFast

> Implements: `Interceptor.replaceFast`

Replaces a target function with a fast replacement implementation.

#### Requirement: ReplaceTarget

The system SHALL the block shall replace the target function with the supplied implementation.

#### Scenario: ReplacementInstalled
- **GIVEN** A valid target and replacement are provided
- **WHEN** The block replaces the target
- **THEN** The replacement handle is returned

### Sub-component: GetFrame

> Implements: `getFrame`

Obtains the frame dimensions used for display adaptation.

#### Requirement: ReadFrame

The system SHALL the block shall provide the current frame dimensions for subsequent resolution decisions.

#### Scenario: FrameAvailable
- **GIVEN** Display state contains frame information
- **WHEN** The block obtains the frame
- **THEN** Frame dimensions are returned

### Sub-component: ChooseResolution

> Implements: `chooseResolution`

Selects a display resolution compatible with the device and frame dimensions.

#### Requirement: SelectResolution

The system SHALL the block shall choose a resolution using the supplied device and frame dimensions.

#### Scenario: ResolutionSelected
- **GIVEN** Positive device and frame dimensions are supplied
- **WHEN** The block chooses a resolution
- **THEN** A resolution suitable for those dimensions is returned

Reads a floating-point frame dimension.

- **WHEN** The block reads the value
- **THEN** The float is returned

Calculates an address within frame data.

- **THEN** The corresponding address is returned

> `source/exvius/android_offline.py`

### Requirement: WriteChangedFile

The system SHALL the module shall write updated text to a file when its contents differ from the current file.

- **Implementation**: `write_changed::source/exvius/android_offline.py`

#### Scenario: ChangedContent
- **GIVEN** A file exists with content different from the supplied text
- **WHEN** write_changed is called
- **THEN** The file is updated with the supplied text

#### Scenario: UnchangedContent
- **GIVEN** A file already contains the supplied text
- **WHEN** write_changed is called
- **THEN** The file is left unchanged

### Requirement: ReplaceSourceMethod

The system SHALL the module shall replace a matching method implementation in source text and report an error if the method signature cannot be found or replaced.

- **Implementation**: `replace_method::source/exvius/android_offline.py`

#### Scenario: MatchingMethod
- **GIVEN** Source text contains the requested method signature
- **WHEN** replace_method is called with a replacement body
- **THEN** The method body in the returned source is replaced

#### Scenario: MissingMethod
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Source text does not contain the requested method signature
- **WHEN** replace_method is called
- **THEN** A ValueError is raised

### Requirement: PrepareOfflineClientCode

The system SHALL the module shall prepare the android client source tree for offline operation by applying the required source transformations and recording changed files.

- **Implementation**: `prepare_offline_code::source/exvius/android_offline.py`

#### Scenario: PrepareClient
- **GIVEN** A client source tree is available
- **WHEN** prepare_offline_code is called
- **THEN** The required offline transformations are applied and changed files are written

#### Scenario: RequiredSourcePatternMissing
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A required method or source pattern is absent
- **WHEN** prepare_offline_code is called
- **THEN** The preparation fails with a ValueError


## Sub-components

> `OfflineCodePreparation` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: ValueError

> Implements: `ValueError`

Represents an error raised when a requested source transformation cannot be completed.

#### Requirement: SignalTransformationFailure

The system SHALL the block must signal a value error when a requested transformation cannot be applied.

#### Scenario: UnapplicableTransformation
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A source transformation cannot be applied to the provided text.
- **WHEN** The failure is reported.
- **THEN** A value error is raised.

### Sub-component: ReplaceMethod

> Implements: `replace_method`

Replaces a method body in source text using the supplied signature and body.

#### Requirement: ReplaceMatchingMethod

The system SHALL the block shall replace the body associated with the supplied method signature and return the updated source text.

#### Scenario: MatchingMethodExists
- **GIVEN** The source text contains a method matching the supplied signature.
- **WHEN** The block replaces the method using the supplied body.
- **THEN** The returned text contains the new body for that method.

#### Requirement: RejectMissingMethod

The system SHALL the block must signal failure when the supplied signature does not identify a replaceable method.

#### Scenario: MethodNotFound
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The source text contains no method matching the supplied signature.
- **WHEN** The block attempts the replacement.
- **THEN** A value error is raised.

### Sub-component: WriteChanged

> Implements: `write_changed`

Writes text to a path only when its contents differ from the existing file.

#### Requirement: WriteChangedContent

The system SHALL the block shall write the supplied text when the path does not exist or its contents differ.

#### Scenario: ContentsDiffer
- **GIVEN** The path exists and its current contents differ from the supplied text.
- **WHEN** The block writes the changed content.
- **THEN** The file contains the supplied text.

#### Scenario: PathDoesNotExist
- **GIVEN** The path does not exist.
- **WHEN** The block writes the supplied text.
- **THEN** The path contains the supplied text.

#### Requirement: SkipUnchangedContent

The system SHALL the block should avoid writing when the existing file already contains the supplied text.

#### Scenario: ContentsMatch
- **GIVEN** The path exists and its current contents equal the supplied text.
- **WHEN** The block checks whether a write is needed.
- **THEN** The file is not rewritten.

### Sub-component: Path

> Implements: `Path`

Provides path values used to locate source and destination files.

#### Requirement: RepresentFilesystemPath

The system SHALL the block shall represent a filesystem location that can be used by path operations.

#### Scenario: ConstructPath
- **GIVEN** A filesystem location is provided.
- **WHEN** The path value is created.
- **THEN** The value identifies that location for subsequent filesystem operations.

### Sub-component: DestinationExists

> Implements: `destination.exists`

Determines whether the destination location already exists.

#### Requirement: CheckDestinationPresence

The system SHALL the block shall report whether the destination exists.

#### Scenario: DestinationPresent
- **GIVEN** The destination location exists.
- **WHEN** Its presence is checked.
- **THEN** The result indicates that it exists.

#### Scenario: DestinationAbsent
- **GIVEN** The destination location does not exist.
- **WHEN** Its presence is checked.
- **THEN** The result indicates that it does not exist.

### Sub-component: CopyTree

> Implements: `shutil.copytree`

Copies a source directory tree to a destination location.

#### Requirement: CopyDirectoryTree

The system SHALL the block shall copy the source directory tree to the requested destination.

#### Scenario: CopySourceTree
- **GIVEN** A source directory tree is available and the destination can be created.
- **WHEN** The tree is copied.
- **THEN** The destination contains the source tree's files and directory structure.

### Sub-component: FindAll

> Implements: `re.findall`

Finds all matches of a pattern in source text.

#### Requirement: CollectPatternMatches

The system SHALL the block shall return all matches of the supplied pattern in the searched text.

#### Scenario: MultipleMatches
- **GIVEN** The searched text contains multiple matches for the pattern.
- **WHEN** All matches are collected.
- **THEN** The result contains each match in source order.

#### Scenario: NoMatches
- **GIVEN** The searched text contains no match for the pattern.
- **WHEN** All matches are collected.
- **THEN** The result is empty.

### Sub-component: SignatureEndsWith

> Implements: `signature.endswith`

Checks whether a signature ends with a specified suffix.

#### Requirement: CheckSignatureSuffix

The system SHALL the block shall indicate whether the signature ends with the specified suffix.

#### Scenario: SuffixMatches
- **GIVEN** The signature ends with the specified suffix.
- **WHEN** The suffix is checked.
- **THEN** The result indicates a match.

#### Scenario: SuffixDoesNotMatch
- **GIVEN** The signature does not end with the specified suffix.
- **WHEN** The suffix is checked.
- **THEN** The result indicates no match.

### Sub-component: SetDefaultChange

> Implements: `changes.setdefault`

Adds a default entry for a change key when no entry is present.

#### Requirement: EnsureChangeEntry

The system SHALL the block shall provide the default value for a key only when that key is absent.

#### Scenario: ChangeKeyAbsent
- **GIVEN** The changes collection has no entry for a key.
- **WHEN** A default is set for that key.
- **THEN** The key is associated with the default value.

#### Scenario: ChangeKeyPresent
- **GIVEN** The changes collection already has an entry for a key.
- **WHEN** A default is set for that key.
- **THEN** The existing entry is preserved.

### Sub-component: ChangeItems

> Implements: `changes.items`

Provides the key-value entries in the changes collection.

#### Requirement: EnumerateChanges

The system SHALL the block shall expose the change entries as key-value pairs.

#### Scenario: CollectionHasChanges
- **GIVEN** The changes collection contains entries.
- **WHEN** Its items are requested.
- **THEN** The result includes the collection's key-value pairs.

### Sub-component: MethodItems

> Implements: `methods.items`

Provides the key-value entries in the methods collection.

#### Requirement: EnumerateMethods

The system SHALL the block shall expose the method entries as key-value pairs.

#### Scenario: CollectionHasMethods
- **GIVEN** The methods collection contains entries.
- **WHEN** Its items are requested.
- **THEN** The result includes the collection's key-value pairs.

### Sub-component: SearchPattern

> Implements: `re.search`

Finds the first match of a pattern in source text.

#### Requirement: FindFirstMatch

The system SHALL the block shall return the first match of the supplied pattern or indicate that no match exists.

#### Scenario: PatternFound
- **GIVEN** The searched text contains a match for the pattern.
- **WHEN** The pattern is searched.
- **THEN** The first match is returned.

#### Scenario: PatternNotFound
- **GIVEN** The searched text contains no match for the pattern.
- **WHEN** The pattern is searched.
- **THEN** The result indicates no match.

### Sub-component: ActivityStartGroup

> Implements: `activity_start.group`

Retrieves a captured group from the activity start match.

#### Requirement: RetrieveActivityStartGroup

The system SHALL the block shall return the requested captured group from the activity start match.

#### Scenario: GroupCaptured
- **GIVEN** The activity start match contains the requested capture group.
- **WHEN** The group is retrieved.
- **THEN** The captured value is returned.

### Sub-component: SubstituteMatches

> Implements: `re.subn`

Replaces pattern matches in text and reports the number of substitutions.

#### Requirement: ReplacePatternMatches

The system SHALL the block shall return text with pattern matches replaced and the count of replacements made.

#### Scenario: MatchesReplaced
- **GIVEN** The text contains one or more matches for the pattern.
- **WHEN** The substitutions are applied.
- **THEN** The matching text is replaced and the reported count equals the number of replacements.

#### Scenario: NoMatchesReplaced
- **GIVEN** The text contains no matches for the pattern.
- **WHEN** The substitutions are applied.
- **THEN** The text is unchanged and the reported count is zero.

### Sub-component: EscapePattern

> Implements: `re.escape`

Escapes a string so it can be matched as literal text in a regular expression.

#### Requirement: EscapeLiteralText

The system SHALL the block shall produce a pattern that treats special characters in the input as literals.

#### Scenario: InputHasPatternCharacters
- **GIVEN** The input contains characters with special meaning in regular expressions.
- **WHEN** The input is escaped.
- **THEN** Those characters are treated literally when the result is used as a pattern.

### Sub-component: ActivityStartStart

> Implements: `activity_start.start`

Retrieves the starting position of the activity start match.

#### Requirement: RetrieveMatchStart

The system SHALL the block shall return the starting position of the activity start match in the searched text.

#### Scenario: MatchHasStartPosition
- **GIVEN** An activity start match exists in the searched text.
- **WHEN** Its start position is retrieved.
- **THEN** The position of the first character in the match is returned.

### Sub-component: ActivityStartEnd

> Implements: `activity_start.end`

Retrieves the ending position of the activity start match.

#### Requirement: RetrieveMatchEnd

The system SHALL the block shall return the ending position of the activity start match in the searched text.

#### Scenario: MatchHasEndPosition
- **GIVEN** An activity start match exists in the searched text.
- **WHEN** Its end position is retrieved.
- **THEN** The position immediately after the last character in the match is returned.

### Sub-component: ReadSource

> Implements: `source.read_text`

Reads source text from the source path.

#### Requirement: LoadSourceText

The system SHALL the block shall return the text contents of the source path.

#### Scenario: SourceFileReadable
- **GIVEN** The source path identifies a readable text file.
- **WHEN** Its text is read.
- **THEN** The file contents are returned.

### Sub-component: ThirdExists

> Implements: `third.exists`

Determines whether the third path exists.

#### Requirement: CheckThirdPathPresence

The system SHALL the block shall report whether the third path exists.

#### Scenario: ThirdPathPresent
- **GIVEN** The third path exists.
- **WHEN** Its presence is checked.
- **THEN** The result indicates that it exists.

#### Scenario: ThirdPathAbsent
- **GIVEN** The third path does not exist.
- **WHEN** Its presence is checked.
- **THEN** The result indicates that it does not exist.

### Sub-component: RelativeTo

> Implements: `source.relative_to`

Expresses the source path relative to a specified base path.

#### Requirement: DeriveRelativeSourcePath

The system SHALL the block shall return the source path relative to the supplied base path when it is within that base.

#### Scenario: SourceWithinBase
- **GIVEN** The source path is contained by the supplied base path.
- **WHEN** The relative path is derived.
- **THEN** The result identifies the source relative to that base.

### Sub-component: MatchGroup

> Implements: `match.group`

Retrieves a captured group from a match.

#### Requirement: RetrieveCapturedGroup

The system SHALL the block shall return the requested captured group from the match.

#### Scenario: RequestedGroupCaptured
- **GIVEN** The match contains the requested capture group.
- **WHEN** The group is retrieved.
- **THEN** The captured value is returned.

### Sub-component: ReplaceSizingText

> Implements: `sizing.replace`

Replaces occurrences of a specified substring in sizing text.

#### Requirement: ReplaceSizingSubstring

The system SHALL the block shall return sizing text with the specified substring replaced.

#### Scenario: SizingContainsSubstring
- **GIVEN** The sizing text contains the specified substring.
- **WHEN** The substring is replaced.
- **THEN** The returned text contains the replacement in place of each occurrence.

### Sub-component: MatchStart

> Implements: `match.start`

Retrieves the starting position of a match.

#### Requirement: RetrieveMatchStartPosition

The system SHALL the block shall return the starting position of the match in the searched text.

#### Scenario: MatchPositionRequested
- **GIVEN** A match exists in the searched text.
- **WHEN** Its start position is retrieved.
- **THEN** The position of the first character in the match is returned.

### Sub-component: MatchEnd

> Implements: `match.end`

Retrieves the ending position of a match.

#### Requirement: RetrieveMatchEndPosition

The system SHALL the block shall return the ending position of the match in the searched text.

#### Scenario: MatchEndPositionRequested
- **GIVEN** A match exists in the searched text.
- **WHEN** Its end position is retrieved.
- **THEN** The position immediately after the last character in the match is returned.

### Sub-component: ReplaceBodyText

> Implements: `body.replace`

Replaces occurrences of a specified substring in a method body.

#### Requirement: ReplaceBodySubstring

The system SHALL the block shall return method body text with the specified substring replaced.

#### Scenario: BodyContainsSubstring
- **GIVEN** The method body contains the specified substring.
- **WHEN** The substring is replaced.
- **THEN** The returned body contains the replacement in place of each occurrence.

### Sub-component: CountBodyText

> Implements: `body.count`

Counts occurrences of a specified substring in a method body.

#### Requirement: CountBodyOccurrences

The system SHALL the block shall return the number of occurrences of the specified substring in the method body.

#### Scenario: BodyHasOccurrences
- **GIVEN** The method body contains the specified substring multiple times.
- **WHEN** The occurrences are counted.
- **THEN** The result equals the number of occurrences.

#### Scenario: BodyHasNoOccurrences
- **GIVEN** The method body does not contain the specified substring.
- **WHEN** The occurrences are counted.
- **THEN** The result is zero.

- **WHEN** Its text is read.
- **THEN** The file contents are returned.

- **GIVEN** A line ends with whitespace characters.
- **WHEN** Trailing whitespace is removed.
- **THEN** The returned line has no trailing whitespace.

- **WHEN** The line is appended.

- **WHEN** The pattern is compiled.

<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->

- **WHEN** audit is called with its path
- **THEN** Detected problems are reported


## Sub-components

> `SourceBundleAudit` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: PathHandling

> Implements: `Path`

The block establishes a filesystem path for the source bundle being audited.

#### Requirement: CreateBundlePath

The system SHALL the block must represent the supplied source bundle location as a path usable by the audit.

#### Scenario: ValidBundleLocation
- **GIVEN** A source bundle location is supplied
- **WHEN** The path is created
- **THEN** A path representing that location is available to the audit

### Sub-component: OpenZipArchive

> Implements: `zipfile.ZipFile`

The block opens the source bundle as a ZIP archive for inspection.

#### Requirement: OpenBundleArchive

The system SHALL the block must make the source bundle available as a zip archive for subsequent audit operations.

#### Scenario: ValidZipBundle
- **GIVEN** The bundle path refers to a valid ZIP archive
- **WHEN** The archive is opened
- **THEN** The archive is available for inspection

### Sub-component: StripNamePrefix

> Implements: `n.removeprefix`

The block removes a designated prefix from an archive entry name.

#### Requirement: NormalizeEntryPrefix

The system SHALL the block must remove the designated prefix from the entry name when it is present.

#### Scenario: EntryHasPrefix
- **GIVEN** An entry name begins with the designated prefix
- **WHEN** The prefix is removed
- **THEN** The resulting name omits that prefix

### Sub-component: ListArchiveNames

> Implements: `z.namelist`

The block obtains the names of entries contained in the ZIP archive.

#### Requirement: EnumerateArchiveEntries

The system SHALL the block must provide the names of the archive entries for audit filtering.

#### Scenario: ArchiveHasEntries
- **GIVEN** The ZIP archive contains named entries
- **WHEN** Its names are requested
- **THEN** The entry names are available to the audit

### Sub-component: IdentifyDirectoryEntry

> Implements: `n.endswith`

The block identifies archive names that end with the directory separator.

#### Requirement: CheckDirectorySuffix

The system SHALL the block must identify whether an archive entry name ends with the specified suffix.

#### Scenario: DirectoryNameHasSuffix
- **GIVEN** An archive entry name ends with the directory suffix
- **WHEN** The suffix is checked
- **THEN** The name is identified as ending with that suffix

### Sub-component: IdentifyIgnoredEntry

> Implements: `n.startswith`

The block identifies archive names that begin with a designated prefix.

#### Requirement: CheckIgnoredPrefix

The system SHALL the block must identify whether an archive entry name begins with the designated prefix.

#### Scenario: EntryHasIgnoredPrefix
- **GIVEN** An archive entry name begins with the designated prefix
- **WHEN** The prefix is checked
- **THEN** The name is identified as beginning with that prefix

### Sub-component: IdentifyNamedPrefix

> Implements: `name.startswith`

The block checks whether a name begins with a designated prefix.

#### Requirement: CheckNamePrefix

The system SHALL the block must determine whether the supplied name begins with the designated prefix.

#### Scenario: NameBeginsWithPrefix
- **GIVEN** A name begins with the designated prefix
- **WHEN** The prefix is checked
- **THEN** The name is identified as beginning with that prefix

### Sub-component: IdentifyNamedSuffix

> Implements: `name.endswith`

The block checks whether a name ends with a designated suffix.

#### Requirement: CheckNameSuffix

The system SHALL the block must determine whether the supplied name ends with the designated suffix.

#### Scenario: NameEndsWithSuffix
- **GIVEN** A name ends with the designated suffix
- **WHEN** The suffix is checked
- **THEN** The name is identified as ending with that suffix

- **WHEN** The entry is read
- **THEN** Its contents are available to the audit

- **WHEN** The digest is computed

#### Scenario: ValidSource

- **WHEN** The source is parsed
- **THEN** A syntax tree is produced

### Sub-component: ReadSourceEntry

> Implements: `read`

The block reads a named source entry while rejecting names that end with the prohibited suffix.

- **WHEN** The source entry is read
- **THEN** A ValueError is raised

#### Scenario: NameHasAllowedSuffix
- **GIVEN** The requested name does not end with the prohibited suffix
- **WHEN** The source entry is read
- **THEN** The entry contents are returned

- **THEN** The banner contains normalized pool entries with rates

### Requirement: BuildXivOffer

The system SHALL the service shall construct an ffxiv offer pool from banner and master data.

- **Implementation**: `xiv_offer::source/exvius/build_catalogue_banners.py`

#### Scenario: FFXIVBannerHasRecognizedContents
- **GIVEN** The banner and master data identify featured heroes and materials
- **WHEN** xiv_offer is called
- **THEN** The banner contains the corresponding offer pool

### Requirement: BuildTypeOffer

The system SHALL the service shall construct a type-based offer from a banner, source filename, and unit roles.

- **Implementation**: `type_offer::source/exvius/build_catalogue_banners.py`

#### Scenario: TypeOfferSourceIsAvailable
<!-- openlore-test: priority=high (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A banner, source filename, and role mappings are provided
- **WHEN** type_offer is called
- **THEN** It returns offer entries associated with the requested roles


## Sub-components

> `BannerCatalogueBuilder` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: Day

> Implements: `day`

Parses a value into a date used by the catalogue.

#### Requirement: ParseDay

The system SHALL the block must convert a supported date value into a date result.

#### Scenario: ValidDateValue
- **GIVEN** A supported date value is provided
- **WHEN** day parses the value
- **THEN** A date result is returned

### Sub-component: CampaignImage

> Implements: `campaign_image`

Determines campaign image information from a filename, release, and unit data.

#### Requirement: ResolveCampaignImage

The system SHALL the block must derive campaign image information from the supplied inputs.

#### Scenario: CampaignImageInputsProvided
- **GIVEN** A filename, release, and unit collection are provided
- **WHEN** campaign_image processes them
- **THEN** Campaign image information is returned

### Sub-component: Next

> Implements: `next`

Retrieves the next value from an iterator during catalogue processing.

#### Requirement: RetrieveNextValue

The system SHALL the block must return the next available value from the supplied iterator.

#### Scenario: IteratorHasValue
- **GIVEN** An iterator has another value
- **WHEN** next is called
- **THEN** The next value is returned

### Sub-component: Iter

> Implements: `iter`

Obtains an iterator for a collection used during catalogue processing.

#### Requirement: ObtainIterator

The system SHALL the block must provide an iterator over the supplied iterable.

#### Scenario: IterableProvided
- **GIVEN** An iterable collection is provided
- **WHEN** iter is called
- **THEN** An iterator over the collection is returned

### Sub-component: Normal

> Implements: `normal`

Normalizes text for consistent catalogue matching.

#### Requirement: NormalizeText

The system SHALL the block must normalize text and account for character categories when producing a comparable value.

#### Scenario: TextNeedsNormalization
- **GIVEN** A text value contains characters requiring normalization
- **WHEN** normal processes the value
- **THEN** Normalized text is returned

### Sub-component: PageGet

> Implements: `page.get`

Retrieves a value associated with a key from a page record.

#### Requirement: GetPageValue

The system SHALL the block must retrieve the requested value from a page record.

#### Scenario: PageContainsKey
- **GIVEN** A page record contains the requested key
- **WHEN** page.get retrieves it
- **THEN** The associated value is returned

### Sub-component: RegexFinditer

> Implements: `re.finditer`

Finds all non-overlapping regular expression matches in text.

#### Requirement: FindMatches

The system SHALL the block must expose each match of the supplied pattern in the input text.

#### Scenario: TextContainsMatches
- **GIVEN** The text contains two matches for the pattern
- **WHEN** re.finditer processes the text
- **THEN** Both matches are available

### Sub-component: TextFind

> Implements: `text.find`

Locates a substring within text.

#### Requirement: LocateSubstring

The system SHALL the block must return the position of the requested substring when present.

#### Scenario: SubstringPresent
- **GIVEN** The text contains the requested substring
- **WHEN** text.find searches the text
- **THEN** The substring position is returned

### Sub-component: MatchEnd

> Implements: `match.end`

Retrieves the ending position of a regular expression match.

#### Requirement: GetMatchEnd

The system SHALL the block must return the end position of the match.

#### Scenario: MatchAvailable
- **GIVEN** A regular expression match is available
- **WHEN** match.end is called
- **THEN** The match end position is returned

### Sub-component: RegexSearch

> Implements: `re.search`

Finds the first regular expression match in text.

#### Requirement: SearchPattern

The system SHALL the block must return the first match of the supplied pattern, if present.

#### Scenario: PatternOccurs
- **GIVEN** The text contains the pattern
- **WHEN** re.search examines it
- **THEN** The first match is returned

### Sub-component: RegexFindall

> Implements: `re.findall`

Collects all matching text fragments from a string.

#### Requirement: CollectMatches

The system SHALL the block must return all matches of the supplied pattern in the text.

#### Scenario: MultipleOccurrences
- **GIVEN** The text contains multiple occurrences of the pattern
- **WHEN** re.findall processes it
- **THEN** All matching fragments are returned

### Sub-component: ResultAppend

> Implements: `result.append`

Adds a processed value to a result collection.

#### Requirement: AppendResult

The system SHALL the block must add the supplied value to the result collection.

#### Scenario: ResultCollectionExists
- **GIVEN** A result collection and value are available
- **WHEN** result.append is called
- **THEN** The value is present in the collection

### Sub-component: SectionSplit

> Implements: `section.split`

Splits a section of text into parts using a delimiter.

#### Requirement: SplitSection

The system SHALL the block must divide the section at the supplied delimiter.

#### Scenario: DelimiterOccurs
- **GIVEN** A section contains the delimiter
- **WHEN** section.split processes it
- **THEN** The section is returned as separate parts

### Sub-component: UniqueSetdefault

> Implements: `unique.setdefault`

Adds a default entry for a key when the unique-value mapping lacks it.

#### Requirement: EnsureUniqueEntry

The system SHALL the block must preserve an existing value or establish the supplied default for a missing key.

#### Scenario: KeyMissing
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The key is absent from the mapping
- **WHEN** unique.setdefault is called
- **THEN** The default value is stored for the key

### Sub-component: UniqueValues

> Implements: `unique.values`

Provides the values held in the unique-value mapping.

#### Requirement: GetUniqueValues

The system SHALL the block must expose the mapping's stored values.

#### Scenario: MappingHasValues
- **GIVEN** The mapping contains stored values
- **WHEN** unique.values is called
- **THEN** Those values are available

### Sub-component: TextOf

> Implements: `text_of`

Extracts text content from a document by traversing its elements.

#### Requirement: ExtractDocumentText

The system SHALL the block must produce text content from the supplied document.

#### Scenario: DocumentHasText
- **GIVEN** A document contains text-bearing elements
- **WHEN** text_of processes the document
- **THEN** The document text is returned

### Sub-component: ReadTable

> Implements: `read_table`

Reads a named preserved table from a history section.

#### Requirement: ReadPreservedTable

The system SHALL the block must locate and read the named preserved table, and must report an error when it cannot be read.

#### Scenario: NamedTableExists
- **GIVEN** The history section contains a table with the requested name
- **WHEN** read_table processes it
- **THEN** The table data is returned

### Sub-component: ValueError

> Implements: `ValueError`

Signals that a requested value or data structure is invalid or unavailable.

#### Requirement: ReportInvalidValue

The system SHALL the block must communicate an invalid-value condition to its caller.

#### Scenario: ValueIsInvalid
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** An input cannot be accepted as a valid value
- **WHEN** ValueError is raised
- **THEN** The caller receives an invalid-value error

### Sub-component: TextSplit

> Implements: `text.split`

Splits text into parts using a delimiter.

#### Requirement: SplitText

The system SHALL the block must divide text at the supplied delimiter.

#### Scenario: DelimiterOccurs
- **GIVEN** Text contains the delimiter
- **WHEN** text.split processes it
- **THEN** Separate text parts are returned

### Sub-component: TablesAppend

> Implements: `tables.append`

Adds a parsed table to the collection of tables.

#### Requirement: AppendTable

The system SHALL the block must add the supplied table to the tables collection.

#### Scenario: ParsedTableAvailable
- **GIVEN** A parsed table and tables collection are available
- **WHEN** tables.append is called
- **THEN** The collection contains the table

### Sub-component: Array

> Implements: `array`

Extracts a named data array from saved source text.

#### Requirement: ExtractArray

The system SHALL the block must locate the named array in the saved source data and return its value.

#### Scenario: NamedArrayExists
- **GIVEN** Saved source data contains the named array
- **WHEN** array processes the name
- **THEN** The array value is returned

### Sub-component: Defaultdict

> Implements: `defaultdict`

Creates a mapping that supplies default values for missing keys.

#### Requirement: CreateDefaultMapping

The system SHALL the block must provide a mapping that can initialize missing entries using the configured default behavior.

#### Scenario: MissingKeyAccessed
<!-- openlore-test: priority=high tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A mapping is configured with a default factory
- **WHEN** A missing key is requested
- **THEN** A default value is established

### Sub-component: LiteralEval

> Implements: `ast.literal_eval`

Converts a saved literal expression into its corresponding data value.

#### Requirement: ParseLiteralData

The system SHALL the block must convert a valid literal expression into data without evaluating arbitrary code.

#### Scenario: ValidLiteral
- **GIVEN** The input is a valid supported literal expression
- **WHEN** ast.literal_eval parses it
- **THEN** The corresponding data value is returned

### Sub-component: UnitsAppend

> Implements: `units.append`

Adds a unit record to the unit collection.

#### Requirement: AppendUnit

The system SHALL the block must add the supplied unit record to the collection.

#### Scenario: UnitRecordAvailable
- **GIVEN** A unit record and collection are available
- **WHEN** units.append is called
- **THEN** The collection contains the unit record

### Sub-component: CodeSplit

> Implements: `code.split`

Splits a code string into parts using a delimiter.

#### Requirement: SplitCode

The system SHALL the block must divide the code string at the supplied delimiter.

#### Scenario: DelimiterOccurs
- **GIVEN** The code contains the delimiter
- **WHEN** code.split processes it
- **THEN** Separate code parts are returned

### Sub-component: UnitGet

> Implements: `u.get`

Retrieves a field from a unit record.

#### Requirement: GetUnitField

The system SHALL the block must retrieve the requested field from the unit record.

#### Scenario: FieldExists
- **GIVEN** A unit record contains the requested field
- **WHEN** u.get retrieves it
- **THEN** The field value is returned

### Sub-component: NamesGet

> Implements: `names.get`

Looks up a name in the name mapping.

#### Requirement: LookupName

The system SHALL the block must return the mapped name when the requested key exists.

#### Scenario: NameExists

- **WHEN** names.get is called
- **THEN** The mapped name is returned

Looks up release information in the release mapping.

- **WHEN** releases.get is called
- **THEN** The associated release information is returned

### Sub-component: FormsGet

Looks up form information in the form mapping.

- **WHEN** forms.get is called
- **THEN** The associated form information is returned

### Sub-component: TotalsValues

> Implements: `totals.values`

Provides the accumulated total values.

#### Requirement: GetTotalValues

- **GIVEN** The totals mapping contains values
- **WHEN** totals.values is called

### Sub-component: TotalsItems

#### Scenario: TotalsExist
- **GIVEN** The totals mapping contains entries
- **WHEN** totals.items is called
- **THEN** The entries are available

### Sub-component: Item

> Implements: `item`

Creates and appends a counted item entry to a pool.

#### Requirement: AppendPoolItem

The system SHALL the block must add an item entry of the supplied kind, identifier, and count to the pool.

#### Scenario: ItemDetailsProvided
- **GIVEN** An item kind, identifier, and count are supplied
- **WHEN** item processes them
- **THEN** A corresponding entry is added to the pool

### Sub-component: Unit

> Implements: `unit`

Creates and appends a unit entry with a rate and optional attributes to a pool.

#### Requirement: AppendPoolUnit

The system SHALL the block must add a unit entry with the supplied identifier, rate, and optional attributes to the pool.

#### Scenario: UnitDetailsProvided
- **GIVEN** A unit identifier and rate are supplied
- **WHEN** unit processes them
- **THEN** A corresponding unit entry is added to the pool

### Sub-component: NameEndswith

> Implements: `name.endswith`

Checks whether a name ends with a requested suffix.

#### Requirement: CheckNameSuffix

The system SHALL the block must report whether the name ends with the supplied suffix.

#### Scenario: SuffixMatches
- **GIVEN** The name ends with the supplied suffix
- **WHEN** name.endswith is evaluated
- **THEN** The result is true

### Sub-component: NameStartswith

> Implements: `name.startswith`

Checks whether a name starts with a requested prefix.

#### Requirement: CheckNamePrefix

The system SHALL the block must report whether the name starts with the supplied prefix.

#### Scenario: PrefixMatches
- **GIVEN** The name starts with the supplied prefix
- **WHEN** name.startswith is evaluated
- **THEN** The result is true

Adds or replaces fields in a banner record.

- **GIVEN** A banner record and update fields are available
- **WHEN** banner.update is called
- **THEN** The banner contains the updated fields

### Sub-component: BannerPop

> Implements: `banner.pop`

Removes a field from a banner record and returns its value when present.

The system SHALL the block must remove the requested field and return its value or the supplied default.

- **GIVEN** The banner contains the requested field
- **WHEN** banner.pop removes it
- **THEN** Its value is returned and the field is absent

Loads and parses a saved JSON table.

#### Scenario: SavedTableIsValid
- **GIVEN** The requested table exists and contains valid JSON
- **WHEN** load processes it
- **THEN** The parsed table is returned

> Implements: `hero`

Normalizes a hero name for consistent matching.

#### Scenario: HeroNameProvided
- **GIVEN** A hero name is supplied
- **WHEN** hero processes the name
- **THEN** A normalized hero name is returned

Adds a material row to the material-row collection.

- **GIVEN** A material row and collection are available
- **WHEN** material_rows.append is called
- **THEN** The collection contains the row

Adds an entry to a simulator pool.

### Sub-component: CalendarRows

> Implements: `calendar_rows`

Builds calendar rows from saved pages, including dates, campaign images, and unique campaign information.

The system SHALL the block must extract and return calendar rows from the supplied pages.

- **GIVEN** Saved pages contain campaign date and content information
- **WHEN** calendar_rows processes the pages
- **THEN** Calendar rows are returned with extracted campaign information

### Sub-component: RateTables

> Implements: `rate_tables`

Reads preserved historical rate tables and associates them with their dates.

- **GIVEN** History contains preserved rate tables for multiple dates
- **WHEN** rate_tables processes it
- **THEN** Each table is returned with its associated date

### Sub-component: Date

> Implements: `date`

Provides the current date using the system clock.

#### Scenario: SystemTimeAvailable
- **GIVEN** The system time source is available
- **WHEN** date is called
- **THEN** The current date is returned

### Sub-component: SimulatorPool

> Implements: `simulator_pool`

Interprets saved simulator data arrays to construct a pool without executing wiki JavaScript.

The system SHALL the block must interpret saved data arrays and return the corresponding simulator pool.

#### Scenario: SavedArraysContainPoolData
- **GIVEN** Saved data arrays and lookup mappings describe a simulator pool
- **WHEN** simulator_pool processes them
- **THEN** A pool reflecting the saved data is returned

- **THEN** Offer data for the banner is returned

### Requirement: BuildClashRewards

The system SHALL the service shall load and interpret source data, derive clash reward entries, and write the resulting data.

#### Scenario: BuildRewardsFromValidSourceData
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The source assets contain readable reward, item, unit, and season data
- **WHEN** the reward build operation runs

#### Scenario: RejectInvalidSourceData
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->

- **WHEN** the reward build operation runs
- **THEN** it reports a validation error instead of producing invalid reward output


## Sub-components

> `ClashRewardsBuilder` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: ItemKey

> Implements: `item_key`

Derives a normalized key from a value for matching item records.

#### Requirement: DeriveItemKey

The system SHALL the function shall derive and return an item key from the supplied value.

#### Scenario: ValueWithRepeatedDelimiter
- **GIVEN** A value containing a repeated delimiter is supplied.
- **WHEN** The item key is derived.
- **THEN** The returned key reflects the value's delimiter structure.

### Sub-component: NameTables

> Implements: `name_tables`

Loads name tables from the configured source archive for downstream reward processing.

#### Requirement: LoadNameTables

The system SHALL the function shall read eligible name table entries from the archive and return them in a lookup structure.

#### Scenario: ArchiveContainsNameTables
- **GIVEN** The archive contains eligible name table files with valid JSON.
- **WHEN** Name tables are loaded.
- **THEN** The returned lookup contains the parsed table entries.

#### Scenario: ArchiveContainsNoNameTables
- **GIVEN** The archive contains no eligible name table files.
- **WHEN** Name tables are loaded.
- **THEN** An empty lookup is returned.

### Sub-component: ValueError

> Implements: `ValueError`

Represents a value-related failure encountered while processing reward data.

#### Requirement: SignalInvalidValue

The system SHALL the processing flow shall signal a value error when an input cannot be accepted as a valid value.

#### Scenario: InvalidValueEncountered
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A value fails a required processing constraint.
- **WHEN** The failure is raised.
- **THEN** A ValueError is signaled.

### Sub-component: Plain

> Implements: `plain`

Converts formatted input into plain text for reward data processing.

#### Requirement: NormalizePlainText

The system SHALL the function shall remove markup and decode escaped text while preserving readable content.

#### Scenario: FormattedTextInput
- **GIVEN** The input contains markup and escaped entities.
- **WHEN** It is converted to plain text.
- **THEN** The result contains readable text without markup.

### Sub-component: MaxLevel

> Implements: `max_level`

Determines the maximum level for a unit using unit master data.

#### Requirement: DetermineMaximumLevel

The system SHALL the function shall return the maximum level supported by the supplied unit master data.

#### Scenario: UnitHasLevelRecords
- **GIVEN** Unit master data contains level records.
- **WHEN** The maximum level is determined.
- **THEN** The highest supported level is returned.

### Sub-component: JsonLoads

> Implements: `json.loads`

Parses serialized JSON data into structured data for reward processing.

#### Requirement: ParseJsonData

The system SHALL the operation shall parse valid json text into its corresponding structured value.

#### Scenario: ValidJsonInput
- **GIVEN** A syntactically valid JSON string is supplied.
- **WHEN** The JSON is parsed.
- **THEN** Its corresponding structured value is produced.

### Sub-component: ReadSourceText

> Implements: `source.read_text`

Reads source content as text for processing.

#### Requirement: ReadSourceContent

The system SHALL the operation shall return the source content as text.

#### Scenario: SourceIsReadable
- **GIVEN** The source is available for reading.
- **WHEN** Its text is read.
- **THEN** The source content is returned as text.

### Sub-component: UpdateEmblems

> Implements: `emblems.update`

Adds or replaces emblem entries in the emblem collection.

#### Requirement: MergeEmblemEntries

The system SHALL the operation shall update the emblem collection with the supplied entries.

#### Scenario: NewEmblemEntries
- **GIVEN** A collection of emblem entries is supplied.
- **WHEN** The collection is updated.
- **THEN** The supplied entries are present in the emblem collection.

### Sub-component: TitleItems

> Implements: `titles.items`

Provides title entries for iteration during reward processing.

#### Requirement: IterateTitleEntries

The system SHALL the operation shall provide the title collection's key-value entries.

#### Scenario: TitlesAreAvailable
- **GIVEN** The title collection contains entries.
- **WHEN** Its entries are requested.
- **THEN** The key-value title entries are available for iteration.

### Sub-component: SearchText

> Implements: `re.search`

Searches text for a pattern used to identify reward data.

#### Requirement: FindPattern

The system SHALL the operation shall report the first match of the supplied pattern in the text, or no match when absent.

#### Scenario: PatternPresent
- **GIVEN** The text contains the supplied pattern.
- **WHEN** The text is searched.
- **THEN** The first matching occurrence is returned.

#### Scenario: PatternAbsent
- **GIVEN** The text does not contain the supplied pattern.
- **WHEN** The text is searched.
- **THEN** No match is returned.

### Sub-component: FindAllMatches

> Implements: `re.findall`

Collects all occurrences of a pattern from text.

#### Requirement: CollectPatternMatches

The system SHALL the operation shall return all non-overlapping matches of the supplied pattern in the text.

#### Scenario: MultipleMatchesPresent
- **GIVEN** The text contains multiple occurrences of the pattern.
- **WHEN** Matches are collected.
- **THEN** All matching occurrences are returned.

### Sub-component: SplitByPattern

> Implements: `re.split`

Splits text into parts using a pattern as the separator.

#### Requirement: SplitTextByPattern

The system SHALL the operation shall divide text at occurrences of the supplied pattern.

#### Scenario: SeparatorOccurs
- **GIVEN** The text contains the separator pattern.
- **WHEN** The text is split.
- **THEN** Parts on either side of each separator are returned.

### Sub-component: SplitText

> Implements: `text.split`

Divides text into segments for downstream parsing.

#### Requirement: DivideText

The system SHALL the operation shall return text segments according to the requested separator.

#### Scenario: TextContainsSeparator
- **GIVEN** The text contains the requested separator.
- **WHEN** The text is split.
- **THEN** The resulting segments are returned in order.

### Sub-component: StripPart

> Implements: `part.strip`

Removes surrounding whitespace from a parsed part.

#### Requirement: TrimPart

The system SHALL the operation shall return the part with surrounding whitespace removed.

#### Scenario: PartHasOuterWhitespace
- **GIVEN** A part has leading or trailing whitespace.
- **WHEN** The part is stripped.
- **THEN** The returned part has no surrounding whitespace.

### Sub-component: AppendRanking

> Implements: `ranking.append`

Adds a parsed ranking entry to the ranking collection.

#### Requirement: AddRankingEntry

The system SHALL the operation shall add the supplied ranking entry to the ranking collection.

#### Scenario: RankingEntryParsed
- **GIVEN** A ranking entry has been parsed.
- **WHEN** The entry is appended.
- **THEN** The entry is present at the end of the ranking collection.

### Sub-component: StripRank

> Implements: `rank.strip`

Removes surrounding whitespace from a rank value.

#### Requirement: TrimRank

The system SHALL the operation shall return the rank value with surrounding whitespace removed.

#### Scenario: RankHasOuterWhitespace
- **GIVEN** A rank value has leading or trailing whitespace.
- **WHEN** The rank is stripped.
- **THEN** The returned rank has no surrounding whitespace.

### Sub-component: ReplaceTitleText

> Implements: `title.replace`

Replaces matching text within a title value.

#### Requirement: ReplaceTitleSubstring

The system SHALL the operation shall return the title with occurrences of the specified text replaced.

#### Scenario: TitleContainsTargetText
- **GIVEN** A title contains the target text.
- **WHEN** The replacement is applied.
- **THEN** The target text is replaced in the returned title.

### Sub-component: WriteTargetText

> Implements: `target.write_text`

Writes processed reward output as text to the target.

#### Requirement: WriteProcessedOutput

The system SHALL the operation shall write the supplied text content to the target.

#### Scenario: TargetAcceptsOutput
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The target is writable and processed text is available.
- **WHEN** The text is written.
- **THEN** The target contains the supplied processed text.

### Sub-component: JsonDumps

> Implements: `json.dumps`

Serializes processed reward data into JSON text.

#### Requirement: SerializeRewardData

The system SHALL the operation shall serialize the supplied data as json text.

#### Scenario: SerializableData
- **GIVEN** The supplied reward data can be represented as JSON.
- **WHEN** The data is serialized.
- **THEN** JSON text representing the data is returned.

### Sub-component: SeasonValues

> Implements: `seasons.values`

Provides season records for iteration during reward processing.

#### Requirement: IterateSeasonValues

The system SHALL the operation shall provide the season collection's values.

#### Scenario: SeasonsAvailable
- **GIVEN** The season collection contains records.
- **WHEN** Its values are requested.
- **THEN** The season records are available for iteration.

### Sub-component: IndexSource

- **WHEN** The key is accessed
- **THEN** A default value is supplied for that key.

- **WHEN** The value is processed
- **THEN** A value error is raised.

- **WHEN** The next item is requested
- **THEN** The first available item is returned.

- **WHEN** Mission derivation runs

### Requirement: NormalizeText

The system SHALL the service shall normalize text values for consistent comparison and output.

#### Scenario: NormalizeTextWithInconsistentFormatting
- **GIVEN** A text value contains Unicode variants or formatting noise
- **WHEN** normal is called
- **THEN** A normalized text value is returned

### Requirement: ParseTableRows

The system SHALL the service shall parse table data into rows, optionally preserving text values.

#### Scenario: ParseAPopulatedTable
- **GIVEN** A table contains serialized row data
- **WHEN** rows is called
- **THEN** The table is returned as parsed rows

### Requirement: ExtractFirstDate

The system SHALL the service shall identify the first supported date in a value and return it in a consistent date representation.

- **Implementation**: `first_date::source/exvius/build_event_archive.py`

#### Scenario: FindADateInText
- **GIVEN** The value contains a date in a supported format
- **WHEN** first_date is called
- **THEN** The first date is returned in a consistent representation

#### Scenario: ValueHasNoDate
- **GIVEN** The value contains no supported date
- **WHEN** first_date is called
- **THEN** An empty result is returned

### Requirement: BuildArchiveRecords

The system SHALL the service shall extract and group event archive entries from the source document into structured records.

- **Implementation**: `archive_records::source/exvius/build_event_archive.py`

#### Scenario: BuildRecordsFromArchivedEventData
- **GIVEN** A document contains event archive rows
- **WHEN** archive_records is called
- **THEN** Structured archive records are grouped and returned

### Requirement: BuildEventArchive

The system SHALL the service shall load source event data, assemble and sort event records, and write the resulting archive output.

#### Scenario: GenerateEventArchive
- **GIVEN** The source event data is available
- **WHEN** main is called
- **THEN** Event records are assembled, sorted, and written to the output file

#### Scenario: NoDateFound
