- **GIVEN** The source path is contained by the configured root
- **WHEN** the builder derives its relative path
- **THEN** the path beneath the root is returned

### Sub-component: Sha256Hasher

> Implements: `hashlib.sha256`

Creates a SHA-256 digest calculator for asset content.

#### Requirement: CalculateAssetDigest

The system SHALL the builder shall calculate a sha-256 digest for the supplied asset content.

#### Scenario: DigestIsProduced
- **GIVEN** Asset content is available
- **WHEN** the builder calculates its SHA-256 digest
- **THEN** a digest corresponding to that content is produced

### Sub-component: SourceBytesReading

> Implements: `source.read_bytes`

Reads binary content from the source asset path.

#### Requirement: ReadSourceAssetBytes

The system SHALL the builder shall read the bytes stored at the source asset path for asset processing.

#### Scenario: SourceBytesAreAvailable
- **GIVEN** The source path refers to a readable asset
- **WHEN** the builder reads its bytes
- **THEN** the asset bytes are returned

### Sub-component: TargetRelativePath

> Implements: `target.relative_to`

Computes a target asset path relative to its configured root.

#### Requirement: DeriveRelativeTargetPath

The system SHALL the builder shall derive the target path relative to the specified root when the target is within that root.

#### Scenario: TargetIsUnderRoot
- **GIVEN** The target path is contained by the configured root
- **WHEN** the builder derives its relative path
- **THEN** the path beneath the root is returned

### Sub-component: TargetBytesReading

> Implements: `target.read_bytes`

Reads binary content from the generated target asset path.

#### Requirement: ReadTargetAssetBytes

The system SHALL the builder shall read the bytes stored at the target path for generated asset processing.

#### Scenario: TargetBytesAreAvailable
- **GIVEN** The target path refers to a readable generated asset
- **WHEN** the builder reads its bytes
- **THEN** the generated asset bytes are returned

### Sub-component: ReportWriting

> Implements: `report.write_text`

Writes the asset recovery report to its designated path.

#### Requirement: WriteRecoveryReport

The system SHALL the builder shall write the recovery report text to the configured report path.

#### Scenario: ReportIsWritten
- **GIVEN** Report text and a writable report path are available
- **WHEN** the builder writes the report
- **THEN** the report path contains the supplied text

> `source/exvius/build_event_battles.py`

### Requirement: BuildEventBattles

The system SHALL the service shall assemble event battle records from available mission, resource, datamine, memorial, and language data, and produce a serialized output with a content fingerprint.

#### Scenario: BuildEventBattleOutput
- **GIVEN** The configured project data sources are available
- **WHEN** main is run
- **THEN** Event battle records are assembled and serialized with a content fingerprint

### Requirement: DeriveMissionRoster

The system SHALL the service shall derive a mission battle roster, including its phases, monsters, drops, break data, and required skills, from the resolved mission sources.

#### Scenario: DeriveAMissionRoster
- **GIVEN** A mission and its related source rows can be resolved
- **WHEN** the mission roster is derived
- **THEN** The resulting roster includes the applicable phases, monsters, drops, break data, and skills

### Requirement: ResolveEncounterParts

The system SHALL the service shall resolve the selected encounter pool into the parts used by its phases without reusing parts already assigned to another phase.

#### Scenario: ResolveSelectedEncounterParts
- **GIVEN** Encounter waves reference pools with candidate parts
- **WHEN** Encounter parts are resolved
- **THEN** The selected pool models and parts are assigned to phases without reusing parts

> `source/exvius/build_named_banners.py`

### Requirement: NormalizeBannerNames

The system SHALL the service shall normalize banner names so visually equivalent names can be matched despite invisible direction marks or unicode variations.

- **Implementation**: `normalized_name::source/exvius/build_named_banners.py`

#### Scenario: EquivalentUnicodeNames
- **GIVEN** Two names differ only by Unicode normalization or invisible direction marks
- **WHEN** The names are normalized
- **THEN** The results match

### Requirement: ReconcileCommunityBannerNames

The system SHALL the service shall restore featured unit names in community banner data by matching normalized names against shipped unit records.

- **Implementation**: `reconcile_community_names::source/exvius/build_named_banners.py`

#### Scenario: RecoverMissingFeaturedNames
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Community banner records contain featured names that cannot be matched due to invisible direction
marks
- **WHEN** Community names are reconciled
- **THEN** Matching shipped unit identities are restored in the banner data

### Requirement: ReconcileCuratedBannerArtwork

The system SHALL the service shall replace mismatched banner artwork with verified artwork for the corresponding original campaign.

- **Implementation**: `reconcile_curated_art::source/exvius/build_named_banners.py`

#### Scenario: ReplaceUnrelatedArtwork
- **GIVEN** A curated banner references artwork that does not match its Global campaign
- **WHEN** Curated artwork is reconciled
- **THEN** The banner references the verified campaign artwork and its matching asset is copied to the runtime
artwork location

### Requirement: BuildWikiCampaignBanners

The system SHALL the service shall create banner records from verified wiki campaign definitions and featured units while retaining original images.

- **Implementation**: `wiki_banners::source/exvius/build_named_banners.py`

#### Scenario: BuildCampaignBannerFromVerifiedData
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A wiki campaign has featured units, offer details, and a verified original image
- **WHEN** Wiki banners are built
- **THEN** A banner record is created with the campaign details and original artwork copied to the runtime
artwork location

### Requirement: FindFeaturedNVUnits

The system SHALL the service shall identify nv units featured on global banners for use in the relevant special pools.

- **Implementation**: `featured_nv_units::source/exvius/build_named_banners.py`

#### Scenario: CollectBannerFeaturedNVUnits
- **GIVEN** Banner records and shipped unit data include featured NV units
- **WHEN** Featured NV units are collected
- **THEN** The result contains the NV units featured by Global banners

### Requirement: CalculateArtworkDigest

The system SHALL the service shall calculate a sha-256 digest for an artwork file so banner assets can be identified consistently.

#### Scenario: DigestAnArtworkFile
- **GIVEN** An artwork file is available
- **WHEN** Its digest is calculated
- **THEN** The file's SHA-256 digest is returned

### Requirement: ExtractPrintedBannerBonuses

The system SHALL the service shall derive banner step bonuses from the original step artwork using shipped item and unit identifiers.

- **Implementation**: `printed_bonuses::source/exvius/build_named_banners.py`

#### Scenario: ReadPrintedStepBonuses
- **GIVEN** A banner has original step artwork with identifiable printed bonuses
- **WHEN** Printed bonuses are extracted
- **THEN** The resulting bonus data uses the corresponding shipped item and unit identifiers

### Requirement: BuildNamedBannerData

The system SHALL the service shall assemble named banner definitions, apply reconciliations and derived banner data, and write the resulting json output.

#### Scenario: GenerateNamedBannerOutput
- **GIVEN** Curated, translated, community, artwork, and shipped unit inputs are available
- **WHEN** The banner build runs
- **THEN** Reconciled named banner records are written to the configured output file


## Sub-components

> `Named Gacha Banner Builder` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: UnitRootsByRarity

> Implements: `unit_roots_by_rarity`

Groups unit root records by rarity.

#### Requirement: GroupRoots

The system SHALL the block shall group each unit root under its rarity and return the grouped records.

#### Scenario: RootsWithMultipleRarities
- **GIVEN** unit root records contain entries with different rarities
- **WHEN** the block groups the records
- **THEN** each entry appears under its corresponding rarity

### Sub-component: NormalizedName

> Implements: `normalized_name`

Normalizes a name for consistent comparison despite Unicode variation.

#### Requirement: NormalizeForComparison

The system SHALL the block shall produce a normalized name suitable for comparing names with invisible or compatibility-related unicode differences.

#### Scenario: DirectionMarkInName
- **GIVEN** a name contains an invisible direction mark
- **WHEN** the block normalizes the name
- **THEN** the result compares equal to the same name without that mark

### Sub-component: UnitMst

> Implements: `unit_mst`

Loads unit master data for resolving unit identifiers and related records.

#### Requirement: LoadUnitMasterData

The system SHALL the block shall provide unit master records for downstream unit and reward resolution.

#### Scenario: MasterDataAvailable
- **GIVEN** the unit master data file contains records
- **WHEN** the block loads unit master data
- **THEN** the records are available to callers

### Sub-component: Next

> Implements: `next`

Selects the next matching element from an iterable.

#### Requirement: SelectNextMatch

The system SHALL the block shall return the next element satisfying the supplied selection criteria.

#### Scenario: MatchingElementExists
- **GIVEN** an iterable contains a matching element
- **WHEN** the block selects the next match
- **THEN** it returns that element

### Sub-component: Any

> Implements: `any`

Determines whether at least one element satisfies a condition.

#### Requirement: CheckAnyMatch

The system SHALL the block shall report whether any element satisfies the supplied condition.

#### Scenario: OneElementMatches
- **GIVEN** at least one element satisfies the condition
- **WHEN** the block checks the iterable
- **THEN** it returns true

### Sub-component: TableReadText

> Implements: `table.read_text`

Reads text content from a table file.

#### Requirement: ReadTableText

The system SHALL the block shall provide the text content of the table file.

#### Scenario: TableExists
- **GIVEN** the table file is readable
- **WHEN** its text is read
- **THEN** the file content is returned

### Sub-component: JsonLoads

> Implements: `json.loads`

Parses JSON text into structured data.

#### Requirement: ParseJson

The system SHALL the block shall parse valid json text into its corresponding data structure.

#### Scenario: ValidJson
- **GIVEN** the input is valid JSON
- **WHEN** the text is parsed
- **THEN** the corresponding structured value is returned

### Sub-component: NamesSetdefault

> Implements: `names.setdefault`

Ensures a name key exists in the names mapping with a default value.

#### Requirement: EnsureNameEntry

The system SHALL the block shall preserve an existing name entry or add the supplied default when the key is absent.

#### Scenario: NameKeyAbsent
- **GIVEN** the name key is absent
- **WHEN** the entry is ensured
- **THEN** the default value is stored for that key

### Sub-component: PathReadText

> Implements: `path.read_text`

Reads text content from a path.

#### Requirement: ReadPathText

The system SHALL the block shall return the text content at the specified path.

#### Scenario: PathIsReadable
- **GIVEN** the path identifies a readable text file
- **WHEN** its text is read
- **THEN** the file content is returned

- **THEN** the supplied default is returned

### Sub-component: IdsUpdate

Adds values from an iterable to an identifier set.

#### Requirement: UpdateIdentifiers

The system SHALL the block shall add all supplied identifiers to the identifier set.

#### Scenario: IdentifiersProvided
- **GIVEN** an iterable of identifiers is supplied
- **WHEN** the set is updated
- **THEN** all supplied identifiers are members of the set

### Sub-component: ReconcileCommunityNames

> Implements: `reconcile_community_names`

Restores wiki featured names lost to invisible direction marks by reconciling community records with unit data.

#### Requirement: RestoreFeaturedNames

The system SHALL the block shall reconcile community featured names against normalized shipped unit names and persist restored identifiers where matches are found.

#### Scenario: InvisibleDirectionMark
- **GIVEN** a wiki featured name differs from a unit name only by an invisible direction mark
- **WHEN** community names are reconciled
- **THEN** the corresponding unit identifier is restored and persisted

### Sub-component: ReconcileCuratedArt

> Implements: `reconcile_curated_art`

Replaces unrelated JP artwork with matching original Global campaign art and updates curated and community data.

#### Requirement: ReplaceMismatchedCampaignArt

The system SHALL the block shall replace eligible mismatched artwork with verified original global campaign artwork and persist the updated records.

#### Scenario: MatchingGlobalCampaignExists
- **GIVEN** a curated banner uses unrelated JP art and a verified matching Global campaign image is available
- **WHEN** art reconciliation runs
- **THEN** the banner references the matching Global image and the updated data is written

### Sub-component: FeaturedNvUnits

> Implements: `featured_nv_units`

Returns the NV units featured on Global banners for use in SP pools.

#### Requirement: ListFeaturedNvUnits

The system SHALL the block shall return the nv units actually featured on global banners.

#### Scenario: FeaturedUnitsAvailable
- **GIVEN** Global banner feature data is available
- **WHEN** featured NV units are requested
- **THEN** only units featured on those banners are returned

### Sub-component: PrintedBonuses

> Implements: `printed_bonuses`

Extracts bonuses shown on original step artwork using shipped item and unit identifiers.

#### Requirement: DerivePrintedBonuses

The system SHALL the block shall return bonus data corresponding to the original step artwork and shipped item or unit identifiers.

#### Scenario: ArtContainsBonuses
- **GIVEN** the original step art has readable bonus information
- **WHEN** printed bonuses are derived
- **THEN** the returned bonuses correspond to the printed information

### Sub-component: RegularOffers

> Implements: `regular_offers`

Applies quantities, caps, and bonuses shown on each Global banner image.

#### Requirement: DeriveRegularOffers

The system SHALL the block shall produce regular offers that match the quantities, caps, and bonuses printed on the global banner image.

#### Scenario: OfferDetailsPrinted
- **GIVEN** a Global banner image specifies offer quantities, caps, and bonuses
- **WHEN** regular offers are derived
- **THEN** the resulting offers contain those specified details

### Sub-component: WikiBanners

> Implements: `wiki_banners`

Keeps original banner images and copies verified complete images to the runtime artwork location.

#### Requirement: LoadWikiBanners

The system SHALL the block shall retain original banner records and make verified complete images available in the runtime artwork location.

#### Scenario: VerifiedWholeImage
- **GIVEN** a banner has a verified complete original image
- **WHEN** wiki banners are prepared
- **THEN** the original is retained and the image is copied to runtime artwork

### Sub-component: KingMog

> Implements: `king_mog`

Builds or updates King Mog event data.

#### Requirement: ProcessKingMogData

The system SHALL the block shall provide the current king mog event data used by the orchestrator.

#### Scenario: EventDataAvailable
- **GIVEN** King Mog source data is available
- **WHEN** the block runs
- **THEN** King Mog event data is returned

### Sub-component: UpdateArena

> Implements: `update_arena`

Updates arena data used by the orchestrator.

#### Requirement: RefreshArenaData

The system SHALL the block shall update and provide the arena data expected by the orchestrator.

#### Scenario: ArenaSourceAvailable
- **GIVEN** arena source data is available
- **WHEN** the arena update runs
- **THEN** updated arena data is provided

### Sub-component: Rebuild

> Implements: `rebuild`

Rebuilds the orchestrator's generated banner data.

#### Requirement: RebuildBannerData

The system SHALL the block shall rebuild the generated banner data from its available curated, community, unit, and artwork sources.

#### Scenario: SourcesAvailable
- **GIVEN** required source data is available
- **WHEN** the rebuild runs
- **THEN** generated banner data is rebuilt and written to its output

### Sub-component: Tier5Update

> Implements: `tier5.update`

Adds or replaces entries in a tier5 mapping.

#### Requirement: UpdateTier5

The system SHALL the block shall add supplied entries to the tier5 mapping and replace existing matching keys.

#### Scenario: EntryProvided
- **GIVEN** entries are supplied
- **WHEN** tier5 is updated
- **THEN** the mapping contains the supplied entries

### Sub-component: FRelativeTo

> Implements: `f.relative_to`

Computes a file path relative to a designated base path.

#### Requirement: MakeFilePathRelative

The system SHALL the block shall return the file path relative to the supplied base path when contained within it.

#### Scenario: FileUnderBase
- **GIVEN** the file path is inside the base path
- **WHEN** a relative path is computed
- **THEN** the relative path is returned

### Sub-component: PrintedGuaranteesGet

> Implements: `PRINTED_GUARANTEES.get`

Retrieves printed guarantee data by key.

#### Requirement: GetPrintedGuarantee

The system SHALL the block shall return the guarantee for a key or the supplied default when absent.

#### Scenario: GuaranteeAbsent
- **GIVEN** the key is absent from printed guarantees
- **WHEN** the guarantee is retrieved
- **THEN** the supplied default is returned

### Sub-component: NvTierUpdate

> Implements: `nv_tier.update`

Adds or replaces entries in the NV tier mapping.

#### Requirement: UpdateNvTier

The system SHALL the block shall add supplied entries to the nv tier mapping and replace matching keys.

#### Scenario: EntriesProvided
- **GIVEN** entries are supplied
- **WHEN** the NV tier is updated
- **THEN** the mapping contains the supplied entries

### Sub-component: RGet

> Implements: `r.get`

Retrieves a value from the mapping named r.

#### Requirement: GetRValue

The system SHALL the block shall return the value for a key or the supplied default when absent.

#### Scenario: KeyAbsent
- **GIVEN** mapping r lacks the requested key
- **WHEN** its value is retrieved
- **THEN** the supplied default is returned

### Sub-component: StemSplit

> Implements: `stem.split`

Splits a stem string using a separator.

#### Requirement: SplitStem

The system SHALL the block shall return the stem components separated by the supplied delimiter.

#### Scenario: DelimiterPresent
- **GIVEN** the stem contains the delimiter
- **WHEN** the stem is split
- **THEN** the resulting components reflect the split

### Sub-component: BannersExtend

> Implements: `banners.extend`

Adds multiple banners to a banner collection.

#### Requirement: ExtendBanners

The system SHALL the block shall add each supplied banner to the end of the collection in order.

#### Scenario: MultipleBannersProvided
- **GIVEN** an iterable contains multiple banners
- **WHEN** the collection is extended
- **THEN** all banners are added in iterable order

### Sub-component: OutWriteText

> Implements: `OUT.write_text`

Writes generated output text to the designated output path.

#### Requirement: WriteGeneratedOutput

The system SHALL the block shall write generated output text to the configured output path.

#### Scenario: OutputPathWritable
- **GIVEN** the configured output path is writable
- **WHEN** generated text is written
- **THEN** the output file contains that text

> `source/exvius/build_nv_vision_cards.py`

### Requirement: ReadDecodedRows

The system SHALL the service shall read and parse rows from a decoded data file.

#### Scenario: ReadADecodedDataFile
- **GIVEN** A file containing JSON data on each line
- **WHEN** rows is called with the file path
- **THEN** The parsed rows are returned

### Requirement: FetchPageData

The system SHALL the service shall retrieve page data for requested titles from the fandom api.

- **Implementation**: `fetch::source/exvius/build_nv_vision_cards.py`

#### Scenario: FetchRequestedPages
- **GIVEN** A collection of page titles
- **WHEN** fetch is called
- **THEN** Page data is retrieved from the Fandom API

### Requirement: ListCategoryPages

The system SHALL the service shall retrieve pages belonging to a specified category.

- **Implementation**: `category_pages::source/exvius/build_nv_vision_cards.py`

#### Scenario: RetrievePagesForACategory
- **GIVEN** A category name
- **WHEN** category_pages is called
- **THEN** Pages belonging to that category are returned

### Requirement: FindGlobalCards

The system SHALL the service shall find global vision card pages and associate matching cards with their unit names, card identifiers, names, pages, and revisions.

- **Implementation**: `global_cards::source/exvius/build_nv_vision_cards.py`

#### Scenario: MatchGlobalVisionCards
- **GIVEN** Card identifiers and available Global Vision Card pages
- **WHEN** global_cards is called
- **THEN** Matching cards are returned with their associated unit and page information

### Requirement: BuildVisionCardDataset

The system SHALL the service shall combine decoded card data, names, and global vision card page information into a vision card json dataset.

#### Scenario: BuildAndWriteTheDataset
- **GIVEN** Decoded game data and card page information are available
- **WHEN** main is called
- **THEN** The combined vision card dataset is written to the configured output file

#### Scenario: HandleCardsWithoutADirectPageMatch
- **GIVEN** A decoded card does not match a page by its primary title
- **WHEN** main resolves card pages
- **THEN** The service checks available aliases and title information before recording unresolved cards


## Sub-components

> `NV Vision Card Builder` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: CategoryPages

> Implements: `category_pages`

Loads the page data for a card category.

#### Requirement: LoadCategoryPages

The system SHALL the category page loader shall return decoded json data for the requested category.

#### Scenario: CategoryPagesLoaded
- **GIVEN** A category with valid page data
- **WHEN** category_pages is called for that category
- **THEN** The decoded page data for the category is returned.

### Sub-component: Fetch

> Implements: `fetch`

Loads card page data for the requested titles.

#### Requirement: LoadTitlePages

The system SHALL the fetch operation shall return decoded json page data for the requested titles.

#### Scenario: RequestedPagesLoaded
- **GIVEN** Titles with valid page data
- **WHEN** fetch is called with those titles
- **THEN** Decoded page data for the titles is returned.

### Sub-component: Field

> Implements: `field`

Extracts a field from card page data.

#### Requirement: ExtractField

The system SHALL the field operation must provide the requested field value from the supplied data.

#### Scenario: FieldPresent
- **GIVEN** Card page data containing the requested field
- **WHEN** field is called for that field
- **THEN** The corresponding field value is returned.

### Sub-component: PageGet

> Implements: `page.get`

Retrieves a value from a page mapping.

#### Requirement: GetPageValue

The system SHALL the page lookup must return the value associated with the requested key when present.

#### Scenario: PageKeyPresent
- **GIVEN** A page mapping containing the requested key
- **WHEN** page.get is called with that key
- **THEN** The associated value is returned.

### Sub-component: RegexSearch

> Implements: `re.search`

Finds the first text match for a regular expression.

#### Requirement: FindRegexMatch

The system SHALL the search operation shall identify the first match of the supplied regular expression in the input text.

#### Scenario: MatchingTextFound
- **GIVEN** Input text containing a match for the expression
- **WHEN** re.search is applied
- **THEN** The first matching result is returned.

### Sub-component: RegexSplit

> Implements: `re.split`

Splits text at occurrences of a regular expression.

#### Requirement: SplitText

The system SHALL the split operation shall divide input text at matches of the supplied regular expression.

#### Scenario: DelimiterPresent
- **GIVEN** Input text containing a match for the expression
- **WHEN** re.split is applied
- **THEN** The text is returned as segments separated at matching positions.

### Sub-component: RegexSub

> Implements: `re.sub`

Replaces regular expression matches in text.

#### Requirement: ReplaceRegexMatches

The system SHALL the substitution operation shall replace matches of the supplied regular expression with the requested replacement.

#### Scenario: MatchReplaced
- **GIVEN** Input text containing a match for the expression
- **WHEN** re.sub is applied with a replacement
- **THEN** The matching text is replaced in the returned text.

### Sub-component: OutSetdefault

> Implements: `out.setdefault`

Provides or initializes an output entry for a key.

#### Requirement: EnsureOutputEntry

The system SHALL the output mapping operation must preserve an existing value for a key or initialize the key with its default value.

#### Scenario: OutputEntryAbsent
- **GIVEN** The output mapping does not contain the requested key
- **WHEN** out.setdefault is called with a default value
- **THEN** The key is initialized with that value and the value is returned.

### Sub-component: Sleep

> Implements: `time.sleep`

Delays execution for a specified duration.

#### Requirement: DelayExecution

The system SHALL the delay operation shall suspend execution for the requested duration.

#### Scenario: PositiveDuration
- **GIVEN** A positive delay duration
- **WHEN** time.sleep is called
- **THEN** Execution resumes after the requested duration.

### Sub-component: Rows

> Implements: `rows`

Parses table data into rows, optionally using text input.

#### Requirement: ParseTableRows

The system SHALL the row parser shall return rows parsed from the supplied table data, honoring whether text input is requested.

#### Scenario: TableParsed
- **GIVEN** A table containing valid row data
- **WHEN** rows is called with the table and text option
- **THEN** The table data is returned as parsed rows.

### Sub-component: GlobalCards

> Implements: `global_cards`

Builds a mapping from unit names to card identifiers, names, pages, and revisions using Global Vision Card pages.

The system SHALL the global card lookup must return available global vision card information for the requested card identifiers, associating each unit name with its card id, card name, page, and revision.

#### Scenario: MatchingGlobalCardsFound
- **GIVEN** Requested card identifiers with corresponding Global Vision Card pages
- **WHEN** global_cards is called
- **THEN** The returned mapping contains the matching unit and card information.

- **WHEN** cards.setdefault is called with a default value
- **THEN** The key is initialized with that value and the value is returned.

> `source/exvius/build_raid_archive.py`

### Requirement: BuildRaidArchive

The system SHALL the builder shall read the raid reference data, reconstruct raid records, and write a normalized archive with a content hash.

#### Scenario: BuildArchiveFromAvailableData
- **GIVEN** The raid reference data and wiki archive are available
- **WHEN** main is called
- **THEN** The reconstructed raid data is written as a normalized archive

### Requirement: ExtractTemplates

The system SHALL the builder shall extract named templates from wiki text for structured processing.

- **Implementation**: `templates::source/exvius/build_raid_archive.py`

#### Scenario: MatchingTemplatesExist
- **GIVEN** Wiki text contains templates with the requested name
- **WHEN** templates is called
- **THEN** The matching template contents are returned

### Requirement: NormalizeWikiText

The system SHALL the builder shall convert wiki markup and html entities into plain text.

- **Implementation**: `plain::source/exvius/build_raid_archive.py`

#### Scenario: MarkupAndEntitiesArePresent
- **GIVEN** The input contains wiki markup or HTML entities
- **WHEN** plain is called
- **THEN** A plain text representation is returned

### Requirement: BuildNameTables

The system SHALL the builder shall load archived wiki data and construct lookup tables for resolving item names.

- **Implementation**: `name_tables::source/exvius/build_raid_archive.py`

#### Scenario: ArchiveContainsNameData
- **GIVEN** The wiki archive contains item name records
- **WHEN** name_tables is called
- **THEN** Lookup tables are returned for resolving item names

### Requirement: ParseReward

The system SHALL the builder shall interpret a reward value and resolve its item and quantity using category-specific lookup data and context.

#### Scenario: RewardCanBeResolved
- **GIVEN** The reward value and lookup data identify an item and quantity
- **WHEN** reward is called
- **THEN** A parsed reward with the resolved item information is returned

#### Scenario: RewardCannotBeResolved
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The reward value does not identify a valid item
- **WHEN** reward is called
- **THEN** The unresolved or invalid reward is handled as an error

### Requirement: ParseMilestones

The system SHALL the builder shall parse milestone text into milestone records, resolving each reward and tracking unresolved entries.

- **Implementation**: `milestones::source/exvius/build_raid_archive.py`

#### Scenario: MilestonesContainResolvableRewards
- **GIVEN** Milestone text includes reward entries identifiable through the lookup tables
- **WHEN** milestones is called
- **THEN** Unique parsed milestone records are returned

#### Scenario: MilestonesContainUnresolvedRewards
- **GIVEN** Milestone text includes rewards that cannot be resolved
- **WHEN** milestones is called
- **THEN** Unresolved entries are tracked while parsed milestone records are returned

### Requirement: DeriveItemKey

The system SHALL the builder shall derive a normalized key for matching an item value across name lookup data.

- **Implementation**: `item_key::source/exvius/build_raid_archive.py`

#### Scenario: ItemValueIsProvided
- **GIVEN** An item value is available
- **WHEN** item_key is called
- **THEN** A normalized key suitable for lookup is returned

- **WHEN** the block is called

### Sub-component: ValueError

> Implements: `ValueError`

Represents an invalid or unresolved value condition.

> `source/exvius/build_raid_rewards.py`

### Requirement: BuildRaidRewards

The system SHALL the builder shall read and parse the raid reference data, extract and normalize reward milestones, and write the resulting rewards data with a content hash.

#### Scenario: GenerateRewardsFromValidReferenceData
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The source file contains valid raid data with reward tables
- **WHEN** main is called
- **THEN** The builder writes normalized reward milestones and a content hash to the output file

#### Scenario: RejectInvalidSourceData
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The source file is missing required data or contains invalid JSON
- **WHEN** main is called
- **THEN** The builder raises an error instead of producing a valid rewards output

### Requirement: ExtractNames

The system SHALL the builder shall collect names from a reward table into a name-indexed result.

- **Implementation**: `names::source/exvius/build_raid_rewards.py`

#### Scenario: CollectNamesFromATable
- **GIVEN** A reward table contains entries with names
- **WHEN** names is called with the table
- **THEN** The result includes the names extracted from those entries


## Sub-components

> `RaidRewardsBuilder` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: TableNameExtraction

> Implements: `names`

Extracts names from a table while initializing missing result entries.

#### Requirement: LoadTableNames

The system SHALL the operation shall parse the table’s json data and provide the parsed names.

#### Scenario: ValidTableData
- **GIVEN** A table contains valid JSON data with names
- **WHEN** names is called with the table
- **THEN** The parsed names are returned or made available to the caller.

#### Requirement: InitializeMissingResult

The system SHALL the operation shall ensure a result entry exists for each required name.

#### Scenario: MissingResultEntry
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A parsed name has no corresponding result entry
- **WHEN** names processes the table
- **THEN** A default result entry is created for that name.

### Sub-component: ValueErrorHandling

> Implements: `ValueError`

Represents invalid value conditions encountered while building raid rewards data.

#### Requirement: ReportInvalidValue

The system SHALL the operation must surface a valueerror when an input value cannot be accepted.

#### Scenario: InvalidInputValue
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A value violates an expected input constraint
- **WHEN** The value is processed
- **THEN** A ValueError is raised.

### Sub-component: JsonParsing

> Implements: `json.loads`

Parses serialized JSON data used by the raid rewards builder.

#### Requirement: ParseJsonData

The system SHALL the operation shall convert valid json text into its corresponding data value.

#### Scenario: ValidJson
- **GIVEN** The input is syntactically valid JSON
- **WHEN** json.loads processes it
- **THEN** The corresponding data value is produced.

#### Scenario: MalformedJson
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The input is malformed JSON
- **WHEN** json.loads processes it
- **THEN** A parsing error is raised.

### Sub-component: SourceTextReading

> Implements: `SOURCE.read_text`

Reads the raid reference source file as text for processing.

#### Requirement: ReadSourceText

The system SHALL the operation shall provide the contents of the source file as text.

#### Scenario: SourceAvailable
- **GIVEN** The source file exists and is readable
- **WHEN** SOURCE.read_text is called
- **THEN** The file contents are returned as text.

### Sub-component: TextSubstitution

> Implements: `re.sub`

Replaces text matching a regular expression in the source content.

#### Requirement: ReplaceMatchingText

The system SHALL the operation shall replace text matching the specified pattern.

#### Scenario: PatternMatches
- **GIVEN** The source text contains a match for the pattern
- **WHEN** re.sub is applied
- **THEN** The matching text is replaced.

#### Scenario: PatternDoesNotMatch
- **GIVEN** The source text contains no match for the pattern
- **WHEN** re.sub is applied
- **THEN** The source text is returned without matching text replacements.

### Sub-component: PatternSearch

> Implements: `re.search`

Finds the first occurrence of a regular expression in text.

#### Requirement: FindPattern

The system SHALL the operation shall identify the first match for the supplied pattern when one exists.

#### Scenario: MatchFound
- **GIVEN** The text contains a match for the pattern
- **WHEN** re.search is applied
- **THEN** A match object for the first occurrence is returned.

#### Scenario: NoMatchFound
- **GIVEN** The text contains no match for the pattern
- **WHEN** re.search is applied
- **THEN** No match is returned.

### Sub-component: MatchStartPosition

> Implements: `match.start`

Obtains the starting position of a regular expression match.

#### Requirement: GetMatchStart

The system SHALL the operation shall report the start index of the match.

#### Scenario: MatchedTextHasStart
- **GIVEN** A valid match object exists
- **WHEN** match.start is called
- **THEN** The index where the match begins is returned.

### Sub-component: NamePrefixCheck

> Implements: `name.startswith`

Determines whether a name begins with a specified prefix.

#### Requirement: CheckNamePrefix

The system SHALL the operation shall indicate whether the name starts with the requested prefix.

#### Scenario: PrefixMatches
- **GIVEN** The name begins with the requested prefix
- **WHEN** name.startswith is evaluated
- **THEN** The result indicates a prefix match.

#### Scenario: PrefixDoesNotMatch
- **GIVEN** The name does not begin with the requested prefix
- **WHEN** name.startswith is evaluated
- **THEN** The result indicates no prefix match.

### Sub-component: PatternPrefixMatch

> Implements: `re.match`

Checks whether a regular expression matches at the beginning of text.

#### Requirement: MatchTextPrefix

The system SHALL the operation shall return a match when the pattern matches at the start of the text.

#### Scenario: PatternMatchesAtStart
- **GIVEN** The text begins with content matching the pattern
- **WHEN** re.match is applied
- **THEN** A match object is returned.

#### Scenario: PatternOnlyMatchesLater
- **GIVEN** The pattern occurs only after the beginning of the text
- **WHEN** re.match is applied
- **THEN** No match is returned.

### Sub-component: TableLookup

> Implements: `tables.get`

Retrieves a table value by key while allowing a default when the key is absent.

#### Requirement: RetrieveTable

The system SHALL the operation shall return the value associated with a key or the supplied default when absent.

#### Scenario: TableExists
- **GIVEN** The key exists in the tables collection
- **WHEN** tables.get is called
- **THEN** The associated table value is returned.

#### Scenario: TableMissing
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The key does not exist in the tables collection
- **WHEN** tables.get is called
- **THEN** The supplied default is returned.

### Sub-component: MilestoneCollection

> Implements: `milestones.append`

Adds a milestone value to the raid rewards milestone collection.

#### Requirement: AddMilestone

The system SHALL the operation shall add the provided milestone to the collection.

#### Scenario: MilestoneAdded
- **GIVEN** A milestone value is ready for collection
- **WHEN** milestones.append is called
- **THEN** The milestone is included in the collection.

### Sub-component: SourceHashing

> Implements: `hashlib.sha256`

Creates a SHA-256 digest context for source data integrity or identification.

#### Requirement: CreateSourceDigest

The system SHALL the operation shall provide a sha-256 digest for the supplied source bytes.

#### Scenario: DigestGenerated
- **GIVEN** Source bytes are provided
- **WHEN** hashlib.sha256 processes them
- **THEN** A SHA-256 digest can be obtained.

### Sub-component: SourceByteReading

> Implements: `SOURCE.read_bytes`

Reads the raid reference source file as bytes for digesting or binary-safe processing.

#### Requirement: ReadSourceBytes

The system SHALL the operation shall provide the exact bytes stored in the source file.

#### Scenario: SourceBytesAvailable
- **GIVEN** The source file exists and is readable
- **WHEN** SOURCE.read_bytes is called
- **THEN** The file contents are returned as bytes.

### Sub-component: OutputFileWriting

> Implements: `OUT.write_text`

Writes the generated raid rewards data as text to the output file.

#### Requirement: WriteRewardsOutput

The system SHALL the operation shall write the provided text to the output file.

#### Scenario: OutputWritten
- **GIVEN** Generated rewards text and a writable output path are available
- **WHEN** OUT.write_text is called
- **THEN** The output file contains the provided text.

### Sub-component: JsonSerialization

> Implements: `json.dumps`

Serializes generated raid rewards data into JSON text.

#### Requirement: SerializeRewards

The system SHALL the operation shall serialize the supplied data into json text.

#### Scenario: SerializableRewards
- **GIVEN** The rewards data can be represented in JSON
- **WHEN** json.dumps is called
- **THEN** JSON text representing the rewards data is returned.

### Sub-component: RaidRewardBuildStart

> Implements: `start`

Coordinates the start of raid reward construction using a specification and request.

#### Requirement: StartRewardBuild

The system SHALL the operation shall invoke the requested call with the provided party context.

#### Scenario: BuildStarted
- **GIVEN** A specification and request are provided
- **WHEN** start is called
- **THEN** The call is made with the applicable party context and its result is returned.

> `source/exvius/build_release_data.py`

### Requirement: BuildReleaseData

The system SHALL the service shall collect eligible release inputs, package them into output parts, and produce a manifest with revision and integrity information.

#### Scenario: BuildReleaseData
- **GIVEN** Eligible content inputs exist and the requested output location is available
- **WHEN** build is called with a tag, revision, and part limit
- **THEN** The inputs are packaged into release archives and a manifest is written with the resulting archive
metadata

#### Scenario: RejectInvalidReleaseInputs
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The source content or release metadata is invalid
- **WHEN** build attempts to collect or package the inputs
- **THEN** The build fails with a validation error

### Requirement: AddClientAssets

The system SHALL the service shall add the small client resource pack and update the existing release manifest without rebuilding unchanged large data parts.

- **Implementation**: `add_client_assets::source/exvius/build_release_data.py`

#### Scenario: AddClientResourcePack
- **GIVEN** An existing release output and manifest are available
- **WHEN** add_client_assets is called
- **THEN** The client resource pack is added and the manifest is updated

### Requirement: PackageClientAssets

The system SHALL the service shall combine selected original apk resources with available overlay resources into a client asset archive.

- **Implementation**: `client_assets::source/exvius/build_release_data.py`

#### Scenario: PackageOriginalAndOverlayResources
- **GIVEN** The original APK resources and any applicable overlays are available
- **WHEN** client_assets is called
- **THEN** The resources are packaged into an archive suitable for Android's standard resource loader

### Requirement: CollectReleaseInputs

The system SHALL the service shall discover eligible content files and derive release metadata from the source tree.

- **Implementation**: `inputs::source/exvius/build_release_data.py`

#### Scenario: CollectEligibleContent
- **GIVEN** The source tree contains supported content and version metadata
- **WHEN** inputs is called
- **THEN** Eligible files and derived metadata are returned for release packaging

#### Scenario: RejectInvalidSourceEntries
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The source tree contains an invalid input or malformed version metadata
- **WHEN** inputs is called
- **THEN** Input collection fails with a validation error

### Requirement: CalculateSha256

The system SHALL the service shall calculate the sha-256 digest of a file for release integrity metadata.

#### Scenario: CalculateFileDigest
- **GIVEN** A readable file exists
- **WHEN** sha256 is called with its path
- **THEN** The file's SHA-256 digest is returned


## Sub-components

> `ReleaseDataBuilder` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: Sha256

> Implements: `sha256`

Computes the SHA-256 digest of a file's contents.

#### Requirement: ComputeFileDigest

The system SHALL the function shall read the file at the supplied path and return its sha-256 digest as a hexadecimal string.

#### Scenario: DigestExistingFile
- **GIVEN** A readable file with known contents
- **WHEN** sha256 is called with its path
- **THEN** The returned hexadecimal digest matches the SHA-256 digest of those contents.

### Sub-component: OutputDirectory

> Implements: `output.mkdir`

Ensures the output directory exists for release data generation.

#### Requirement: EnsureOutputDirectory

The system SHALL the output directory shall exist after the operation completes.

#### Scenario: CreateMissingOutputDirectory
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The output directory does not exist
- **WHEN** The directory creation operation runs
- **THEN** The output directory exists afterward.

- **GIVEN** A valid archive path and supported mode
- **WHEN** The archive is opened
- **THEN** An archive handle is available for archive operations.

- **THEN** Metadata for the archive entries is returned.

- **WHEN** The prefix check runs
- **THEN** The result indicates a match.

### Sub-component: ArchiveEntryDirectoryCheck

### Sub-component: NameParts

### Requirement: BuildRegularOffers

The system SHALL the service shall build regular reward summon offers using the quantities, caps, bonuses, and featured unit data specified for the banner.

#### Scenario: RegularBannerOffers
- **GIVEN** A banner, digest, and rarity information are available
- **WHEN** regular_offers is called
- **THEN** The banner is updated with offers reflecting the specified quantities, caps, bonuses, and featured
units

### Requirement: BuildDestructionBoxes

The system SHALL the service shall provide recovered destruction box rewards with their specified quantities and material types.

- **Implementation**: `destruction_boxes::source/exvius/build_reward_summons.py`

#### Scenario: RecoveredBoxContents
- **GIVEN** Destruction box reward definitions are requested
- **WHEN** destruction_boxes is called
- **THEN** The returned boxes contain the recovered rewards, quantities, and material types

### Requirement: UpdateArenaRewards

The system SHALL the service shall update arena reward data.

- **Implementation**: `update_arena::source/exvius/build_reward_summons.py`

#### Scenario: ArenaRewardUpdate
- **GIVEN** Arena reward data is available for updating
- **WHEN** update_arena is called
- **THEN** Arena reward data is updated

### Requirement: UpdateKingMogRewards

The system SHALL the service shall update king mog reward data.

- **Implementation**: `king_mog::source/exvius/build_reward_summons.py`

#### Scenario: KingMogRewardUpdate
- **GIVEN** King Mog reward data is available for updating
- **WHEN** king_mog is called
- **THEN** King Mog reward data is updated


## Sub-components

> `Reward Summon Builder` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: Item

> Implements: `item`

Creates a reward item representation from its kind, identifier, and count.

#### Requirement: CreateRewardItem

The system SHALL the function shall represent the supplied item kind, identifier, and count as a reward item.

#### Scenario: ItemWithSuppliedValues
- **GIVEN** A kind, item identifier, and count are supplied
- **WHEN** The reward item is created
- **THEN** The result represents the supplied kind, identifier, and count

### Sub-component: DestructionBoxes

> Implements: `destruction_boxes`

Builds the recovered Global reward box quantities for each material type.

#### Requirement: BuildRecoveredRewardBoxes

The system SHALL the function shall provide recovered global reward box quantities, including each material type.

#### Scenario: IncludeMaterialQuantities
- **GIVEN** Recovered Global reward data is available
- **WHEN** Reward boxes are built
- **THEN** The result includes the recovered quantities for each material type

#### Scenario: RejectInvalidRewardData
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The reward data cannot be decoded
- **WHEN** Reward boxes are built
- **THEN** A ValueError is raised

### Sub-component: Collaboration

> Implements: `_collaboration`

Handles collaboration-specific reward data used by the summon builder.

#### Requirement: ProcessCollaborationRewards

The system SHALL the collaboration block must handle collaboration-specific reward data when building summon offers.

#### Scenario: ProcessCollaborationData
- **GIVEN** Collaboration-specific reward data is present
- **WHEN** The collaboration block is processed
- **THEN** A collaboration reward result is produced from that data

### Sub-component: UnitMst

> Implements: `unit_mst`

Loads unit master data used to determine unit-related reward mappings.

#### Requirement: LoadUnitMasterData

The system SHALL the function shall load unit master data for reward processing.

#### Scenario: LoadUnitRecords
- **GIVEN** A unit master data source is available
- **WHEN** Unit master data is requested
- **THEN** The loaded unit records are returned

#### Scenario: PreserveEventMoogleTrustTarget
- **GIVEN** An event moogle has a specific trust target
- **WHEN** Unit master data is used for reward mapping
- **THEN** The event moogle retains its specific trust target

#### Scenario: MapExSpecificStmrMoogleTargets
- **GIVEN** EX-specific STMR moogle records and Global target mappings are present
- **WHEN** Unit master data is used for reward mapping
- **THEN** The EX-specific STMR moogle and Global target mapping are preserved

#### Scenario: BindEx2MoogleToMatchingUnit
- **GIVEN** An EX2-bound moogle corresponds to one unit
- **WHEN** The moogle reward is mapped
- **THEN** The STMR reward is granted only to its matching unit

### Sub-component: ValueError

> Implements: `ValueError`

Represents invalid reward data encountered during reward processing.

#### Requirement: SignalInvalidRewardData

The system SHALL invalid reward data must be reported as a valueerror when the relevant processing block rejects it.

#### Scenario: RaiseForInvalidData
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Reward data violates the block's accepted requirements
- **WHEN** The data is processed
- **THEN** A ValueError is raised

### Sub-component: UnitRootsByRarity

> Implements: `unit_roots_by_rarity`

Groups unit root identifiers by rarity from loaded unit records.

#### Scenario: GroupRecordsByRarity
- **GIVEN** Unit records with rarity information are available
- **WHEN** Unit roots are grouped
- **THEN** Each root identifier is associated with its rarity group

#### Scenario: HandleMissingRarity
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->

- **WHEN** Unit roots are grouped

### Requirement: BuildSummonArtIndex

The system SHALL the service shall collect banner and detail art dimensions and map qualifying detail images to a wide image from the same campaign.

#### Scenario: IndexBannerArt
- **GIVEN** Banners contain banner, detail banner, or detail extra image references
- **WHEN** The index is built
- **THEN** Each distinct referenced image is recorded with its original width and height

#### Scenario: SelectWideAlternatives
- **GIVEN** A banner image has an aspect ratio of at least 2.8 and a detail image from the same campaign has an
aspect ratio below 2.8
- **WHEN** The index is built
- **THEN** The detail image is mapped to that banner image as a wide alternative

#### Scenario: WriteIndex
- **GIVEN** The banner art index has been built
- **WHEN** The operation completes
- **THEN** A compact JSON index is written to the server summon art file

> `source/exvius/colosseum_reconstruction.py`

### Requirement: ExtractEnemyStatsFromWikiText

The system SHALL the service shall parse an enemy’s wiki text and return its stats, preserving separate body and limb rows for multipart enemies.

- **Implementation**: `wiki_stats::source/exvius/colosseum_reconstruction.py`

#### Scenario: MultipartEnemy
- **GIVEN** Wiki text contains separate body and limb stat rows
- **WHEN** wiki_stats is called
- **THEN** The returned data preserves separate rows for the body and limbs

### Requirement: ExtractEnemyResistances

The system SHALL the service shall identify and normalize an enemy’s elemental and ailment resistances from wiki text for the requested round.

- **Implementation**: `resistance::source/exvius/colosseum_reconstruction.py`

#### Scenario: ResistanceFieldsPresent
- **GIVEN** Wiki text contains elemental or ailment resistance fields
- **WHEN** resistance is called
- **THEN** The matching resistance values are returned in normalized form

### Requirement: ReconstructAnEnemy

The system SHALL the service shall build an enemy record from its name, round, local identifier, and expected level, incorporating parsed stats and resistances.

#### Scenario: ValidEnemyData
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A matching enemy is described in the wiki text
- **WHEN** enemy is called with its expected level and identifier
- **THEN** A reconstructed enemy record is returned with parsed stats and resistances

#### Scenario: InvalidEnemyData
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Required enemy information is missing or inconsistent
- **WHEN** enemy is called
- **THEN** The service raises a ValueError

### Requirement: ReconstructARound

The system SHALL the service shall assemble reconstructed enemy records and provenance for the requested colosseum progress from wiki data and missing-entry definitions.

#### Scenario: RoundDataAvailable
- **GIVEN** Wiki data and missing-entry definitions describe the requested progress
- **WHEN** round is called
- **THEN** The service returns the round groups, monster rows, multipart rows, and provenance

#### Scenario: InvalidProgress
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The progress value does not match an accepted format
- **WHEN** round is called
- **THEN** The service raises a ValueError


## Sub-components

> `ColosseumReconstructor` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: TextNormalization

> Implements: `normalized`

Normalizes text for consistent comparison and parsing.

#### Requirement: NormalizeText

The system SHALL the function must return text with the substitutions required for consistent parsing applied.

#### Scenario: SubstitutionsAreApplied
- **GIVEN** Text contains characters or formatting matched by the normalization rules
- **WHEN** The text is normalized
- **THEN** The returned text reflects those substitutions.

### Sub-component: MatchIteration

> Implements: `re.finditer`

Finds all non-overlapping matches of a pattern in text for subsequent processing.

#### Requirement: IterateMatches

The system SHALL the operation must make each pattern match available for processing in source order.

#### Scenario: MultipleMatchesAreFound
- **GIVEN** Text contains multiple matches for the pattern
- **WHEN** Matches are iterated
- **THEN** Each match is available in source order.

### Sub-component: MatchGroup

> Implements: `h.group`

Retrieves a captured value from a regex match.

#### Requirement: RetrieveCapturedValue

The system SHALL the operation must return the requested captured value from the match.

#### Scenario: CaptureExists
- **GIVEN** Match h contains the requested capture
- **WHEN** The capture is retrieved
- **THEN** The captured value is returned.

### Sub-component: MatchEnd

> Implements: `h.end`

Returns the end position of a regex match or capture.

#### Requirement: GetMatchEnd

The system SHALL the operation must report the end offset of the requested match or capture.

#### Scenario: MatchHasAnEndOffset
- **GIVEN** Match h identifies text ending at a known offset
- **WHEN** Its end position is requested
- **THEN** The corresponding end offset is returned.

### Sub-component: TemplateStart

> Implements: `template.start`

Determines where a template match begins in the source text.

#### Requirement: GetTemplateStart

The system SHALL the operation must return the starting offset of the template match.

#### Scenario: TemplateBeginsAtKnownOffset
- **GIVEN** A template match begins at a known position
- **WHEN** Its start position is requested
- **THEN** The starting offset is returned.

### Sub-component: PatternSearch

> Implements: `re.search`

Searches text for the first occurrence matching a pattern.

#### Requirement: SearchPattern

The system SHALL the operation must return the first match when one exists and indicate no match otherwise.

#### Scenario: PatternOccursInText
- **GIVEN** Text contains an occurrence of the pattern
- **WHEN** The text is searched
- **THEN** The first matching occurrence is returned.

### Sub-component: RankedMatchCollection

> Implements: `ranked.append`

Adds a candidate match to the ordered collection used to select a result.

#### Requirement: AppendRankedCandidate

The system SHALL the operation must add the candidate to the ranked collection for later selection.

#### Scenario: CandidateIsRecorded
- **GIVEN** A candidate has been evaluated for ranking
- **WHEN** The candidate is appended
- **THEN** The ranked collection contains the candidate.

### Sub-component: MatchExtraction

> Implements: `re.findall`

Extracts all occurrences of a pattern from text.

#### Requirement: ExtractAllMatches

The system SHALL the operation must return all pattern matches found in the text.

#### Scenario: TextContainsRepeatedMatches
- **GIVEN** Text contains more than one occurrence of the pattern
- **WHEN** All matches are extracted
- **THEN** Each matching occurrence is included in the result.

### Sub-component: ChosenMatchGroup

> Implements: `chosen.group`

Retrieves a captured value from the selected match.

#### Requirement: RetrieveChosenCapture

The system SHALL the operation must return the requested capture from the chosen match.

#### Scenario: ChosenMatchContainsCapture
- **GIVEN** The chosen match contains the requested capture
- **WHEN** The capture is retrieved
- **THEN** The captured value is returned.

### Sub-component: TrimWhitespace

> Implements: `raw.strip`

Removes leading and trailing whitespace from a raw value.

#### Requirement: StripRawValue

The system SHALL the operation must return the raw value without leading or trailing whitespace.

#### Scenario: ValueHasSurroundingWhitespace
- **GIVEN** A raw value has leading and trailing whitespace
- **WHEN** The value is stripped
- **THEN** The returned value has no surrounding whitespace.

### Sub-component: LowercaseField

> Implements: `field.lower`

Converts a field label to lowercase for normalized comparison.

#### Requirement: NormalizeFieldCase

The system SHALL the operation must return the field label in lowercase.

#### Scenario: FieldContainsUppercaseLetters
- **GIVEN** A field label contains uppercase letters
- **WHEN** The label is lowercased
- **THEN** The returned label is lowercase.

### Sub-component: FullPatternMatch

> Implements: `re.fullmatch`

Checks whether an entire value matches a pattern.

#### Requirement: MatchEntireValue

The system SHALL the operation must report a match only when the entire value satisfies the pattern.

#### Scenario: ValueHasUnmatchedTrailingContent
- **GIVEN** A value begins with pattern-matching content but includes extra trailing content
- **WHEN** The entire value is checked
- **THEN** The operation reports no full match.

### Sub-component: TrimTrailingWhitespace

> Implements: `raw.rstrip`

Removes trailing whitespace from a raw value.

#### Requirement: StripTrailingWhitespace

The system SHALL the operation must return the raw value without trailing whitespace while preserving leading content.

#### Scenario: ValueHasTrailingWhitespace
- **GIVEN** A raw value ends with whitespace
- **WHEN** Trailing whitespace is removed
- **THEN** The returned value has no trailing whitespace.

### Sub-component: OrchestrationStart

> Implements: `start`

Coordinates reconstruction for a specification and request.

#### Requirement: ReconstructRequestedData

The system SHALL the function must use the specification and request to produce the requested reconstruction result.

#### Scenario: ValidSpecificationAndRequest
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A valid specification and request are provided
- **WHEN** Reconstruction starts
- **THEN** The function returns the result produced by its reconstruction flow.

### Sub-component: ValidationError

> Implements: `ValueError`

Signals that an input or reconstructed value violates an expected value constraint.

#### Requirement: ReportInvalidValue

The system SHALL the operation must raise a value error when a value cannot satisfy the required value constraints.

#### Scenario: InvalidValueIsEncountered
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** An input or derived value violates a required constraint
- **WHEN** The violation is handled
- **THEN** A ValueError is raised.

### Sub-component: DefaultDictionary

> Implements: `defaultdict`

Provides a mapping that supplies default values for missing keys.

#### Requirement: CreateDefaultMapping

The system SHALL the operation must provide a mapping whose missing-key behavior supplies the configured default.

#### Scenario: MissingKeyIsAccessed
<!-- openlore-test: priority=high tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A mapping is configured with a default factory and does not contain a key
- **WHEN** The missing key is accessed
- **THEN** The mapping supplies the factory's default value.

### Sub-component: JsonDecoding

> Implements: `json.loads`

Decodes a JSON document into its corresponding data value.

#### Requirement: DecodeJson

The system SHALL the operation must return the data represented by valid json input.

#### Scenario: ValidJSONInput
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The input is a valid JSON document
- **WHEN** The document is decoded
- **THEN** Its represented data is returned.

### Sub-component: ReferenceReading

> Implements: `REFERENCE.read_text`

Reads the reference document as text for use in reconstruction.

#### Requirement: ReadReferenceText

The system SHALL the operation must return the text contents of the reference document.

#### Scenario: ReferenceDocumentIsReadable
- **GIVEN** The reference document exists and is readable
- **WHEN** Its text is read
- **THEN** The document contents are returned as text.

### Sub-component: ContentHashing

> Implements: `hashlib.sha256`

Creates a SHA-256 digest for content identification or integrity tracking.

#### Requirement: CreateSha256Digest

The system SHALL the operation must produce a sha-256 digest for the supplied content.

#### Scenario: ContentIsHashed
- **GIVEN** Content is supplied for hashing
- **WHEN** A SHA-256 digest is computed
- **THEN** A digest corresponding to the content is produced.

### Sub-component: BaseLoading

> Implements: `base.load`

Loads the existing base data used to reconstruct enemy and round information.

#### Requirement: LoadBaseData

The system SHALL the operation must return the base data available to the reconstruction flow.

#### Scenario: BaseDataIsAvailable
- **GIVEN** The configured base source contains readable data
- **WHEN** The base is loaded
- **THEN** The base data is returned.

### Sub-component: RowSorting

> Implements: `rows.sort`

Orders reconstructed rows according to the applicable ordering criteria.

#### Requirement: SortRows

The system SHALL the operation must order rows according to the supplied or default ordering criteria.

#### Scenario: RowsAreOutOfOrder
- **GIVEN** Rows contain values that do not follow the required order
- **WHEN** Rows are sorted
- **THEN** The rows follow the required ordering criteria.

### Sub-component: NameLookup

> Implements: `names.get`

Looks up a value associated with a name and provides the configured fallback when absent.

#### Requirement: LookupName

The system SHALL the operation must return the value associated with the name or the provided fallback when no value is associated.

#### Scenario: NameIsAbsent
- **GIVEN** The names mapping does not contain the requested name
- **WHEN** The name is looked up with a fallback
- **THEN** The fallback value is returned.

### Sub-component: WikiStatistics

> Implements: `wiki_stats`

Extracts enemy body and limb statistics from wiki text while preserving multipart enemy rows.

#### Requirement: ExtractWikiStats

The system SHALL the function must extract applicable statistics for the specified round and keep body and limb rows distinct for multipart enemies.

#### Scenario: MultipartEnemyHasSeparateRows
- **GIVEN** Wiki text describes an enemy with separate body and limb statistics
- **WHEN** Statistics are extracted for its round
- **THEN** Body and limb statistics are returned as distinct rows.

### Sub-component: ResistanceExtraction

> Implements: `resistance`

Extracts and selects resistance information from wiki text for a specified round.

#### Requirement: ExtractResistance

The system SHALL the function must identify applicable resistance information in the text and return the selected resistance value for the specified round.

#### Scenario: ResistanceEntryMatchesRound
- **GIVEN** Wiki text contains resistance information applicable to the specified round
- **WHEN** Resistance is extracted
- **THEN** The applicable resistance value is returned.

### Sub-component: TextQuoting

> Implements: `quote`

Converts text into the representation required for safe embedding in reconstructed output.

#### Requirement: QuoteValue

The system SHALL the operation must return a quoted representation of the supplied value suitable for its intended output context.

#### Scenario: ValueRequiresQuoting
- **GIVEN** A value contains characters requiring quoting in the output context
- **WHEN** The value is quoted
- **THEN** The returned representation preserves the value safely.

### Sub-component: DeepCopy

> Implements: `copy.deepcopy`

Creates an independent deep copy of a value for reconstruction.

#### Requirement: CopyValue

The system SHALL the operation must create a copy whose nested mutable values are independent of the source.

#### Scenario: NestedMutableValuesAreCopied
- **GIVEN** The source contains nested mutable values
- **WHEN** A deep copy is created
- **THEN** Changing a copied nested value does not change the source.

### Sub-component: LevelRounding

> Implements: `math.ceil`

Rounds a numeric value upward to the next integer boundary.

#### Requirement: RoundLevelUp

The system SHALL the operation must return the smallest integer greater than or equal to the supplied numeric value.

#### Scenario: FractionalValueIsRoundedUpward
- **GIVEN** The numeric value has a fractional component
- **WHEN** It is rounded upward
- **THEN** The result is the next integer above the value.

### Sub-component: RowUpdate

> Implements: `row.update`

Adds or replaces fields in a reconstructed row.

#### Requirement: UpdateRow

The system SHALL the operation must apply the supplied fields to the row, replacing values for keys that already exist.

#### Scenario: ExistingFieldIsUpdated
- **GIVEN** The row contains a field also present in the supplied mapping
- **WHEN** The row is updated
- **THEN** The row contains the supplied value for that field.

### Sub-component: RowAppend

> Implements: `rows.append`

Adds a reconstructed row to a row collection.

#### Requirement: AppendRow

The system SHALL the operation must add the supplied row to the end of the collection.

#### Scenario: RowIsAdded
- **GIVEN** A row collection and a reconstructed row are available
- **WHEN** The row is appended
- **THEN** The row appears at the end of the collection.

### Sub-component: BossMatchGroup

> Implements: `boss_match.group`

Retrieves a captured value from the boss match.

#### Requirement: RetrieveBossCapture

The system SHALL the operation must return the requested captured value from the boss match.

#### Scenario: BossMatchContainsCapture
- **GIVEN** The boss match contains the requested capture
- **WHEN** The capture is retrieved
- **THEN** The captured value is returned.

### Sub-component: EnemyReconstruction

> Implements: `enemy`

Builds enemy rows for a round using existing enemy data and applicable wiki statistics.

#### Requirement: ReconstructEnemy

The system SHALL the function must produce enemy rows for the given name, round, local identifier, and expected level, incorporating applicable wiki statistics and resistance data.

#### Scenario: EnemyHasMultipartStatistics
- **GIVEN** An enemy has applicable body and limb statistics in the wiki data
- **WHEN** The enemy is reconstructed for a round
- **THEN** The resulting rows preserve the enemy parts and their applicable statistics.

### Sub-component: MonsterRowAppend

> Implements: `monster_rows.append`

Adds a reconstructed monster row to the monster row collection.

#### Requirement: AppendMonsterRow

The system SHALL the operation must add the monster row to the end of the monster row collection.

#### Scenario: MonsterRowIsRecorded
- **GIVEN** A monster row has been reconstructed
- **WHEN** It is appended to the collection
- **THEN** The collection contains the monster row.

### Sub-component: PartRowExtension

> Implements: `part_rows.extend`

Adds multiple reconstructed part rows to the part row collection.

#### Requirement: ExtendPartRows

The system SHALL the operation must add each supplied part row to the end of the part row collection in order.

#### Scenario: MultiplePartRowsAreAdded
- **GIVEN** A sequence of reconstructed part rows is available
- **WHEN** The collection is extended
- **THEN** All supplied rows appear at the end in their original order.

### Sub-component: ProvenanceAppend

> Implements: `provenance.append`

Records provenance information for reconstructed data.

#### Requirement: RecordProvenance

The system SHALL the operation must add the provenance record to the provenance collection.

#### Scenario: ProvenanceRecordIsRecorded
- **GIVEN** A provenance record is available for reconstructed data
- **WHEN** The record is appended
- **THEN** The provenance collection contains the record.

### Sub-component: GroupAppend

> Implements: `groups.append`

Adds a reconstructed enemy group to the group collection.

#### Requirement: AppendGroup

The system SHALL the operation must add the enemy group to the end of the group collection.

#### Scenario: EnemyGroupIsRecorded
- **GIVEN** An enemy group has been reconstructed
- **WHEN** It is appended to the group collection
- **THEN** The group collection contains the enemy group.

> `source/exvius/decode_client_data.py`

### Requirement: ResolveClientManifest

The system SHALL the service shall validate the apk identity, inspect its client data, and return the manifest of recognized data files and their metadata.

- **Implementation**: `client_manifest::source/exvius/decode_client_data.py`

#### Scenario: ValidClientAPK
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** An APK with the expected client data and hash
- **WHEN** The client manifest is resolved
- **THEN** The service returns the recognized data-file manifest

#### Scenario: UnsupportedOrMalformedAPK
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** An APK with an unexpected identity or missing required data
- **WHEN** The client manifest is resolved
- **THEN** The service reports a validation error

### Requirement: ResolveNativeGetterManifest

The system SHALL the service shall identify literal-returning native getters and map their names to the strings they expose.

- **Implementation**: `getter_manifest::source/exvius/decode_client_data.py`

#### Scenario: RecognizedNativeGetters
- **GIVEN** Native client data containing supported literal-returning getters
- **WHEN** The getter manifest is resolved
- **THEN** The service returns the names and corresponding strings

#### Scenario: UnresolvableGetterAddress
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A getter refers to an address that cannot be mapped to a file offset
- **WHEN** The getter manifest is resolved
- **THEN** The service reports an error for the invalid address


## Sub-components

> `ClientManifestService` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: PathHandling

> Implements: `Path`

Represents filesystem paths used to locate and read the APK and its related files.

#### Requirement: RepresentFilesystemPath

The system SHALL the service shall interpret supplied filesystem locations as paths for manifest processing.

#### Scenario: AcceptApkPath
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A valid APK filesystem location
- **WHEN** The service constructs a path for it
- **THEN** A path representation for that location is available

### Sub-component: ValueError

> Implements: `ValueError`

Signals invalid inputs or data that cannot be processed according to the manifest and decoding contracts.

#### Requirement: RejectInvalidData

The system SHALL the service shall report invalid values when manifest inputs or encoded data cannot be processed.

#### Scenario: InvalidInputRejected
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A value violates a processing requirement
- **WHEN** The service processes that value
- **THEN** A ValueError is raised

### Sub-component: GetterManifest

> Implements: `getter_manifest`

Resolves literal-returning ARM64 native getters into a manifest without executing them.

#### Requirement: ResolveNativeGetters

The system SHALL the service shall identify literal-returning arm64 getters and associate their values with manifest entries without executing the getters.

#### Scenario: SelectNativeIndexedNames
- **GIVEN** Native data contains indexed event and enemy names
- **WHEN** The getter manifest is resolved
- **THEN** All names selected by the native index are included

#### Scenario: PreserveLargeTables
- **GIVEN** Native data contains large original lookup tables
- **WHEN** The getter manifest is resolved
- **THEN** The decoded tables match the original tables

### Sub-component: Sha256Hashing

> Implements: `hashlib.sha256`

Computes a SHA-256 digest for data used in client manifest processing.

#### Requirement: ComputeDigest

The system SHALL the service should compute a sha-256 digest for the relevant client data.

#### Scenario: DigestAvailable
- **GIVEN** Client data bytes are available
- **WHEN** The service computes their digest
- **THEN** A SHA-256 digest is available

### Sub-component: OpenApkArchive

Opens the APK archive so its embedded files can be inspected.

#### Scenario: OpenReadableApk
- **GIVEN** A readable APK archive exists at the supplied path
- **WHEN** The service opens the archive
- **THEN** Its embedded files are available for reading

### Sub-component: ReadArchiveEntry

The system SHALL the service shall return the bytes of a requested entry in the open apk archive.

#### Scenario: ReadExistingEntry
- **GIVEN** The APK archive contains the requested entry
- **WHEN** The service reads that entry
- **THEN** The entry bytes are returned

### Sub-component: FindTriplets

> Implements: `TRIPLET.finditer`

Finds all triplet-pattern matches in source data for manifest processing.

The system SHALL the service shall identify each occurrence matching the triplet pattern in the input.

#### Scenario: FindAllMatches
- **GIVEN** Input contains multiple triplet-pattern occurrences
- **WHEN** The service scans the input
- **THEN** Each matching occurrence is available for processing

### Sub-component: DecodeTextValue

Converts a byte value into text for manifest processing.

The system SHALL the service shall decode the supplied byte value into text.

- **GIVEN** A byte value contains valid text in the expected encoding
- **WHEN** The service decodes it
- **THEN** The corresponding text is returned

### Sub-component: ExtractMatchGroups

- **WHEN** The service extracts its groups
- **THEN** The captured values are returned in match order

### Sub-component: EncodeName

> Implements: `name.encode`

Encodes a manifest name as bytes for comparison or processing.

- **GIVEN** A manifest name is valid text
- **WHEN** The service encodes the name
- **THEN** The corresponding bytes are returned

### Sub-component: ReadNamedData

> Implements: `read`

Reads named client data while rejecting unsupported or invalid names.

The system SHALL the service shall read data identified by a supported name and shall reject names with an unsupported suffix.

#### Scenario: RejectUnsupportedSuffix
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A requested data name has an unsupported suffix
- **WHEN** The service reads the name
- **THEN** A ValueError is raised

#### Scenario: ReadSupportedName
- **GIVEN** A requested data name is supported
- **WHEN** The service reads the name
- **THEN** The corresponding data is returned

### Sub-component: AssembleClientGroups

> Implements: `groups`

Builds structured client data groups from player and banner inputs.

#### Requirement: BuildGroups

The system SHALL the service shall assemble the supported player and banner data into the output groups while preserving their associated rows and metadata.
