- **GIVEN** Player and banner inputs contain supported data rows
- **WHEN** The service builds the groups
- **THEN** The corresponding rows and metadata appear in the output groups

#### Scenario: PreserveExpectedGroupTypes
- **GIVEN** The inputs contain data for defined group categories
- **WHEN** The service builds the groups
- **THEN** Each category is represented by its expected data type

### Sub-component: CbcDecrypt

> Implements: `cbc_decrypt`

Decodes and decrypts base64-encoded AES-CBC data using the supplied key and initialization vector.

#### Requirement: DecryptCbcPayload

The system SHALL the service shall decode the base64 payload and decrypt it with the supplied key and iv, reporting invalid payloads as errors.

#### Scenario: DecryptValidPayload
- **GIVEN** A valid base64 AES-CBC payload and matching key and IV
- **WHEN** The service decrypts the payload
- **THEN** The original plaintext bytes are returned

#### Scenario: PreserveLargeTables
- **GIVEN** An encrypted payload contains large original tables
- **WHEN** The payload is decrypted and decoded
- **THEN** The resulting tables match the original tables

### Sub-component: DeriveKey16

> Implements: `key16`

Converts an eight-character EncodeKey token into a UTF-8 key padded with NUL bytes to 16 bytes.

#### Requirement: DerivePaddedKey

The system SHALL the service shall encode the token as utf-8 and produce the corresponding 16-byte, nul-padded key.

#### Scenario: PadEightCharacterToken
<!-- openlore-test: priority=high (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** An eight-character EncodeKey token is supplied
- **WHEN** The service derives the key
- **THEN** The UTF-8 token bytes are NUL-padded to 16 bytes

### Sub-component: AesEcbDecrypt

> Implements: `aes_ecb_decrypt`

Decrypts data using raw AES-128-ECB without applying padding.

#### Requirement: DecryptRawEcb

The system SHALL the service shall decrypt the input using aes-128-ecb without removing padding and shall report invalid key or cipher processing failures.

#### Scenario: DecryptValidCiphertext
- **GIVEN** A valid AES-128 key and ECB ciphertext are supplied
- **WHEN** The service decrypts the ciphertext
- **THEN** The raw plaintext bytes are returned without padding removal

### Sub-component: ParseLocalizedRecords

> Implements: `localized_records`

Parses localized text data into records while preserving its record values.

#### Requirement: ParseLocalizedText

The system SHALL the service shall decode localized input and parse its records while preserving localized keys and all value columns.

#### Scenario: PreserveLocalizedColumns
- **GIVEN** Localized input contains keys and multiple value columns
- **WHEN** The service parses the records
- **THEN** Localized keys and every value column are preserved

#### Scenario: RejectInvalidLocalizedData
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Localized input cannot be decoded or parsed
- **WHEN** The service parses the input
- **THEN** A ValueError is raised

### Sub-component: ParseJsonRecord

> Implements: `json_record`

Decodes raw data and parses it as a JSON record.

#### Requirement: ParseJsonData

The system SHALL the service shall decode and parse valid json input and shall reject data that cannot be decoded or parsed.

#### Scenario: ParseValidJson
- **GIVEN** Raw data contains valid JSON
- **WHEN** The service parses the data
- **THEN** The corresponding JSON record is returned

#### Scenario: RejectInvalidJson
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Raw data is invalid or cannot be decoded
- **WHEN** The service parses the data
- **THEN** A ValueError is raised

### Sub-component: DecodeRawText

> Implements: `raw.decode`

Decodes raw bytes into text for record parsing.

#### Requirement: DecodeRawBytes

The system SHALL the service shall decode raw bytes into text for subsequent parsing.

#### Scenario: DecodeValidRawText
- **GIVEN** Raw bytes represent valid text
- **WHEN** The service decodes the bytes
- **THEN** The corresponding text is returned

### Sub-component: StripLineWhitespace

> Implements: `line.strip`

Removes surrounding whitespace from a decoded input line before processing.

#### Requirement: NormalizeInputLine

The system SHALL the service should remove surrounding whitespace from each line before interpreting it.

#### Scenario: TrimLineBoundaries
- **GIVEN** An input line has surrounding whitespace
- **WHEN** The service prepares the line
- **THEN** The returned line has no surrounding whitespace

### Sub-component: DecodeBase64

> Implements: `base64.b64decode`

Converts base64-encoded input into bytes for decryption or parsing.

#### Requirement: DecodeEncodedBytes

The system SHALL the service shall convert valid base64 input into its corresponding bytes.

#### Scenario: DecodeValidBase64
- **GIVEN** Input contains valid base64 data
- **WHEN** The service decodes it
- **THEN** The corresponding bytes are returned

### Sub-component: ExtendRecords

> Implements: `records.extend`

Adds multiple parsed records to the current record collection.

#### Requirement: AppendRecordSequence

The system SHALL the service shall add every supplied record to the destination collection in input order.

#### Scenario: PreserveRecordOrder
- **GIVEN** A record sequence contains multiple records
- **WHEN** The service extends the destination collection
- **THEN** All records appear after existing records in input order

### Sub-component: AppendRecord

> Implements: `records.append`

Adds one parsed record to the current record collection.

#### Requirement: AddSingleRecord

The system SHALL the service shall add the supplied record to the end of the destination collection.

#### Scenario: AppendOneRecord
- **GIVEN** A parsed record and destination collection are available
- **WHEN** The service appends the record
- **THEN** The record is present at the end of the collection

### Sub-component: CollectValidEntry

> Implements: `valid.append`

Adds a successfully validated entry to the valid-entry collection.

#### Requirement: RetainValidEntry

The system SHALL the service shall add an entry to the valid-entry collection only after it has been accepted as valid.

#### Scenario: RetainAcceptedEntry
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** An entry has passed validation
- **WHEN** The service collects valid entries
- **THEN** The entry is included in the valid-entry collection

### Sub-component: DoublePaddedCbcScenario

> Implements: `test_double_padded_cbc_multiple_records_preserved`

Verifies that decoding double-padded CBC input preserves multiple records.

#### Requirement: PreserveMultipleRecords

The system SHALL the decoder shall preserve all records when processing double-padded cbc input.

#### Scenario: DecodeDoublePaddedRecords
- **GIVEN** Encrypted input uses double padding and contains multiple records
- **WHEN** The input is decoded
- **THEN** All original records are preserved

### Sub-component: NativeIndexSelectionScenario

> Implements: `test_native_index_selects_all_event_enemy_names`

Verifies that native index selection includes all event and enemy names.

#### Requirement: IncludeIndexedNames

The system SHALL the native index selection shall include every matching event and enemy name.

#### Scenario: SelectAllEventEnemyNames
- **GIVEN** Native index data references event and enemy names
- **WHEN** The names are selected
- **THEN** All referenced event and enemy names are included

### Sub-component: ClientManifest

> Implements: `client_manifest`

Resolves the client’s embedded data-file manifest from an APK, including native getter names and associated file metadata.

#### Requirement: ResolveApkManifest

The system SHALL the service shall inspect the apk and resolve its embedded data-file manifest, including native getter names and associated file metadata.

#### Scenario: ResolveManifestFromApk
- **GIVEN** An APK contains embedded data files and native getter information
- **WHEN** The service resolves its client manifest
- **THEN** The manifest includes the relevant names and file metadata

#### Scenario: RejectInvalidApkData
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The APK cannot be read or contains invalid manifest data
- **WHEN** The service resolves the manifest
- **THEN** A ValueError is raised

### Sub-component: Decode

> Implements: `decode`

Decrypts and parses raw client data into localized records or JSON records.

#### Requirement: DecodeClientData

The system SHALL the service shall decrypt and parse raw client data according to the supplied key and localization mode, and shall reject invalid data.

#### Scenario: DecodeLocalizedRecords
- **GIVEN** Valid encrypted data and a key are supplied with localized mode enabled
- **WHEN** The service decodes the data
- **THEN** Localized records are returned

#### Scenario: DecodeJsonRecords
- **GIVEN** Valid encrypted data and a key are supplied with localized mode disabled
- **WHEN** The service decodes the data
- **THEN** JSON records are returned

#### Scenario: PreserveDoublePaddedRecords
- **GIVEN** The encrypted input is double-padded CBC data containing multiple records
- **WHEN** The service decodes the input
- **THEN** All records are preserved

### Sub-component: FileExistsError

> Implements: `FileExistsError`

Signals that an output path already exists when the operation requires a new file.

#### Scenario: ExistingOutputRejected
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->

- **WHEN** The service attempts to create the output
- **THEN** A FileExistsError is raised

<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->

- **WHEN** The option is registered
- **THEN** The parser recognizes that option

### Sub-component: ParseArguments

Parses the command-line arguments supplied to the utility.

The system SHALL the utility shall parse supplied command-line arguments according to the registered options.

#### Scenario: ParseSupportedOptions
- **GIVEN** Arguments contain supported command-line options
- **WHEN** The utility parses them
- **THEN** The resulting values are available as parsed arguments

### Sub-component: CollectResults

> Implements: `results.append`

Adds a processed result to the collection returned by the utility.

> `source/exvius/decode_client_data.py`

### Requirement: DecodeClientData

The system SHALL the service shall decode client data using the supplied key and localized-data mode, preserving valid records and rejecting invalid data or keys.

- **Implementation**: `decode::source/exvius/decode_client_data.py`

#### Scenario: ValidEncryptedRecords
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Encoded client data and a valid key
- **WHEN** The data is decoded
- **THEN** The service returns the decoded records

#### Scenario: InvalidKeyOrPadding
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Encrypted client data that cannot be decrypted with the supplied key
- **WHEN** The data is decoded
- **THEN** The service reports a decoding error

#### Scenario: MultiplePaddedRecords
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Encoded data containing multiple valid records with padding
- **WHEN** The data is decoded
- **THEN** All valid records are preserved in the result

### Requirement: ParseJSONRecord

The system SHALL the service shall parse a decrypted record as json and report malformed json or invalid text.

- **Implementation**: `json_record::source/exvius/decode_client_data.py`

#### Scenario: ValidJSONRecord
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A record containing valid UTF-8 JSON
- **WHEN** The record is parsed
- **THEN** The JSON value is returned

#### Scenario: MalformedJSONRecord
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A record containing invalid UTF-8 or malformed JSON
- **WHEN** The record is parsed
- **THEN** The service reports a parsing error

### Requirement: ParseLocalizedRecords

The system SHALL the service shall parse localized data rows while retaining their keys and all value columns.

- **Implementation**: `localized_records::source/exvius/decode_client_data.py`

#### Scenario: LocalizedRowsWithMultipleValues
- **GIVEN** Valid localized text containing keys and multiple value columns
- **WHEN** The localized records are parsed
- **THEN** Each key and every value column are preserved

#### Scenario: MalformedLocalizedData
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Localized data that cannot be decoded or parsed into rows
- **WHEN** The localized records are parsed
- **THEN** The service reports a parsing error

> `source/exvius/event_select.py`

### Requirement: SaveEventSelection

The system SHALL the service shall save the requested event area selection and permanence policy.

#### Scenario: SaveSelectedAreas
- **GIVEN** One or more event areas are provided
- **WHEN** The event selection is saved
- **THEN** The selected areas are persisted with permanent set according to the historical-window option

#### Scenario: DisableAddedEvents
- **GIVEN** The disable-added-events option is selected
- **WHEN** The event selection is saved
- **THEN** An empty area selection is persisted

> `source/exvius/fill_content_from_memorial.py`

### Requirement: RepairStagedPackageIndexes

The system SHALL the service shall repair package indexes in staged content and refresh manifest provenance for packages changed by the repair.

- **Implementation**: `repair_staged_indexes::source/exvius/fill_content_from_memorial.py`

#### Scenario: StagedPackageIndexIsRepaired
- **GIVEN** The manifest records a staged package whose index requires Global-compatible flags
- **WHEN** staged indexes are repaired
- **THEN** the package header is updated and its file offsets are preserved

#### Scenario: ManifestProvenanceFollowsRepairedContent
- **GIVEN** A staged source package changes during index repair
- **WHEN** the repair completes
- **THEN** the manifest records refreshed source metadata for derived packages

### Requirement: ApplyReviewedEnglishDialogue

The system SHALL the service shall apply reviewed english translations only when the preserved source text matches the approved translation entry.

- **Implementation**: `apply_english_dialogue::source/exvius/fill_content_from_memorial.py`

#### Scenario: ExactApprovedSourceIsTranslated
- **GIVEN** An archive text member contains a source string matching an approved translation
- **WHEN** reviewed English dialogue is applied
- **THEN** the matching dialogue is replaced with its reviewed English translation

#### Scenario: UnmatchedSourceIsPreserved
- **GIVEN** An archive text member does not exactly match an approved source string
- **WHEN** reviewed English dialogue is applied
- **THEN** the source text remains unchanged

### Requirement: RepairDialogueCommands

The system SHALL the service shall normalize supported numeric dialogue commands when a closing bracket is missing before the next command.

- **Implementation**: `normalize_dialogue_commands::source/exvius/fill_content_from_memorial.py`

#### Scenario: MissingCommandBracketIsRepaired
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A supported numeric command is immediately followed by another game command without a closing
bracket
- **WHEN** dialogue commands are normalized
- **THEN** the missing closing bracket is inserted

#### Scenario: WellFormedCommandIsUnchanged
- **GIVEN** Dialogue commands already have closing brackets
- **WHEN** dialogue commands are normalized
- **THEN** the dialogue text remains unchanged

### Requirement: PreserveTownAtlasOverrides

The system SHALL the service shall preserve complete original town atlases for configured native-reader fallback cases.

- **Implementation**: `town_atlas_overrides::source/exvius/fill_content_from_memorial.py`

#### Scenario: ConfiguredAtlasFallbackIsStaged
- **GIVEN** A configured town atlas exists in Memorial content and the target override is absent
- **WHEN** town atlas overrides are prepared
- **THEN** the complete original atlas is written as an override

#### Scenario: ExistingAtlasOverrideIsPreserved
- **GIVEN** The target atlas override already exists
- **WHEN** town atlas overrides are prepared
- **THEN** the existing override is not overwritten


## Sub-components

> `Memorial Content Staging Service` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: LocalizedRows

> Implements: `localized_rows`

Read localized table rows while preserving empty cells.

#### Requirement: PreserveLocalizedCells

The system SHALL the function shall decode the table and split each row into cells without discarding empty cells.

#### Scenario: EmptyCellIsPreserved
- **GIVEN** A decoded row contains adjacent delimiters
- **WHEN** localized_rows processes the row
- **THEN** The result SHALL include an empty cell at that position.

### Sub-component: EnglishSource

> Implements: `english_source`

Normalize and identify an English source string.

#### Requirement: ExtractEnglishSource

The system SHALL the function shall clean the supplied text and identify its english source content.

#### Scenario: SurroundingWhitespace
- **GIVEN** Text contains surrounding whitespace and a searchable English source
- **WHEN** english_source processes it
- **THEN** The result SHALL identify the source without surrounding whitespace.

### Sub-component: ValueError

> Implements: `ValueError`

Represent an invalid value encountered during content processing.

#### Requirement: ReportInvalidValue

The system SHALL the operation shall expose an error when a supplied value violates the required content contract.

#### Scenario: InvalidValueEncountered
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A content value is invalid
- **WHEN** the operation reports the condition
- **THEN** A ValueError SHALL be raised.

### Sub-component: NormalizeDialogueCommands

> Implements: `normalize_dialogue_commands`

Repair a missing closing bracket before a following numeric game command.

#### Requirement: RepairCommandBoundary

The system SHALL the function shall repair a missing closing bracket before another numeric game command.

#### Scenario: AdjacentNumericCommand
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Dialogue contains a command missing its closing bracket immediately before another numeric command
- **WHEN** normalize_dialogue_commands processes it
- **THEN** The missing closing bracket SHALL be restored.

### Sub-component: JsonLoads

> Implements: `json.loads`

Parse serialized JSON content.

#### Requirement: ParseJson

The system SHALL the operation shall parse valid json input into its corresponding value.

#### Scenario: ValidJson
- **GIVEN** The input is valid JSON
- **WHEN** json.loads processes it
- **THEN** The corresponding value SHALL be returned.

### Sub-component: PathReadText

> Implements: `path.read_text`

Read text content from a path.

#### Requirement: ReadPathText

The system SHALL the operation shall return the text content available at the path.

#### Scenario: ExistingTextFile
- **GIVEN** The path identifies a readable text file
- **WHEN** path.read_text is called
- **THEN** The file text SHALL be returned.

### Sub-component: PathIsFile

> Implements: `path.is_file`

Determine whether a path identifies a file.

#### Requirement: CheckPathFile

The system SHALL the operation shall indicate whether the path identifies a file.

#### Scenario: PathIsDirectory
- **GIVEN** The path identifies a directory
- **WHEN** path.is_file is called
- **THEN** The result SHALL indicate that it is not a file.

### Sub-component: MembersItems

> Implements: `members.items`

Provide member name and value pairs.

#### Requirement: IterateMembers

The system SHALL the operation shall provide the entries contained in members as name and value pairs.

#### Scenario: MembersHaveEntries
- **GIVEN** Members contains named entries
- **WHEN** members.items is evaluated
- **THEN** Each entry SHALL be available as a name and value pair.

### Sub-component: NameEndswith

> Implements: `name.endswith`

Check whether a name has a specified suffix.

#### Requirement: CheckNameSuffix

The system SHALL the operation shall report whether name ends with the supplied suffix.

#### Scenario: MatchingSuffix
- **GIVEN** Name ends with the supplied suffix
- **WHEN** name.endswith is called
- **THEN** The result SHALL indicate a match.

### Sub-component: ArchiveFileBytes

Retrieve a named file's bytes from an archive.

#### Requirement: ReadArchivedFile

The system SHALL the operation shall return the bytes for the named archive member.

#### Scenario: MemberExists
- **GIVEN** The archive contains the named member
- **WHEN** archive.file_bytes is called
- **THEN** The member bytes SHALL be returned.

### Sub-component: ValuesItems

> Implements: `values.items`

Provide value mapping entries.

#### Requirement: IterateValues

The system SHALL the operation shall provide the entries contained in values as key and value pairs.

- **WHEN** values.items is evaluated
- **THEN** Each entry SHALL be available as a key and value pair.

Create a SHA-256 digest for supplied content.

- **WHEN** hashlib.sha256 processes it

- **THEN** The member SHALL contain the supplied bytes.

- **WHEN** global_cpk_header processes it

### Sub-component: ChangesGet

#### Scenario: FileAdded
- **GIVEN** A named file and its bytes are supplied
- **WHEN** compatible.add_bytes is called
- **THEN** The compatibility archive SHALL contain that file.

> Implements: `archive.save_bytes`

Serialize an archive as bytes.

The system SHALL the operation shall return a byte representation of the archive.

#### Scenario: ArchiveReady
- **GIVEN** The archive contains its final content
- **WHEN** archive.save_bytes is called
- **THEN** Serialized archive bytes SHALL be returned.

- **GIVEN** A valid package contains eligible localized content and required attachments
- **WHEN** localized_package processes it
- **THEN** A client-compatible package containing the applicable repairs and translations SHALL be produced.

Return the absolute resolved output path.

- **WHEN** out.resolve is called
- **THEN** An absolute resolved path SHALL be returned.

Split a directory string into path components.

Check whether a directory string begins with a prefix.

- **WHEN** directory.startswith is called
- **THEN** The result SHALL indicate a match.

Remove trailing characters from a string.

- **GIVEN** P has trailing whitespace
- **WHEN** p.rstrip is called
- **THEN** The trailing whitespace SHALL be removed.

> `source/exvius/finalize_distribution.py`

### Requirement: Finalize

The system SHALL the distribution process shall validate the supplied version information and package contents, calculate package metadata, and write the finalized distribution artifacts.

- **Implementation**: `finalize::source/exvius/finalize_distribution.py`

#### Scenario: FinalizeAValidDistribution
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A valid APK and distribution manifest exist for the supplied version
- **WHEN** finalize is called with the version, client version, and version code
- **THEN** The package contents are processed, metadata is updated, and the finalized APK and manifest are
written

#### Scenario: RejectInvalidDistributionInput
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The supplied package or manifest is invalid or inconsistent
- **WHEN** finalize is called
- **THEN** The operation raises a ValueError and does not report a successful finalized distribution

> `source/exvius/friends_menu.js`

### Requirement: InstallFriendsMenu

The system SHALL the friends screen shall display a local profile caption when no valid server-issued friend code is available, while preserving searched npc ids and native search validation.

- **Implementation**: `installFriendsMenu::source/exvius/friends_menu.js`

#### Scenario: ShowLocalProfileWithoutAServerFriendCode
- **GIVEN** The own-profile caption is being generated and its value is not a nine-digit friend code
- **WHEN** The Friends menu hooks are installed and the caption is processed
- **THEN** The caption is replaced with "Local profile"

#### Scenario: PreserveSearchedNPCIDs
- **GIVEN** A friend ID is being generated for a searched NPC rather than the own-profile caption
- **WHEN** The Friends menu hooks process the ID
- **THEN** The ID is left unchanged

#### Scenario: DisableServerDependentFriendsControls
- **GIVEN** The Friends screen lays out its buttons
- **WHEN** The share, Facebook, or reinforcement-setting button is created
- **THEN** The button is disabled for touch and its base and caption sprites are dimmed

#### Scenario: RetainLocalCompanionGiftControls
- **GIVEN** The Friends screen is laid out
- **WHEN** The menu hooks run
- **THEN** Controls outside the configured blocked-button set remain available

> `source/exvius/home_carousel.js`

### Requirement: DetermineHomeMenuState

The system SHALL the service shall derive the home menu state from the esper count and expedition flag.

- **Implementation**: `homeMenuState::source/exvius/home_carousel.js`

#### Scenario: MenuStateReflectsPlayerData
- **GIVEN** An esper count and expedition flag are provided
- **WHEN** The home menu state is determined
- **THEN** The returned state reflects those inputs

> `source/exvius/home_carousel.js`

### Requirement: CalculateCarouselItems

The system SHALL the service shall calculate the visible carousel item positions from the current position, item count, and width.

- **Implementation**: `carouselItems::source/exvius/home_carousel.js`

#### Scenario: ItemsLaidOutForCarousel
- **GIVEN** A carousel position, item count, and width
- **WHEN** Carousel items are calculated
- **THEN** The result provides item positions appropriate to the supplied dimensions

### Requirement: ResolveCarouselRelease

The system SHALL the service shall determine the carousel position after a drag is released.

- **Implementation**: `carouselRelease::source/exvius/home_carousel.js`

#### Scenario: ReleaseResolvesDragMovement
- **GIVEN** A starting position, drag distance, and carousel width
- **WHEN** The release is processed
- **THEN** The returned position reflects the drag

### Requirement: IdentifyCarouselHit

The system SHALL the service shall identify which carousel card, if any, corresponds to a point.

- **Implementation**: `carouselHit::source/exvius/home_carousel.js`

#### Scenario: PointHitsACard
- **GIVEN** A set of cards and a point within one card
- **WHEN** The hit is resolved
- **THEN** The matching card is returned

#### Scenario: PointMissesAllCards
- **GIVEN** A set of cards and a point outside them
- **WHEN** The hit is resolved
- **THEN** No card is returned

> `source/exvius/home_carousel.js`

### Requirement: InstallHomeCarousel

The system SHALL the service shall install the home carousel integration into the game module and retain runtime hooks needed to manage it.

- **Implementation**: `installHomeCarousel::source/exvius/home_carousel.js`

#### Scenario: CarouselIntegrationInstalled
- **GIVEN** A game module and runtime support values
- **WHEN** The integration is installed
- **THEN** The carousel hooks are attached and retained for runtime use

#### Scenario: InstallationEncountersARuntimeError
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A runtime operation fails during installation
- **WHEN** The integration handles the error
- **THEN** The error is reported through the supplied note function


## Sub-components

> `Home Carousel Integration` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: ModuleExportLookup

> Implements: `module.getExportByName`

Resolves a named export from a module.

#### Requirement: ResolveExport

The system SHALL the block must resolve the requested export by name from the specified module.

#### Scenario: ExportExists
- **GIVEN** The module exposes the requested export.
- **WHEN** The block looks up the export by name.
- **THEN** The matching export is returned.

### Sub-component: IconAddressRead

> Implements: `getIconAddress.readU32`

Reads a 32-bit value at the icon address.

#### Requirement: ReadIconAddress

The system SHALL the block must read and return the 32-bit value at the icon address.

#### Scenario: AddressIsReadable
- **GIVEN** The icon address points to a readable value.
- **WHEN** The block reads the address.
- **THEN** The value is returned as a 32-bit integer.

### Sub-component: IconAddressOffset

> Implements: `getIconAddress.add`

Computes an address offset from the icon address.

#### Requirement: OffsetIconAddress

The system SHALL the block must return the address reached by adding the requested offset to the icon address.

#### Scenario: OffsetProvided
- **GIVEN** An icon address and offset are available.
- **WHEN** The block adds the offset.
- **THEN** The resulting address is returned.

### Sub-component: NativeExportLookup

> Implements: `native`

Obtains native functionality through a module export lookup.

#### Requirement: ResolveNative

The system SHALL the block must obtain the requested native export using module.getexportbyname.

#### Scenario: NativeExportExists
- **GIVEN** The requested native export is available.
- **WHEN** The block resolves the native function.
- **THEN** The native export is returned.

### Sub-component: RetainedReference

> Implements: `retained.push`

Retains a reference for later use.

#### Requirement: RetainReference

The system SHALL the block must add the supplied reference to the retained collection.

#### Scenario: ReferenceProvided
- **GIVEN** A reference is supplied for retention.
- **WHEN** The block retains it.
- **THEN** The reference is present in the retained collection.

### Sub-component: InterceptorAttach

> Implements: `Interceptor.attach`

Attaches interception behavior to a target address.

#### Requirement: AttachInterceptor

The system SHALL the block must attach the supplied interception callbacks to the target.

#### Scenario: TargetAvailable
- **GIVEN** A target address and callbacks are provided.
- **WHEN** The block attaches the interceptor.
- **THEN** An attachment is established for the target.

### Sub-component: CurrentThreadId

> Implements: `Process.getCurrentThreadId`

Obtains the identifier of the current thread.

#### Requirement: GetThreadId

The system SHALL the block must return the current thread identifier.

#### Scenario: ThreadIsRunning
- **GIVEN** Execution is running on a thread.
- **WHEN** The block requests the current thread identifier.
- **THEN** The current thread identifier is returned.

### Sub-component: Mission

> Implements: `mission`

Processes the mission-related block identified as mission.

#### Requirement: ProcessMission

The system SHALL the block should perform the mission-related work required by its caller.

#### Scenario: MissionProcessed
- **GIVEN** Mission-related input is available.
- **WHEN** The block processes the mission.
- **THEN** A mission-related result is produced.

### Sub-component: Missions

> Implements: `missions`

Loads and evaluates dungeon mission data.

#### Requirement: EvaluateMissions

The system SHALL the block must process mission data for the supplied dungeon, including reward resolution.

#### Scenario: DungeonMissionsLoaded
- **GIVEN** A dungeon identifier and mission data are available.
- **WHEN** The block evaluates missions for the dungeon.
- **THEN** Mission rewards are resolved and only newly earned level rewards are paid.

### Sub-component: CurrentTime

> Implements: `Date.now`

Obtains the current time value.

#### Requirement: GetCurrentTime

The system SHALL the block must return the current time value.

#### Scenario: TimeRequested
- **GIVEN** The block is invoked.
- **WHEN** It obtains the current time.
- **THEN** A current time value is returned.

### Sub-component: OriginalMission

> Implements: `originalMission`

Provides access to the original mission behavior.

#### Requirement: RunOriginalMission

The system SHALL the block must invoke or preserve the original mission behavior when required.

#### Scenario: OriginalBehaviorRequired
- **GIVEN** A mission operation requires original behavior.
- **WHEN** The block delegates to the original mission.
- **THEN** The original mission result is produced.

### Sub-component: Note

> Implements: `note`

Records and sends a diagnostic note.

#### Requirement: RecordNote

The system SHALL the block must format and deliver the supplied note through the configured messaging and logging channels.

#### Scenario: NoteProvided
- **GIVEN** A note is supplied.
- **WHEN** The block records it.
- **THEN** The note is sent and logged.

### Sub-component: FastReplace

> Implements: `Interceptor.replaceFast`

Replaces a target function using fast interception.

#### Requirement: ReplaceTarget

The system SHALL the block must replace the target function with the supplied replacement and preserve the original callable when available.

#### Scenario: ReplacementProvided
- **GIVEN** A target and replacement function are available.
- **WHEN** The block replaces the target.
- **THEN** The target uses the replacement and the original callable is returned.

### Sub-component: WithString

> Implements: `withString`

Runs an action with a constructed native string and ensures its lifecycle is handled.

#### Requirement: RunWithNativeString

The system SHALL the block must construct a native string from the input, provide it to the action, and destroy it after use.

#### Scenario: ActionCompletes
- **GIVEN** A string value and action are supplied.
- **WHEN** The block runs the action with the constructed string.
- **THEN** The action result is returned and the native string is destroyed.

### Sub-component: FindFrame

> Implements: `findFrame`

Finds a sprite frame by its identifier.

#### Requirement: LookupFrame

The system SHALL the block must return the frame matching the requested identifier when one exists.

#### Scenario: FrameExists
- **GIVEN** The requested frame is available.
- **WHEN** The block searches for it.
- **THEN** The matching frame is returned.

### Sub-component: FrameCache

> Implements: `frameCache`

Provides access to the sprite frame cache.

#### Requirement: GetFrameCache

The system SHALL the block must return the frame cache used for sprite frame lookups.

#### Scenario: CacheRequested
- **GIVEN** A frame lookup needs the cache.
- **WHEN** The block accesses the frame cache.
- **THEN** The frame cache is returned.

### Sub-component: FrameNullCheck

> Implements: `frame.isNull`

Checks whether a sprite frame reference is null.

#### Requirement: CheckFrameNull

The system SHALL the block must report whether the frame reference is null.

#### Scenario: FrameIsNull
- **GIVEN** The frame reference is null.
- **WHEN** The block checks it.
- **THEN** The result indicates that the frame is null.

### Sub-component: MakeGameSprite

> Implements: `makeGameSprite`

Creates a sprite for game content.

#### Requirement: CreateGameSprite

The system SHALL the block must create and return a game sprite for the supplied content.

- **GIVEN** Sprite content is available.
- **WHEN** The block creates a game sprite.
- **THEN** A game sprite is returned.

### Sub-component: MakeSprite

> Implements: `makeSprite`

Creates a sprite from the supplied resource.

#### Requirement: CreateSprite

The system SHALL the block must create and return a sprite for the supplied resource.

#### Scenario: ResourceProvided
- **GIVEN** A sprite resource is available.
- **WHEN** The block creates a sprite.
- **THEN** A sprite is returned.

### Sub-component: NodeNullCheck

> Implements: `node.isNull`

Checks whether a node reference is null.

#### Requirement: CheckNodeNull

The system SHALL the block must report whether the node reference is null.

#### Scenario: NodeIsNull
- **GIVEN** The node reference is null.
- **WHEN** The block checks it.
- **THEN** The result indicates that the node is null.

- **WHEN** The block retains it.
- **THEN** The node remains available for subsequent use.

### Sub-component: AddChild

Adds a child node to a parent node.

#### Requirement: AttachChild

The system SHALL the block must add the specified child to the parent node.

#### Scenario: ParentAndChildExist
- **GIVEN** A parent and child node are available.
- **WHEN** The block adds the child.
- **THEN** The child is attached to the parent.

### Sub-component: Layers

> Implements: `layers`

Provides the relevant layer nodes for carousel content.

#### Scenario: LayersRequested

- **WHEN** The block retrieves layers.
- **THEN** The relevant layers are returned.

### Sub-component: Snapshot

> Implements: `snapshot`

Captures a node's relevant layout and transform properties.

#### Requirement: CaptureSnapshot

#### Scenario: NodePropertiesAvailable
- **GIVEN** The node exposes its layout and transform properties.
- **WHEN** The block captures a snapshot.
- **THEN** The snapshot contains the requested property values.

### Sub-component: IsNull

> Implements: `isNull`

Checks whether the referenced value is null.

#### Requirement: CheckNull

The system SHALL the block must report whether the value is null.

#### Scenario: ValueIsNull
- **GIVEN** The value is null.
- **WHEN** The block checks it.
- **THEN** The result indicates that the value is null.

### Sub-component: SsResource

> Implements: `ssResource`

Obtains a resource for a sprite sheet.

#### Requirement: GetSpriteSheetResource

The system SHALL the block must return the resource associated with the requested sprite sheet.

#### Scenario: ResourceExists
- **GIVEN** A sprite sheet resource is registered.
- **WHEN** The block requests it.
- **THEN** The resource is returned.

### Sub-component: SsList

> Implements: `ssList`

Lists sprite sheet resources or entries.

#### Requirement: ListSpriteSheets

The system SHALL the block must return the relevant sprite sheet list.

#### Scenario: ListRequested
- **GIVEN** Sprite sheet entries are available.

> `source/exvius/import_client_cache.py`

### Requirement: ImportCache

The system SHALL the service shall import supported client cache files from an archive into the data directory while validating paths and avoiding replacement of existing files.

- **Implementation**: `import_cache::source/exvius/import_client_cache.py`

#### Scenario: ImportSupportedCacheContent
- **GIVEN** An archive contains files under the configured client data prefix and supported content categories
- **WHEN** import_cache is called
- **THEN** Supported files are copied into the corresponding data locations

#### Scenario: RejectUnsafeArchivePaths
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** An archive entry resolves to an absolute or invalid destination path
- **WHEN** import_cache processes the entry
- **THEN** The unsafe entry is rejected

#### Scenario: PreserveExistingFiles
- **GIVEN** A destination file already exists
- **WHEN** import_cache processes the corresponding archive entry
- **THEN** The existing file is not replaced

### Requirement: ExtractArt

The system SHALL the service shall extract art assets from client data into the server’s extracted-art layout.

- **Implementation**: `extract_art::source/exvius/import_client_cache.py`

#### Scenario: ExtractArtAssets
- **GIVEN** Client data contains a supported art archive
- **WHEN** extract_art is called
- **THEN** Art files are extracted into the server’s extracted-art layout

#### Scenario: HandleMissingArtInput
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The expected art input is missing
- **WHEN** extract_art is called
- **THEN** The operation reports that the required input is unavailable


## Sub-components

> `ClientCacheImporter` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: PosixPathConstruction

> Implements: `PurePosixPath`

Interprets archive member names as POSIX paths for safe path handling.

#### Requirement: ParseArchivePath

The system SHALL the importer shall interpret each archive member name as a posix path before evaluating its location.

#### Scenario: ParseNestedMember
- **GIVEN** an archive member named assets/ui/icon.png
- **WHEN** the member name is interpreted
- **THEN** the resulting path SHALL preserve its nested components

### Sub-component: ValueErrorHandling

> Implements: `ValueError`

Handles invalid values encountered while importing client data.

#### Requirement: RejectInvalidValue

The system SHALL the importer shall treat a valueerror raised during import processing as an invalid input condition.

#### Scenario: InvalidValue
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** an import operation raises ValueError
- **WHEN** the error is handled
- **THEN** the importer SHALL report or propagate the invalid-value failure

### Sub-component: Crc32Checksum

> Implements: `crc32`

Computes a CRC32 checksum for a file.

#### Requirement: ComputeFileChecksum

The system SHALL the checksum operation shall return the crc32 value for the contents of the specified file.

#### Scenario: ChecksumKnownContents
- **GIVEN** a file containing known bytes
- **WHEN** its checksum is computed
- **THEN** the returned checksum SHALL match the CRC32 of those bytes

### Sub-component: FileExistsErrorHandling

> Implements: `FileExistsError`

Handles attempts to create or place data where a file already exists.

#### Requirement: HandleExistingFile

The system SHALL the importer shall handle a fileexistserror raised during import processing.

#### Scenario: DestinationAlreadyExists
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** an import operation encounters an existing file
- **WHEN** it raises FileExistsError
- **THEN** the importer SHALL report or propagate the conflict

### Sub-component: ZipArchiveOpen

> Implements: `zipfile.ZipFile`

Opens a ZIP archive as the source of client data.

#### Requirement: OpenZipArchive

The system SHALL the importer shall open the supplied archive for reading its members.

#### Scenario: OpenReadableArchive
- **GIVEN** a valid ZIP archive path
- **WHEN** the importer opens the archive
- **THEN** the archive SHALL be available for member inspection

### Sub-component: ListZipEntries

> Implements: `source.infolist`

Retrieves the entries contained in the source ZIP archive.

#### Requirement: EnumerateArchiveEntries

The system SHALL the importer shall obtain the archive's entry list for subsequent selection and extraction.

#### Scenario: ListArchiveMembers
- **GIVEN** an opened archive containing files and directories
- **WHEN** its entries are enumerated
- **THEN** the returned list SHALL include the archive entries

### Sub-component: IdentifyDirectoryEntry

> Implements: `entry.is_dir`

Determines whether an archive entry represents a directory.

#### Requirement: ClassifyArchiveEntry

The system SHALL the importer shall identify directory entries so they can be handled according to their type.

#### Scenario: RecognizeDirectoryEntry
- **GIVEN** an archive entry representing a directory
- **WHEN** its type is checked
- **THEN** the entry SHALL be classified as a directory

### Sub-component: ValidateRelativePath

> Implements: `relative.is_absolute`

Checks whether a candidate relative path is absolute.

#### Requirement: RejectAbsoluteRelativePath

The system SHALL the importer shall detect an absolute path where a relative archive path is required.

#### Scenario: DetectAbsolutePath
- **GIVEN** a candidate path beginning at the filesystem root
- **WHEN** its absoluteness is checked
- **THEN** it SHALL be identified as absolute

### Sub-component: JoinDestinationPath

> Implements: `destination.joinpath`

Builds a destination path from the destination root and a relative path.

#### Requirement: ResolveDestinationMember

The system SHALL the importer shall construct the target location for a member beneath the destination root.

#### Scenario: JoinNestedDestination
- **GIVEN** a destination root and a nested relative member path
- **WHEN** the target path is constructed
- **THEN** the target SHALL represent that member beneath the destination root

### Sub-component: CheckTargetExists

> Implements: `target.exists`

Checks whether the intended destination target already exists.

#### Requirement: DetectExistingTarget

The system SHALL the importer shall determine whether a destination target is already present before handling it.

#### Scenario: ExistingTarget
- **GIVEN** a target path that names an existing filesystem object
- **WHEN** its existence is checked
- **THEN** the result SHALL indicate that the target exists

### Sub-component: InspectTargetMetadata

> Implements: `target.stat`

Retrieves filesystem metadata for a destination target.

#### Requirement: ReadTargetMetadata

The system SHALL the importer shall retrieve target metadata when evaluating an existing destination.

#### Scenario: InspectExistingTarget
- **GIVEN** an existing destination target
- **WHEN** its metadata is requested
- **THEN** the importer SHALL receive the target's filesystem metadata

### Sub-component: CreatePendingPath

> Implements: `target.with_name`

Creates a sibling path used as a pending destination for an import.

#### Requirement: NamePendingTarget

The system SHALL the importer shall derive a pending path alongside the target using a distinct name.

#### Scenario: CreateSiblingPendingPath
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** a target path and a pending filename
- **WHEN** the pending path is derived
- **THEN** it SHALL share the target's parent directory and use the pending filename

### Sub-component: CopyFileContent

> Implements: `shutil.copyfileobj`

Copies file data from a source stream to a destination stream.

#### Requirement: CopyStreamContents

The system SHALL the importer shall copy the source stream's contents to the destination stream.

#### Scenario: CopyNonemptyStream
- **GIVEN** a readable source stream containing bytes and a writable destination stream
- **WHEN** the contents are copied
- **THEN** the destination SHALL contain the source bytes in order

### Sub-component: ReplaceTarget

> Implements: `pending.replace`

Promotes the pending file to the intended destination path.

#### Requirement: CommitPendingFile

The system SHALL the importer shall replace the target path with the completed pending file.

#### Scenario: PromoteCompletedFile
- **GIVEN** a completed pending file and an intended target
- **WHEN** the pending file replaces the target
- **THEN** the target SHALL contain the pending file's contents

### Sub-component: FileNotFoundErrorHandling

> Implements: `FileNotFoundError`

Handles missing files encountered during import processing.

#### Requirement: HandleMissingFile

The system SHALL the importer shall handle a filenotfounderror raised while locating or importing data.

#### Scenario: SourceFileMissing
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** an expected file is absent
- **WHEN** an import operation raises FileNotFoundError
- **THEN** the importer SHALL report or propagate the missing-file failure

### Sub-component: GlobFiles

> Implements: `files.glob`

Selects filesystem paths matching a pattern under a files location.

#### Requirement: FindMatchingFiles

The system SHALL the importer shall enumerate paths matching the requested pattern.

#### Scenario: MatchExistingFiles
- **GIVEN** a files location containing paths that match a pattern
- **WHEN** the pattern is enumerated
- **THEN** the matching paths SHALL be returned

### Sub-component: RelativePathFromRoot

> Implements: `path.relative_to`

Derives a path relative to a designated root.

#### Requirement: DeriveRelativePath

The system SHALL the importer shall represent a path relative to the designated root when it is beneath that root.

#### Scenario: PathWithinRoot
- **GIVEN** a path beneath the designated root
- **WHEN** its relative path is derived
- **THEN** the result SHALL contain the path components below the root

### Sub-component: LoadCpkArchive

Loads a CPK archive for inspection and extraction.

#### Requirement: LoadCpk

The system SHALL the importer shall load the supplied cpk archive for access to its contents.

- **WHEN** the archive is loaded
- **THEN** its contents SHALL be available for inspection

### Sub-component: ReadCpkFileInfos

> Implements: `archive.file_infos`

Retrieves file metadata from a loaded CPK archive.

#### Requirement: EnumerateCpkFiles

The system SHALL the importer shall obtain file information for the entries in the cpk archive.

#### Scenario: ListCpkFiles
- **GIVEN** a loaded CPK archive containing file entries
- **WHEN** its file information is retrieved
- **THEN** the returned information SHALL describe the archive files

### Sub-component: ValidateCpkName

> Implements: `name.is_absolute`

Checks whether a CPK entry name is an absolute path.

#### Requirement: CheckCpkEntryPath

The system SHALL the importer shall detect absolute entry names when evaluating cpk output paths.

#### Scenario: AbsoluteCpkEntry
- **GIVEN** a CPK entry name beginning at the filesystem root
- **WHEN** its absoluteness is checked
- **THEN** it SHALL be classified as absolute

### Sub-component: JoinCpkOutputPath

> Implements: `output.joinpath`

Builds an output path for a CPK entry.

#### Requirement: ConstructCpkOutputPath

The system SHALL the importer shall construct an output location from the extraction root and entry name.

#### Scenario: JoinCpkEntryPath
- **GIVEN** an output root and a relative CPK entry name
- **WHEN** the entry output path is constructed
- **THEN** the result SHALL identify the entry beneath the output root

### Sub-component: ResolveOutputPath

> Implements: `output.resolve`

Resolves the output path to its canonical filesystem location.

#### Requirement: CanonicalizeOutputRoot

The system SHALL the importer shall resolve the output path before using it as an extraction location.

#### Scenario: ResolveRelativeOutput
- **GIVEN** a relative output path
- **WHEN** the path is resolved
- **THEN** the result SHALL identify its canonical filesystem location

### Sub-component: ExtractCpkEntry

> Implements: `archive.extract`

Extracts a selected entry from the CPK archive to its output location.

#### Requirement: ExtractSelectedEntry

The system SHALL the importer shall extract the requested archive entry to the specified output location.

#### Scenario: ExtractSelectedFile
- **GIVEN** a selected file entry in a loaded CPK archive and a valid output location
- **WHEN** the entry is extracted
- **THEN** the file SHALL be written to the output location

### Sub-component: ScanOutputTree

> Implements: `output.rglob`

Recursively finds paths in the output tree matching a pattern.

#### Requirement: EnumerateOutputTree

The system SHALL the importer shall recursively enumerate output paths matching the requested pattern.

#### Scenario: FindNestedOutput
- **GIVEN** an output tree containing nested paths matching a pattern
- **WHEN** the tree is recursively scanned
- **THEN** all matching paths SHALL be returned

### Sub-component: IdentifyRegularFile

> Implements: `p.is_file`

Determines whether an output path is a regular file.

#### Requirement: FilterRegularFiles

The system SHALL the importer shall identify regular files when processing paths found in the output tree.

#### Scenario: RecognizeRegularFile
- **GIVEN** an output path that names a regular file
- **WHEN** its type is checked
- **THEN** it SHALL be classified as a file

### Sub-component: InspectOutputFileMetadata

> Implements: `p.stat`

Retrieves filesystem metadata for an output file.

#### Requirement: ReadOutputFileMetadata

The system SHALL the importer shall retrieve metadata for an output file when processing extracted content.

#### Scenario: InspectExtractedFile
- **GIVEN** an extracted regular file
- **WHEN** its metadata is requested
- **THEN** the importer SHALL receive the file's filesystem metadata

> `source/exvius/import_field_treasures.py`

### Requirement: ImportFieldTreasuresAndMissionLinks

The system SHALL the importer shall assemble field treasure records, validate their group and asset bindings, apply overrides, and write treasure data, field data, and mission-to-scenario links.

#### Scenario: ValidSources
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Source assets and database contain valid treasure, group, and mission data
- **WHEN** main is called
- **THEN** The importer writes treasure records and associated field and mission link outputs.

#### Scenario: OverridesProvided
- **GIVEN** An override file contains changes for imported treasures
- **WHEN** main is called
- **THEN** The resulting treasure records reflect the applicable overrides.

#### Scenario: InvalidTreasureBindings
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Source data contains bindings that do not match valid groups or assets
- **WHEN** main is called
- **THEN** Invalid bindings are excluded from the generated treasure data.

> `source/exvius/map_interaction_audit.js`

### Requirement: InstallMapInteractionAudit

The system SHALL the module shall install diagnostic hooks that record map touch boundaries, touch targets and geometry, and incoming field treasure rows.

- **Implementation**: `installMapInteractionAudit::source/exvius/map_interaction_audit.js`

#### Scenario: AuditHooksInstalled
- **GIVEN** The target module exports the map and treasure functions
- **WHEN** installMapInteractionAudit is called
- **THEN** Hooks are attached and their handles are retained, and a ready diagnostic is logged

#### Scenario: MapTouchIsObserved
- **GIVEN** A map touch begins and a map event processes the touch
- **WHEN** The hooked touch functions run
- **THEN** Touch boundaries and the event target details are logged, including readable touch geometry when
available

#### Scenario: TreasureRowsAreReceived
- **GIVEN** A field treasure row is read
- **WHEN** The hooked row reader is entered
- **THEN** The cumulative number of loaded treasure rows is logged

#### Scenario: UnreadableDiagnosticData
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A touch target or geometry cannot be read
- **WHEN** The diagnostic hook encounters the unreadable data
- **THEN** It records the applicable audit error or unreadable marker without stopping the audit

> `source/exvius/menu_assets.js`

### Requirement: InstallMenuAssetHooks

The system SHALL the module shall install the hooks needed to intercept menu asset loading and adjust menu presentation.

- **Implementation**: `installMenuAssets::source/exvius/menu_assets.js`

#### Scenario: InstallMenuAssetHooks
- **GIVEN** A game module, logging callback, retention collection, bridge, and optional mode chooser name are
available
- **WHEN** installMenuAssets is called
- **THEN** The relevant menu asset and presentation hooks are attached and their references are retained

### Requirement: LoadMenuTextures

The system SHALL the module shall supply available local or nested image assets when the game requests a menu texture, and fall back to the original reader when a replacement is unavailable.

- **Implementation**: `installMenuAssets::source/exvius/menu_assets.js`

#### Scenario: ReplacementTextureIsAvailable
- **GIVEN** A requested menu texture has a mapped local or nested image asset
- **WHEN** The texture reader processes the request
- **THEN** The mapped asset is supplied to the game

#### Scenario: ReplacementTextureIsUnavailable
- **GIVEN** No usable replacement asset is available for the requested filename
- **WHEN** The texture reader processes the request
- **THEN** The original texture reader is used

### Requirement: AdaptMenuCaptions

The system SHALL the module shall adjust caption presentation for menu images, including positioning and sizing the caption strip and label.

- **Implementation**: `installMenuAssets::source/exvius/menu_assets.js`

#### Scenario: CaptionBelongsToAMenuImage
- **GIVEN** A caption batch is associated with a supported menu image
- **WHEN** The caption hook processes the batch
- **THEN** The caption strip and label are added or adjusted to fit the image layout

#### Scenario: ImageIsNotEligibleForCaptionAdaptation
- **GIVEN** The image is unsupported or marked as a fallback image
- **WHEN** The caption hook processes the image
- **THEN** The caption layout is left unchanged

### Requirement: AdaptRaidAndDungeonPresentation

The system SHALL the module shall adapt presentation for recognized raid pictures and dungeon scenes as they enter and leave the relevant ui flow.

- **Implementation**: `installMenuAssets::source/exvius/menu_assets.js`

#### Scenario: RecognizedRaidPictureEntersTheUI
- **GIVEN** The hooked UI flow identifies a raid picture
- **WHEN** The entry hook runs
- **THEN** The picture is tracked and the relevant asset handling is applied

#### Scenario: TrackedRaidPictureLeavesTheUI
- **GIVEN** A raid picture is being removed from the UI
- **WHEN** The leave hook runs
- **THEN** Its scale and position are adjusted and tracking is cleared


## Sub-components

> `MenuAssets` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: NativeLookup

> Implements: `module.getExportByName`

Resolves a named native export from the module.

#### Requirement: ResolveExport

The system SHALL the block shall resolve the requested native export by name.

#### Scenario: ExportFound
- **GIVEN** The module exports the requested name
- **WHEN** The block resolves the export
- **THEN** The export address is returned

### Sub-component: RetainedPush

> Implements: `retained.push`

Retains a value in the retained collection.

#### Requirement: RetainValue

The system SHALL the block shall append the supplied value to the retained collection.

#### Scenario: ValueRetained
- **GIVEN** A value and retained collection are available
- **WHEN** The value is appended
- **THEN** The collection contains the value

### Sub-component: InterceptorAttach

> Implements: `Interceptor.attach`

Attaches interception behavior to a target address.

#### Requirement: AttachInterceptor

The system SHALL the block shall attach the supplied interception callbacks to the target address.

#### Scenario: CallbacksAttached
- **GIVEN** A valid target address and callbacks are provided
- **WHEN** The interception is attached
- **THEN** The target is monitored by the callbacks

### Sub-component: ExportLookup

> Implements: `module.getExportByName`

Looks up a named module export.

#### Requirement: LookupExport

The system SHALL the block shall return the address of the requested module export.

#### Scenario: NamedExportResolved
- **GIVEN** The requested export exists
- **WHEN** The block looks it up
- **THEN** Its address is returned

### Sub-component: InfoNullCheck

> Implements: `info.isNull`

Determines whether the info pointer is null.

#### Requirement: CheckInfoNull

The system SHALL the block shall report whether the info pointer is null.

#### Scenario: NullInfo
- **GIVEN** The info pointer is null
- **WHEN** Its null status is checked
- **THEN** The result indicates null

### Sub-component: RankingGambits

> Implements: `rankingGambits`

Provides the ranking gambits used to select a menu asset.

#### Requirement: RankGambits

The system SHALL the block shall provide ranking gambits for asset selection.

#### Scenario: RankingAvailable
- **GIVEN** A menu asset context is available
- **WHEN** Ranking gambits are requested
- **THEN** The applicable ranking gambits are provided

### Sub-component: SelectedAdd

> Implements: `selected.add`

Adds an item to the selected collection.

#### Requirement: AddSelection

The system SHALL the block shall add the supplied item to the selected collection.

#### Scenario: ItemSelected
- **GIVEN** An item is available
- **WHEN** It is added to the selection
- **THEN** The selected collection contains it

### Sub-component: SelectedReadPointer

> Implements: `selected.readPointer`

Reads a pointer from the selected value.

#### Requirement: ReadSelectedPointer

The system SHALL the block shall read and return the pointer represented by the selected value.

#### Scenario: PointerRead
- **GIVEN** The selected value contains a readable pointer
- **WHEN** The pointer is read
- **THEN** The pointer is returned

### Sub-component: ItemCompare

> Implements: `item.compare`

Compares an item against a supplied value.

#### Requirement: CompareItem

The system SHALL the block shall compare the item with the supplied comparison value.

#### Scenario: ItemsCompared
- **GIVEN** An item and comparison value are available
- **WHEN** The comparison is performed
- **THEN** A comparison result is returned

### Sub-component: ItemAdd

> Implements: `item.add`

Advances the item pointer by an offset.

#### Requirement: AdvanceItem

The system SHALL the block shall produce the item address advanced by the supplied offset.

#### Scenario: AddressAdvanced
- **GIVEN** An item address and offset are supplied
- **WHEN** The offset is applied
- **THEN** The advanced address is returned

### Sub-component: ItemReadS32

> Implements: `item.readS32`

Reads a signed 32-bit value from an item address.

#### Requirement: ReadItemInteger

The system SHALL the block shall read and return a signed 32-bit value from the item address.

#### Scenario: IntegerRead
- **GIVEN** The item address references a readable signed 32-bit value
- **WHEN** The value is read
- **THEN** The signed value is returned

### Sub-component: OriginalGambit

> Implements: `originalGambit`

Provides the original gambit behavior for a menu asset operation.

#### Requirement: InvokeOriginalGambit

The system SHALL the block shall provide the original gambit behavior when required.

#### Scenario: OriginalBehaviorUsed
- **GIVEN** The original gambit behavior is required
- **WHEN** The block is invoked
- **THEN** The original gambit result is provided

### Sub-component: ReplaceFast

> Implements: `Interceptor.replaceFast`

Replaces a target function while preserving its original callable behavior.

#### Requirement: ReplaceTarget

The system SHALL the block shall replace the target function with the supplied replacement and expose the original callable function.

#### Scenario: TargetReplaced
- **GIVEN** A target and replacement function are available
- **WHEN** The replacement is installed
- **THEN** The target uses the replacement and the original callable is returned

### Sub-component: ObjectValues

> Implements: `Object.values`

Extracts enumerable values from an object.

#### Requirement: ExtractValues

The system SHALL the block shall return the object's enumerable property values.

#### Scenario: ValuesExtracted
- **GIVEN** An object has enumerable properties
- **WHEN** Its values are extracted
- **THEN** The values are returned as an array

### Sub-component: OwnedAdd

> Implements: `owned.add`

Marks a value as owned by adding it to the owned collection.

#### Requirement: MarkOwned

The system SHALL the block shall add the supplied value to the owned collection.

#### Scenario: OwnershipRecorded
- **GIVEN** A value is supplied
- **WHEN** It is marked owned
- **THEN** The owned collection contains the value

### Sub-component: ObjectEntries

Extracts enumerable key-value pairs from an object.

The system SHALL the block shall return the object's enumerable key-value pairs.

- **GIVEN** An object has enumerable properties
- **WHEN** Its entries are extracted
- **THEN** The key-value pairs are returned

Associates a key with a nested image value.

- **GIVEN** A key and image are available
- **WHEN** The association is stored
- **THEN** The map returns that image for the key

### Sub-component: ManagedAdd

> Implements: `managed.add`

Adds a value to the managed collection.

The system SHALL the block shall add the supplied value to the managed collection.

#### Scenario: ValueManaged

- **WHEN** It is marked managed
- **THEN** The managed collection contains it

Allocates a memory region of the requested size.

- **GIVEN** A valid allocation size is supplied
- **WHEN** Memory is allocated
- **THEN** A memory address is returned

### Sub-component: MiddleAdd

> Implements: `middle.add`

Advances the middle pointer by an offset.

#### Requirement: AdvanceMiddle

The system SHALL the block shall produce the middle address advanced by the supplied offset.

- **GIVEN** A middle address and offset are supplied
- **WHEN** The offset is applied
- **THEN** The advanced address is returned
