> `source/exvius/native_bridge.js`

### Requirement: CreateNativeBridge

The system SHALL the bridge shall expose a callable interface for invoking methods through the supplied native module and must manage jni exceptions, string values, and local references during calls.

- **Implementation**: `createNativeBridge::source/exvius/native_bridge.js`

#### Scenario: CreateBridgeFromNativeModule
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A native module with the required exports is available
- **WHEN** createNativeBridge is called
- **THEN** A bridge interface is returned that can invoke methods through the module

#### Scenario: HandleJNIExceptionDuringInvocation
- **GIVEN** A bridged method invocation raises a JNI exception
- **WHEN** The bridge checks the invocation result
- **THEN** The exception is detected and cleared before the call proceeds or returns

#### Scenario: ConvertReturnedJavaString
- **GIVEN** A bridged method returns a non-null Java string
- **WHEN** The bridge reads the result
- **THEN** The string is converted to a JavaScript string and JNI resources are released


## Sub-components

> `NativeBridge` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: ModuleExportLookup

> Implements: `module.getExportByName`

Resolves the named native module export needed for Java method access.

#### Requirement: ResolveExport

The system SHALL the block must resolve the requested export from the native module.

#### Scenario: ExportExists
- **GIVEN** the native module exposes the requested export
- **WHEN** the export is resolved
- **THEN** the export address is returned

#### Scenario: ExportMissing
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** the native module does not expose the requested export
- **WHEN** the export is resolved
- **THEN** resolution reports failure

### Sub-component: Utf8StringAllocation

> Implements: `Memory.allocUtf8String`

Creates native memory containing a null-terminated UTF-8 representation of a string.

#### Requirement: AllocateUtf8Input

The system SHALL the block must allocate a native utf-8 string representing the supplied value.

#### Scenario: AllocateNonemptyString
- **GIVEN** a nonempty string value
- **WHEN** UTF-8 memory is allocated
- **THEN** the returned memory contains the encoded value and a terminator

### Sub-component: NativeMemoryAllocation

> Implements: `Memory.alloc`

Allocates native memory for data used by the bridge.

#### Requirement: AllocateNativeBuffer

The system SHALL the block must allocate a native buffer of the requested size.

#### Scenario: AllocateRequestedSize
- **GIVEN** a valid allocation size
- **WHEN** native memory is allocated
- **THEN** a buffer of the requested size is returned

### Sub-component: MethodMetadata

> Implements: `methodInfo`

Obtains metadata needed to identify and invoke a Java method.

#### Requirement: ObtainMethodMetadata

The system SHALL the block must provide the method metadata required by subsequent bridge operations.

#### Scenario: MetadataAvailable
- **GIVEN** a method description that can be resolved
- **WHEN** method metadata is requested
- **THEN** metadata for that method is returned

### Sub-component: MetadataPointerRead

> Implements: `info.readPointer`

Reads a pointer value from method metadata.

#### Requirement: ReadMetadataPointer

The system SHALL the block must read and return the pointer stored at the metadata location.

#### Scenario: ReadPointerValue
- **GIVEN** metadata contains a readable pointer
- **WHEN** the pointer is read
- **THEN** the stored pointer value is returned

### Sub-component: MetadataOffset

> Implements: `info.add`

Calculates an address at an offset from method metadata.

#### Requirement: OffsetMetadataAddress

The system SHALL the block must produce the address at the requested offset from the metadata pointer.

#### Scenario: ApplyMetadataOffset
- **GIVEN** a metadata pointer and valid offset
- **WHEN** the offset is applied
- **THEN** the address corresponding to that offset is returned

### Sub-component: EnvironmentPointerRead

> Implements: `env.readPointer`

Reads a JNI function pointer from the environment table.

#### Requirement: ReadEnvironmentFunction

The system SHALL the block must return the function pointer stored at the jni environment location.

#### Scenario: EnvironmentEntryReadable
- **GIVEN** the JNI environment contains a readable function pointer
- **WHEN** the pointer is read
- **THEN** that function pointer is returned

### Sub-component: JniFunctionAddress

> Implements: `jni`

Computes the address of a JNI function-table entry.

#### Requirement: LocateJniFunction

The system SHALL the block must calculate the address of the requested jni function-table entry.

#### Scenario: LocateTableEntry
- **GIVEN** a function-table base and valid entry offset
- **WHEN** the JNI function address is calculated
- **THEN** the address of the selected table entry is returned

### Sub-component: MaximumValue

> Implements: `Math.max`

Selects the greater of the supplied numeric values.

#### Requirement: SelectMaximum

The system SHALL the block must return the greater of the supplied numeric values.

#### Scenario: FirstValueIsGreater
- **GIVEN** the first numeric value is greater than the second
- **WHEN** the maximum is selected
- **THEN** the first value is returned

### Sub-component: ByteArrayWrite

> Implements: `values.writeByteArray`

Writes byte data into the native values buffer.

#### Requirement: WriteArgumentBytes

The system SHALL the block must write the supplied bytes to the values buffer.

#### Scenario: WriteByteSequence
- **GIVEN** a values buffer and a byte sequence
- **WHEN** the byte sequence is written
- **THEN** the buffer contains the supplied bytes in order

### Sub-component: PointerConversion

> Implements: `ptr`

Converts a value into the pointer representation expected by the bridge.

#### Requirement: ConvertToPointer

The system SHALL the block must provide the pointer representation of the supplied value.

#### Scenario: ConvertPointerValue
- **GIVEN** a value representing a native address
- **WHEN** the value is converted
- **THEN** a pointer representing that address is returned

### Sub-component: JavaStringCreation

> Implements: `newString`

Creates a Java string from bridge-provided text.

#### Requirement: CreateJavaString

The system SHALL the block must create a java string representing the supplied text.

#### Scenario: CreateFromText
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** valid text and an active JNI context
- **WHEN** a Java string is created
- **THEN** a Java string reference for that text is returned

### Sub-component: JniExceptionCheck

> Implements: `check`

Checks for a pending JNI exception and handles it through the available exception operations.

#### Requirement: CheckPendingException

The system SHALL the block must check for a jni exception and must clear it when the exception handling contract requires clearing.

#### Scenario: ExceptionPending
- **GIVEN** the JNI context has a pending exception
- **WHEN** check is called
- **THEN** the exception is detected and cleared

#### Scenario: NoExceptionPending
- **GIVEN** the JNI context has no pending exception
- **WHEN** check is called
- **THEN** the check completes without clearing an exception

### Sub-component: StringNullCheck

> Implements: `string.isNull`

Determines whether a string pointer is null.

#### Requirement: CheckStringPointer

The system SHALL the block must report whether the string pointer is null.

#### Scenario: StringPointerIsNull
- **GIVEN** the string pointer is null
- **WHEN** its null status is checked
- **THEN** the result indicates null

### Sub-component: ReferenceTracking

> Implements: `refs.push`

Adds a JNI reference to the collection of references managed for cleanup.

#### Requirement: TrackReference

The system SHALL the block must add the supplied reference to the cleanup collection.

#### Scenario: TrackCreatedReference
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** a reference collection and a JNI reference
- **WHEN** the reference is tracked
- **THEN** the reference is present in the collection

### Sub-component: ValuesOffset

> Implements: `values.add`

Calculates an address at an offset in the values buffer.

#### Requirement: OffsetValuesAddress

The system SHALL the block must return the address at the requested offset from the values buffer.

#### Scenario: ApplyValuesOffset
- **GIVEN** a values buffer and valid offset
- **WHEN** the offset is applied
- **THEN** the corresponding buffer address is returned

### Sub-component: IntegerValidation

> Implements: `Number.isInteger`

Determines whether a value is an integer.

#### Requirement: ValidateIntegerValue

The system SHALL the block must indicate whether the supplied value is an integer.

#### Scenario: ValueIsInteger
- **GIVEN** the supplied value is an integer
- **WHEN** integer validation is performed
- **THEN** the result indicates an integer

#### Scenario: ValueIsNotInteger
- **GIVEN** the supplied value is fractional
- **WHEN** integer validation is performed
- **THEN** the result indicates it is not an integer

### Sub-component: MethodInvocation

> Implements: `invoke`

Invokes the selected Java method through the native bridge.

#### Requirement: InvokeJavaMethod

The system SHALL the block must invoke the selected java method with the prepared receiver and arguments.

#### Scenario: InvokeWithArguments
- **GIVEN** a resolvable method, receiver, and valid arguments
- **WHEN** the method is invoked
- **THEN** the method result is returned

### Sub-component: OutputNullCheck

> Implements: `output.isNull`

Determines whether the invocation output pointer is null.

#### Requirement: CheckOutputPointer

The system SHALL the block must report whether the output pointer is null.

#### Scenario: OutputIsNull
- **GIVEN** the invocation output pointer is null
- **WHEN** its null status is checked
- **THEN** the result indicates null

### Sub-component: CharacterAccess

> Implements: `getChars`

Obtains the character data associated with a Java string.

#### Requirement: ObtainStringCharacters

The system SHALL the block must provide access to the characters of the supplied java string.

#### Scenario: AccessStringCharacters
<!-- openlore-test: priority=high (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** a valid Java string reference
- **WHEN** its characters are obtained
- **THEN** the string character data is returned

### Sub-component: TextNullCheck

> Implements: `text.isNull`

Determines whether the text pointer is null.

#### Requirement: CheckTextPointer

The system SHALL the block must report whether the text pointer is null.

#### Scenario: TextPointerIsNull
- **GIVEN** the text pointer is null
- **WHEN** its null status is checked
- **THEN** the result indicates null

### Sub-component: Utf8TextRead

> Implements: `text.readUtf8String`

Reads UTF-8 text from native memory.

#### Requirement: ReadNativeText

The system SHALL the block must return the utf-8 string stored at the text pointer.

#### Scenario: ReadTerminatedText
- **GIVEN** the text pointer references a null-terminated UTF-8 string
- **WHEN** the string is read
- **THEN** the decoded text is returned

### Sub-component: CharacterRelease

> Implements: `releaseChars`

Releases character data previously obtained from a Java string.

#### Requirement: ReleaseStringCharacters

The system SHALL the block must release the character data associated with the java string.

#### Scenario: ReleaseObtainedCharacters
- **GIVEN** character data was obtained for a Java string
- **WHEN** the data is released
- **THEN** the release operation is performed for that string and data

### Sub-component: ReverseReferenceOrder

> Implements: `refs.reverse`

Reverses the order of tracked references before cleanup.

#### Requirement: ReverseTrackedReferences

The system SHALL the block must reverse the order of references in the cleanup collection.

#### Scenario: ReverseMultipleReferences
- **GIVEN** a cleanup collection containing multiple references
- **WHEN** the collection is reversed
- **THEN** the references appear in reverse order

### Sub-component: ReferenceNullCheck

> Implements: `ref.isNull`

Determines whether a tracked reference is null.

#### Requirement: CheckReferencePointer

The system SHALL the block must report whether the reference is null.

#### Scenario: ReferenceIsNull
- **GIVEN** the reference is null
- **WHEN** its null status is checked
- **THEN** the result indicates null

### Sub-component: ReferenceDeletion

> Implements: `deleteRef`

Deletes a JNI reference that is no longer needed.

#### Requirement: DeleteTrackedReference

The system SHALL the block must delete the supplied jni reference.

#### Scenario: DeleteNonNullReference
- **GIVEN** a non-null JNI reference that is no longer needed
- **WHEN** the reference is deleted
- **THEN** the reference deletion operation is performed

### Sub-component: NullCheck

> Implements: `isNull`

Determines whether the current value is null.

#### Requirement: CheckCurrentValue

The system SHALL the block must report whether the current value is null.

#### Scenario: CurrentValueIsNull
- **GIVEN** the current value is null
- **WHEN** isNull is called
- **THEN** the result indicates null

### Sub-component: Utf8Read

> Implements: `readUtf8String`

Reads a UTF-8 string from the current native pointer.

#### Requirement: ReadCurrentUtf8String

The system SHALL the block must return the utf-8 string stored at the current pointer.

#### Scenario: ReadCurrentString
- **GIVEN** the current pointer references a null-terminated UTF-8 string
- **WHEN** readUtf8String is called
- **THEN** the decoded string is returned

> `source/exvius/preflight.py`

### Requirement: Audit

The system SHALL the preflight module shall inspect the selected bundle, data root, and profile and return audit findings, including relevant event facts and dependency results.

#### Scenario: AuditSelectedProjectData
- **GIVEN** A bundle, data root, and profile are provided
- **WHEN** audit is called
- **THEN** The module evaluates the selected data and returns findings and dependency results

#### Scenario: AuditMissingOrInvalidData
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The selected profile references missing or invalid data
- **WHEN** audit is called
- **THEN** The module reports the detected issues in its audit results

### Requirement: ReadRows

The system SHALL the preflight module shall read rows from a data file and return the parsed records.

- **Implementation**: `read_rows::source/exvius/preflight.py`

#### Scenario: ReadValidRows
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A data file contains readable row records
- **WHEN** read_rows is called with its path
- **THEN** The parsed rows are returned

#### Scenario: HandleInvalidRowData
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A data file contains invalid row data
- **WHEN** read_rows is called with its path
- **THEN** The operation reports the invalid data

### Requirement: Read

The system SHALL a bundle shall provide the contents of a named entry.

#### Scenario: ReadBundleEntry
- **GIVEN** A bundle contains the named entry
- **WHEN** read is called
- **THEN** The entry contents are returned

#### Scenario: ReadUnavailableEntry
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The requested bundle entry is unavailable or invalid
- **WHEN** read is called
- **THEN** The operation reports an error

### Requirement: Close

The system SHALL a bundle shall release its resources when closed.

#### Scenario: CloseBundle
- **GIVEN** A bundle has been opened
- **WHEN** close is called
- **THEN** The bundle's resources are released

- **GIVEN** Source banner data contains raid banners with local personal-reward links
- **WHEN** raid_reward_banners is called
- **THEN** The target data includes the local reward links and retains every original banner row

### Requirement: RestoreStoryEventEntries

The system SHALL the service shall restore story-event scene links while retaining all existing retail story entries.

- **Implementation**: `story_event_entries::source/exvius/prepare_offline_cache.py`

#### Scenario: RestoreSceneLinks
- **GIVEN** Source data contains scene links missing from the target
- **WHEN** story_event_entries is called
- **THEN** The target includes the restored links and preserves every existing retail story entry

### Requirement: SelectArenaEntryEvents

The system SHALL the service shall use the client's direct arena entry when the optional foyer is unavailable.

- **Implementation**: `arena_entry_events::source/exvius/prepare_offline_cache.py`

#### Scenario: OptionalFoyerIsAbsent
- **GIVEN** Arena data has no optional foyer
- **WHEN** arena_entry_events is called
- **THEN** The target uses the client's normal direct entry

### Requirement: UpdateLoadingText

The system SHALL the service shall apply requested loading-text replacements and optionally append missing entries.

- **Implementation**: `loading_text::source/exvius/prepare_offline_cache.py`

#### Scenario: ReplaceLoadingText
- **GIVEN** The source contains loading text matching a requested replacement
- **WHEN** loading_text is called
- **THEN** The target contains the replacement text

#### Scenario: AppendMissingTextWhenRequested
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** append_missing is enabled and requested text is absent
- **WHEN** loading_text is called
- **THEN** The missing text is added to the target

### Requirement: RestoreEventSkillSets

The system SHALL the service shall add preserved encounter skill sets missing from the global client cache.

- **Implementation**: `event_skill_sets::source/exvius/prepare_offline_cache.py`

#### Scenario: AddMissingEncounterSkillSets
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Preserved encounter skill sets are absent from the Global client cache
- **WHEN** event_skill_sets is called
- **THEN** The target includes those skill sets without duplicating existing entries

### Requirement: RestoreExbonusMasterData

The system SHALL the service shall merge restored ex bonus master data into the specified table.

- **Implementation**: `exbonus_master::source/exvius/prepare_offline_cache.py`

#### Scenario: MergeRestoredMasterRows
- **GIVEN** Restored EX bonus rows are available for the specified table
- **WHEN** exbonus_master is called
- **THEN** The target includes the restored rows while preserving existing data

### Requirement: UpdateLocalizedTextVersions

The system SHALL the service shall update localized-text versions using the localized-text index to select the target file.

- **Implementation**: `localized_text_versions::source/exvius/prepare_offline_cache.py`

#### Scenario: SelectLocalizedTextFileFromIndex
- **GIVEN** The localized-text index identifies the file to update
- **WHEN** localized_text_versions is called
- **THEN** The selected file receives the requested replacements

### Requirement: RestoreExchangeShopComments

The system SHALL the service shall fill absent exchange-shop greetings while preserving the shipped english lines.

- **Implementation**: `exchange_shop_comments::source/exvius/prepare_offline_cache.py`

#### Scenario: FillMissingGreetings
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Exchange-shop data lacks greetings available in the source
- **WHEN** exchange_shop_comments is called
- **THEN** Missing greetings are added and shipped English lines remain unchanged

### Requirement: PrepareOfflineCache

The system SHALL the service shall coordinate cache restoration and preparation across the configured client data sources.

#### Scenario: PrepareConfiguredCache
- **GIVEN** Client manifests and source data are available
- **WHEN** main is called
- **THEN** Configured cache data is processed and the resulting offline data is written


## Sub-components

> `Offline Cache Preparation` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: JsonRecord

> Implements: `json_record`

Parses a raw JSON record, decoding bytes when necessary.

#### Requirement: ParseJsonRecord

The system SHALL the function must decode byte input and parse the resulting json value.

#### Scenario: ValidJsonBytes
- **GIVEN** raw is valid JSON encoded as bytes
- **WHEN** json_record is called
- **THEN** the parsed JSON value is returned

#### Requirement: RejectInvalidJsonRecord

The system SHALL the function must reject invalid json records.

#### Scenario: InvalidRecord
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** raw cannot be parsed as JSON
- **WHEN** json_record is called
- **THEN** a ValueError is raised

### Sub-component: CbcDecrypt

> Implements: `cbc_decrypt`

Decodes and decrypts a base64-encoded CBC payload.

#### Requirement: DecryptPayload

The system SHALL the function must base64-decode and cbc-decrypt the supplied payload using the given key and iv.

#### Scenario: ValidPayload
- **GIVEN** the key and base64 payload correspond to a valid CBC-encrypted body
- **WHEN** cbc_decrypt is called
- **THEN** the original plaintext bytes are returned

#### Requirement: RejectInvalidPayload

The system SHALL the function must reject malformed or invalid encrypted payloads.

#### Scenario: InvalidPayload
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** the payload cannot be decoded or decrypted
- **WHEN** cbc_decrypt is called
- **THEN** a ValueError is raised

### Sub-component: Key16

> Implements: `key16`

Converts an eight-character EncodeKey token into a 16-byte key.

#### Requirement: NormalizeEncodeKey

The system SHALL the function must encode the token as utf-8 and nul-pad it to 16 bytes.

#### Scenario: ShortUtf8Token
<!-- openlore-test: priority=high (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** token is an eight-character EncodeKey
- **WHEN** key16 is called
- **THEN** the returned key is UTF-8 encoded and NUL-padded to 16 bytes

### Sub-component: BytesConversion

> Implements: `bytes`

Converts an input value to bytes where required by cache preparation.

#### Requirement: ConvertToBytes

The system SHALL the conversion must produce bytes from a supported input value.

#### Scenario: ConvertSupportedValue
- **GIVEN** the input is supported by bytes conversion
- **WHEN** bytes is called
- **THEN** a bytes value is returned

### Sub-component: ValueError

> Implements: `ValueError`

Signals invalid values encountered during cache preparation.

#### Requirement: SignalInvalidValue

The system SHALL the operation must report invalid input using valueerror.

#### Scenario: InvalidInput
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** an operation encounters an invalid value
- **WHEN** the error is raised
- **THEN** the error is a ValueError

### Sub-component: CbcEncrypt

> Implements: `cbc_encrypt`

Encrypts a reply body and returns its base64 representation.

#### Requirement: EncryptReplyBody

The system SHALL the function must cbc-encrypt the plaintext with the supplied key and iv and return a base64 string.

#### Scenario: EncryptPlaintext
- **GIVEN** a key and plaintext body are supplied
- **WHEN** cbc_encrypt is called
- **THEN** a base64 encrypted string is returned

### Sub-component: ReadSourceText

> Implements: `source.read_text`

Reads source cache data as text.

#### Requirement: ReadSourceText

The system SHALL the operation must return the source file contents as text.

#### Scenario: SourceExists
- **GIVEN** the source file is readable
- **WHEN** source.read_text is called
- **THEN** its text contents are returned

### Sub-component: ParseJson

> Implements: `json.loads`

Parses JSON text into a data value.

#### Requirement: ParseJsonText

The system SHALL the operation must parse valid json text into its corresponding value.

#### Scenario: ValidJson
- **GIVEN** input is valid JSON text
- **WHEN** json.loads is called
- **THEN** the corresponding value is returned

### Sub-component: ArchiveExists

> Implements: `archive.exists`

Checks whether an archive path exists.

#### Requirement: CheckArchivePresence

The system SHALL the operation must report whether the archive exists.

#### Scenario: ArchivePresent
- **GIVEN** the archive path exists
- **WHEN** archive.exists is called
- **THEN** true is returned

### Sub-component: ReadArchiveText

> Implements: `archive.read_text`

Reads archive-related file contents as text.

#### Requirement: ReadArchiveText

The system SHALL the operation must return the archive file contents as text.

#### Scenario: ArchiveReadable
- **GIVEN** the archive file is readable as text
- **WHEN** archive.read_text is called
- **THEN** its text contents are returned

### Sub-component: AppendLine

> Implements: `lines.append`

Adds a line to a collection of text lines.

#### Requirement: AppendTextLine

The system SHALL the operation must add the supplied line to the end of the lines collection.

#### Scenario: AppendLineToCollection
- **GIVEN** a lines collection and a line are available
- **WHEN** lines.append is called
- **THEN** the line is present at the end of the collection

### Sub-component: SerializeJson

> Implements: `json.dumps`

Serializes a data value as JSON text.

#### Requirement: SerializeJsonValue

The system SHALL the operation must produce json text representing the supplied value.

#### Scenario: SerializeValue
- **GIVEN** a JSON-serializable value is supplied
- **WHEN** json.dumps is called
- **THEN** JSON text representing the value is returned

### Sub-component: AddExisting

> Implements: `existing.add`

Adds an item to the set of existing values.

#### Requirement: RecordExistingItem

The system SHALL the operation must include the supplied item in the existing-value set.

#### Scenario: AddNewItem
- **GIVEN** an item is not yet in the set
- **WHEN** existing.add is called
- **THEN** the item is present in the set

### Sub-component: WriteTargetText

> Implements: `target.write_text`

Writes prepared text to the target file.

#### Requirement: WriteTargetText

The system SHALL the operation must write the supplied text to the target path.

#### Scenario: WriteText
- **GIVEN** the target is writable
- **WHEN** target.write_text is called
- **THEN** the supplied text is stored at the target

### Sub-component: NextValue

> Implements: `next`

Retrieves the next value from an iterator.

#### Requirement: RetrieveNextValue

The system SHALL the operation must return the next available iterator value.

#### Scenario: IteratorHasValue
- **GIVEN** the iterator has a next value
- **WHEN** next is called
- **THEN** that value is returned

### Sub-component: Decode

> Implements: `decode`

Decodes encrypted or encoded cache data into records, optionally applying localized-record handling.

#### Requirement: DecodeCacheRecords

The system SHALL the function must decode the supplied cache payload and return its valid records.

#### Scenario: ValidPayloadRecords
- **GIVEN** raw contains valid encoded cache records and a matching key is supplied
- **WHEN** decode is called
- **THEN** the decoded records are returned

#### Requirement: PreserveMultiplePaddedRecords

The system SHALL the function must preserve all records when decoding double-padded cbc data.

#### Scenario: DoublePaddedMultipleRecords
- **GIVEN** the payload contains multiple double-padded records
- **WHEN** decode is called
- **THEN** all records are preserved

#### Requirement: SelectNativeIndexNames

The system SHALL the function must select all event enemy names indicated by the native index.

#### Scenario: NativeIndexSelectsNames
- **GIVEN** the native index identifies event enemy names
- **WHEN** decode is called
- **THEN** all indicated names are included

#### Requirement: RejectUndecodablePayload

The system SHALL the function must reject payloads that cannot be decoded as valid records.

#### Scenario: InvalidPayload
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** the payload is malformed or cannot be decrypted
- **WHEN** decode is called
- **THEN** a ValueError is raised

### Sub-component: PopPending

> Implements: `pending.pop`

Removes and returns an item from the pending mapping.

#### Requirement: RemovePendingItem

The system SHALL the operation must remove and return the requested pending item.

#### Scenario: KeyIsPending
- **GIVEN** the requested key exists in pending
- **WHEN** pending.pop is called
- **THEN** its value is returned and the key is removed

### Sub-component: AppendOutput

> Implements: `output.append`

Adds a value to the output collection.

#### Requirement: AppendOutputValue

The system SHALL the operation must add the supplied value to the end of output.

#### Scenario: AppendValue
- **GIVEN** an output collection and value are available
- **WHEN** output.append is called
- **THEN** the value is present at the end of output

### Sub-component: PendingValues

> Implements: `pending.values`

Provides the values stored in the pending mapping.

#### Requirement: GetPendingValues

The system SHALL the operation must expose the values currently stored in pending.

#### Scenario: PendingHasValues
- **GIVEN** pending contains one or more entries
- **WHEN** pending.values is called
- **THEN** the stored values are provided

### Sub-component: WriteTargetBytes

> Implements: `target.write_bytes`

Writes prepared binary data to the target file.

#### Requirement: WriteTargetBytes

The system SHALL the operation must write the supplied bytes to the target path.

#### Scenario: WriteBytes
- **GIVEN** the target is writable
- **WHEN** target.write_bytes is called
- **THEN** the supplied bytes are stored at the target

### Sub-component: ReadTargetBytes

> Implements: `target.read_bytes`

Reads the target file as bytes.

#### Requirement: ReadTargetBytes

The system SHALL the operation must return the target file contents as bytes.

#### Scenario: TargetReadable
- **GIVEN** the target file exists and is readable
- **WHEN** target.read_bytes is called
- **THEN** its contents are returned as bytes

### Sub-component: WriteRuntimeBytes

> Implements: `runtime.write_bytes`

Writes binary data to the runtime file.

#### Requirement: WriteRuntimeBytes

The system SHALL the operation must write the supplied bytes to the runtime path.

#### Scenario: RuntimeWritable
- **GIVEN** the runtime path is writable
- **WHEN** runtime.write_bytes is called
- **THEN** the supplied bytes are stored

### Sub-component: Any

> Implements: `any`

Checks whether at least one item satisfies a condition.

#### Requirement: CheckAnyItem

The system SHALL the operation must return true when at least one item satisfies the supplied condition.

#### Scenario: MatchingItemExists
- **GIVEN** at least one iterable item satisfies the condition
- **WHEN** any is called
- **THEN** true is returned

### Sub-component: RootRglob

> Implements: `root.rglob`

Finds files under the root path matching a pattern.

#### Requirement: FindMatchingPaths

The system SHALL the operation must provide matching paths beneath the root.

#### Scenario: MatchingFilesExist
- **GIVEN** files beneath root match the pattern
- **WHEN** root.rglob is called
- **THEN** the matching paths are provided

### Sub-component: CopyFile

> Implements: `shutil.copyfile`

Copies file contents from a source path to a destination path.

#### Requirement: CopyFileContents

The system SHALL the operation must copy the source file contents to the destination.

#### Scenario: SourceExists
- **GIVEN** the source file is readable and the destination is writable
- **WHEN** shutil.copyfile is called
- **THEN** the destination contains the source contents

### Sub-component: GetRowValue

> Implements: `row.get`

Retrieves a value from a row by key.

#### Requirement: RetrieveRowField

The system SHALL the operation must return the row value associated with the requested key, or its default when absent.

#### Scenario: FieldPresent
- **GIVEN** the row contains the requested key
- **WHEN** row.get is called
- **THEN** the associated value is returned

### Sub-component: Cipher

> Implements: `Cipher`

Provides a cipher object for encrypting or decrypting cache data.

#### Requirement: CreateCipher

The system SHALL the operation must provide a cipher for the specified algorithm and key configuration.

#### Scenario: ValidConfiguration
- **GIVEN** a supported algorithm and valid key configuration are supplied
- **WHEN** Cipher is called
- **THEN** a cipher object is provided

### Sub-component: All

> Implements: `all`

Checks whether every item satisfies a condition.

#### Requirement: CheckAllItems

The system SHALL the operation must return true only when every item satisfies the supplied condition.

#### Scenario: AllItemsMatch
- **GIVEN** every iterable item satisfies the condition
- **WHEN** all is called
- **THEN** true is returned

### Sub-component: CreateDecryptor

> Implements: `cipher.decryptor`

Creates a decryptor for the configured cipher.

#### Requirement: CreateDecryptor

The system SHALL the operation must provide a decryptor for the configured cipher.

#### Scenario: ConfiguredCipher
- **GIVEN** a cipher is configured for decryption
- **WHEN** cipher.decryptor is called
- **THEN** a decryptor is returned

### Sub-component: DecryptorUpdate

> Implements: `decryptor.update`

Decrypts an input chunk using the decryptor.

#### Requirement: DecryptChunk

The system SHALL the operation must return decrypted output for the supplied input chunk.

#### Scenario: EncryptedChunk
- **GIVEN** an encrypted chunk is supplied to a valid decryptor
- **WHEN** decryptor.update is called
- **THEN** decrypted bytes are returned

### Sub-component: Base64Decode

> Implements: `base64.b64decode`

Decodes base64 input into bytes.

#### Requirement: DecodeBase64

The system SHALL the operation must return the bytes represented by valid base64 input.

#### Scenario: ValidBase64
- **GIVEN** input is valid base64
- **WHEN** base64.b64decode is called
- **THEN** the represented bytes are returned

### Sub-component: DecryptorFinalize

> Implements: `decryptor.finalize`

Finalizes decryption and returns any remaining plaintext bytes.

#### Requirement: FinalizeDecryption

The system SHALL the operation must complete decryption and return any remaining plaintext bytes.

#### Scenario: ValidCiphertextComplete
- **GIVEN** the decryptor has processed valid ciphertext
- **WHEN** decryptor.finalize is called
- **THEN** the remaining plaintext bytes are returned

### Sub-component: DecodePlaintext

> Implements: `plain.decode`

Decodes plaintext bytes into text.

#### Requirement: DecodePlaintextBytes

The system SHALL the operation must decode plaintext bytes using the requested encoding.

#### Scenario: ValidEncodedPlaintext
- **GIVEN** plaintext bytes are valid in the requested encoding
- **WHEN** plain.decode is called
- **THEN** the corresponding text is returned

### Sub-component: RstripLine

> Implements: `line.rstrip`

Removes trailing characters from a line.

#### Requirement: StripLineEnding

The system SHALL the operation must return the line without its trailing whitespace or specified trailing characters.

#### Scenario: LineHasTrailingWhitespace
- **GIVEN** the line ends in removable whitespace
- **WHEN** line.rstrip is called
- **THEN** the trailing whitespace is removed

### Sub-component: AddChanged

> Implements: `changed.add`

Records an item as changed.

#### Requirement: RecordChangedItem

The system SHALL the operation must include the supplied item in the changed set.

#### Scenario: RecordItem
- **GIVEN** an item has changed
- **WHEN** changed.add is called
- **THEN** the item is present in changed

### Sub-component: CreateEncryptor

> Implements: `cipher.encryptor`

Creates an encryptor for the configured cipher.

#### Requirement: CreateEncryptor

The system SHALL the operation must provide an encryptor for the configured cipher.

#### Scenario: ConfiguredCipher
- **GIVEN** a cipher is configured for encryption
- **WHEN** cipher.encryptor is called
- **THEN** an encryptor is returned

### Sub-component: Base64Encode

> Implements: `base64.b64encode`

Encodes bytes as base64.

#### Requirement: EncodeBase64

The system SHALL the operation must return the base64 representation of the supplied bytes.

#### Scenario: EncodeBytes
- **GIVEN** a bytes value is supplied
- **WHEN** base64.b64encode is called
- **THEN** its base64 representation is returned

### Sub-component: EncryptorUpdate

> Implements: `encryptor.update`

Encrypts an input chunk using the encryptor.

#### Requirement: EncryptChunk

The system SHALL the operation must return encrypted output for the supplied plaintext chunk.

#### Scenario: PlaintextChunk
- **GIVEN** plaintext is supplied to a valid encryptor
- **WHEN** encryptor.update is called
- **THEN** encrypted bytes are returned

### Sub-component: EncryptorFinalize

> Implements: `encryptor.finalize`

Finalizes encryption and returns any remaining ciphertext bytes.

#### Requirement: FinalizeEncryption

The system SHALL the operation must complete encryption and return any remaining ciphertext bytes.

#### Scenario: EncryptionComplete
- **GIVEN** the encryptor has processed plaintext
- **WHEN** encryptor.finalize is called
- **THEN** remaining ciphertext bytes are returned

Provides the values stored in the encounters mapping.

### Sub-component: LoadingText

> Implements: `loading_text`

Restores and updates encrypted text cache entries, optionally appending missing entries.

The system SHALL the function must decrypt source entries, apply requested replacements, and write updated encrypted text to the target.

- **WHEN** loading_text is called

### Sub-component: StoryEventEntries

## Sub-components

> `Distribution Publisher` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: AnyMatch

> Implements: `any`

Determines whether at least one candidate meets a condition.

#### Requirement: DetectMatchingCandidate

The system SHALL the block shall report whether any candidate satisfies the supplied condition.

#### Scenario: CandidateMatches
- **GIVEN** The candidate collection contains an item that satisfies the condition
- **WHEN** The block checks the candidates
- **THEN** It SHALL report that a match exists

#### Scenario: NoCandidateMatches
- **GIVEN** No candidate satisfies the condition
- **WHEN** The block checks the candidates
- **THEN** It SHALL report that no match exists

### Sub-component: PathConstruction

> Implements: `Path`

Creates a filesystem path value from the supplied path components.

#### Requirement: ConstructPath

The system SHALL the block shall represent the supplied path components as a filesystem path.

#### Scenario: ValidComponents
- **GIVEN** The supplied components describe a path
- **WHEN** The block constructs the path
- **THEN** It SHALL return a path representing those components

### Sub-component: ValueError

> Implements: `ValueError`

Signals that an input value or requested operation is invalid.

#### Requirement: ReportInvalidValue

The system SHALL the block shall make an invalid value condition available as a valueerror.

#### Scenario: InvalidValue
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A value violates an expected constraint
- **WHEN** The block reports the condition
- **THEN** It SHALL provide a ValueError describing the invalid value

### Sub-component: StageResolve

> Implements: `STAGE.resolve`

Resolves the staging directory to an absolute filesystem path.

#### Requirement: ResolveStagePath

The system SHALL the block shall resolve the staging path against its filesystem context.

#### Scenario: RelativeStagePath
- **GIVEN** The staging path is relative
- **WHEN** The block resolves it
- **THEN** It SHALL return the corresponding resolved path

### Sub-component: StageMkdir

> Implements: `STAGE.mkdir`

Ensures that the staging directory exists.

#### Requirement: CreateStageDirectory

The system SHALL the block shall ensure the staging directory is present before staging files.

#### Scenario: StageDirectoryAbsent
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The staging directory does not exist
- **WHEN** The block prepares the staging directory
- **THEN** It SHALL create the directory

### Sub-component: SourceRelativePath

> Implements: `source.relative_to`

Determines a source path relative to its expected base directory.

#### Requirement: DeriveSourceRelativePath

The system SHALL the block shall derive the source path relative to the designated base path.

#### Scenario: SourceWithinBase
- **GIVEN** The source path is beneath the base path
- **WHEN** The block derives its relative path
- **THEN** It SHALL return the source path relative to the base

### Sub-component: SourceIsFile

> Implements: `source.is_file`

Checks whether a source path refers to a regular file.

#### Requirement: CheckSourceFile

The system SHALL the block shall report whether the source path refers to a regular file.

#### Scenario: SourceIsRegularFile
- **GIVEN** The source path refers to a regular file
- **WHEN** The block checks the source
- **THEN** It SHALL report that the source is a file

#### Scenario: SourceIsNotFile
- **GIVEN** The source path does not refer to a regular file
- **WHEN** The block checks the source
- **THEN** It SHALL report that the source is not a file

### Sub-component: SourceIsSymlink

> Implements: `source.is_symlink`

Checks whether a source path is a symbolic link.

#### Requirement: CheckSourceSymlink

The system SHALL the block shall report whether the source path is a symbolic link.

#### Scenario: SourceIsSymbolicLink
- **GIVEN** The source path is a symbolic link
- **WHEN** The block checks the source
- **THEN** It SHALL report that the source is a symbolic link

### Sub-component: AppendSelected

> Implements: `selected.append`

Adds one eligible item to the selected distribution entries.

#### Requirement: AddSelectedItem

The system SHALL the block shall add the supplied item to the selected entries.

#### Scenario: EligibleItemSelected
- **GIVEN** An item has been determined eligible for distribution
- **WHEN** The block adds it to the selection
- **THEN** The selected entries SHALL include that item

### Sub-component: ExtendSelected

> Implements: `selected.extend`

Adds multiple eligible items to the selected distribution entries.

#### Requirement: AddSelectedItems

The system SHALL the block shall add each supplied item to the selected entries.

#### Scenario: EligibleItemsSelected

- **WHEN** The block adds the collection to the selection
- **THEN** The selected entries SHALL include every supplied item

### Sub-component: PathRelativePath

> Implements: `path.relative_to`

Determines a path relative to its expected base directory.

#### Requirement: DeriveRelativePath

The system SHALL the block shall derive a path relative to the designated base path.

#### Scenario: PathWithinBase
- **GIVEN** The path is beneath the base path
- **WHEN** The block derives its relative path
- **THEN** It SHALL return the path relative to the base

### Sub-component: PathIsFile

> Implements: `path.is_file`

Checks whether a path refers to a regular file.

#### Requirement: CheckPathFile

The system SHALL the block shall report whether the path refers to a regular file.

#### Scenario: PathIsRegularFile
- **GIVEN** The path refers to a regular file
- **WHEN** The block checks the path
- **THEN** It SHALL report that the path is a file

### Sub-component: SourceStat

> Implements: `source.stat`

Retrieves filesystem metadata for a source path.

#### Requirement: ReadSourceMetadata

The system SHALL the block shall obtain filesystem metadata for the source path.

#### Scenario: SourceMetadataAvailable
- **GIVEN** The source path exists and its metadata is accessible
- **WHEN** The block requests source metadata
- **THEN** It SHALL return metadata for that source

### Sub-component: SensitiveSearch

> Implements: `SENSITIVE.search`

Checks content for a match against the sensitive-data pattern.

#### Requirement: DetectSensitiveContent

The system SHALL the block shall identify whether the supplied content matches the sensitive-data pattern.

#### Scenario: SensitivePatternFound
- **GIVEN** Content contains a value matching the sensitive-data pattern
- **WHEN** The block searches the content
- **THEN** It SHALL return a match

#### Scenario: SensitivePatternAbsent
- **GIVEN** Content contains no value matching the sensitive-data pattern
- **WHEN** The block searches the content
- **THEN** It SHALL return no match

### Sub-component: SourceReadBytes

> Implements: `source.read_bytes`

Reads the contents of a source file as bytes.

#### Requirement: ReadSourceContent

The system SHALL the block shall return the source file contents as bytes.

#### Scenario: SourceReadable
- **GIVEN** The source file is readable
- **WHEN** The block reads its contents
- **THEN** It SHALL return the file contents as bytes

### Sub-component: CopyFile

> Implements: `shutil.copyfile`

Copies source file contents to a destination file.

#### Requirement: CopyDistributionFile

The system SHALL the block shall copy the source file contents to the destination.

#### Scenario: SourceAndDestinationProvided
- **GIVEN** A readable source file and destination path are provided
- **WHEN** The block copies the file
- **THEN** The destination SHALL contain the source contents

### Sub-component: CommandRun

> Implements: `run`

Runs a command and returns its result, optionally capturing its output.

#### Requirement: ExecuteCommand

The system SHALL the block shall execute the supplied command and shall return its result; when capture is enabled, it shall capture command output, and it shall raise runtimeerror when execution fails.

#### Scenario: CommandSucceeds
- **GIVEN** A command completes successfully
- **WHEN** run is called with the command
- **THEN** It SHALL return the command result

#### Scenario: CaptureOutput
- **GIVEN** A command completes and capture is enabled
- **WHEN** run executes the command
- **THEN** It SHALL return the result with command output captured

#### Scenario: CommandFails
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A command fails
- **WHEN** run executes the command
- **THEN** It SHALL raise RuntimeError

### Sub-component: PrepareBundle

> Implements: `prepare`

Prepares a sanitized distribution bundle in the staging area.

#### Requirement: StageSanitizedBundle

The system SHALL the block shall select eligible bundle paths, exclude invalid or unsafe entries, detect sensitive content, and copy accepted files into the staging area.

#### Scenario: ValidBundle
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The bundle contains eligible regular files without sensitive content
- **WHEN** prepare processes the bundle
- **THEN** It SHALL copy the accepted files into the staging area

#### Scenario: InvalidBundle
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The bundle contains an invalid path or unsupported entry
- **WHEN** prepare processes the bundle
- **THEN** It SHALL reject the bundle with ValueError

#### Scenario: SensitiveFile
- **GIVEN** A candidate file contains content matching the sensitive-data pattern
- **WHEN** prepare processes the bundle
- **THEN** It SHALL exclude that file from the staged distribution

### Sub-component: JsonLoad

> Implements: `json.loads`

Parses a JSON document from a string or byte sequence.

#### Requirement: ParseJsonDocument

The system SHALL the block shall parse valid json input into its corresponding data value.

#### Scenario: ValidJson
- **GIVEN** The input contains valid JSON
- **WHEN** The block parses the document
- **THEN** It SHALL return the corresponding data value

### Sub-component: StripStatus

> Implements: `status.strip`

Removes leading and trailing whitespace from a status string.

#### Requirement: NormalizeStatus

The system SHALL the block shall return the status string without leading or trailing whitespace.

#### Scenario: StatusHasSurroundingWhitespace
- **GIVEN** The status string has leading or trailing whitespace
- **WHEN** The block strips the status
- **THEN** It SHALL return the status without surrounding whitespace

### Sub-component: SubprocessRun

> Implements: `subprocess.run`

Executes a subprocess command and provides its completion result.

#### Requirement: ExecuteSubprocess

The system SHALL the block shall execute the supplied command and shall provide the subprocess completion result.

#### Scenario: SubprocessCompletes
- **GIVEN** A valid command is supplied
- **WHEN** The block executes the subprocess
- **THEN** It SHALL provide the command completion result

### Sub-component: AppendPath

> Implements: `paths.append`

Adds a discovered path to the paths collection.

#### Requirement: RecordPath

The system SHALL the block shall add the supplied path to the paths collection.

#### Scenario: PathDiscovered
- **GIVEN** A path has been identified for processing
- **WHEN** The block records it
- **THEN** The paths collection SHALL include that path

### Sub-component: PathStat

> Implements: `path.stat`

Retrieves filesystem metadata for a path.

#### Requirement: ReadPathMetadata

The system SHALL the block shall obtain filesystem metadata for the path.

#### Scenario: PathMetadataAvailable
- **GIVEN** The path exists and its metadata is accessible
- **WHEN** The block requests path metadata
- **THEN** It SHALL return metadata for that path

### Sub-component: AppendMissing

> Implements: `missing.append`

Records an item identified as missing.

#### Requirement: RecordMissingItem

The system SHALL the block shall add the supplied item to the missing-items collection.

#### Scenario: ItemMissing

> `source/exvius/push_client_cache.py`

### Requirement: UpdateResourceRegistry

The system SHALL the service shall update the client registry entries for managed resources using the supplied template, while retaining unrelated preferences and preserving a backup for restoration.

- **Implementation**: `cache_registry::source/exvius/push_client_cache.py`

#### Scenario: UpdateManagedResourceVersions
- **GIVEN** The client has a registry containing managed resources and unrelated preferences
- **WHEN** cache_registry is called with a template
- **THEN** Managed resource versions are updated and unrelated preferences are retained

#### Scenario: RejectInvalidRegistryData
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The client registry or template is invalid
- **WHEN** cache_registry is called
- **THEN** The operation reports a validation or parsing error without silently replacing unrelated preferences

### Requirement: StageAndPushResources

The system SHALL the service shall collect eligible project, recovered, overlay, and template resources, prepare them for transfer, push them to the connected client, and verify the resulting content against expected checksums.

#### Scenario: PushSelectedResources
- **GIVEN** Eligible resource files are available and a client is connected
- **WHEN** main is run with valid arguments
- **THEN** Resources are staged, transferred to the client, and checked against expected content

#### Scenario: RejectInvalidArgumentsOrResourcePaths
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Arguments or selected resource paths violate the command's validation rules
- **WHEN** main is run
- **THEN** The operation reports an error instead of proceeding with an invalid transfer

> `source/exvius/raid_menu.js`

### Requirement: CaptureRaidMenuData

The system SHALL the service shall inspect raid menu results and report available entries and banner data.

#### Scenario: RaidMenuResultIsAvailable
- **GIVEN** A raid menu result contains readable entries and banner data
- **WHEN** The raid menu result hook runs
- **THEN** The service reports the available menu data through the note mechanism

#### Scenario: RaidMenuResultIsNull
- **GIVEN** The raid menu result is null
- **WHEN** The raid menu result hook runs
- **THEN** The service handles the absent result without attempting to decode its entries

### Requirement: CaptureRaidBattleParty

The system SHALL the service shall inspect the player party and battle state to report battle unit identifiers.

#### Scenario: PlayerPartyHasBattleUnits
- **GIVEN** A player party and battle state contain readable units
- **WHEN** The battle party hook runs
- **THEN** The service collects and reports unit identifiers through the bridge and note mechanism

#### Scenario: PartyOrBattleArrayIsAbsent
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The player party or battle unit array is null
- **WHEN** The battle party hook runs
- **THEN** The service handles the missing data without reading unit entries


## Sub-components

> `RaidMenu` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: ResultNullCheck

Determines whether the result pointer is null before the result is used.

- **WHEN** the null check runs
- **THEN** the block reports that the result is null

#### Scenario: ResultIsPresent
- **GIVEN** the result pointer is present
- **WHEN** the null check runs
- **THEN** the block reports that the result is not null

- **WHEN** the pointer is advanced
- **THEN** the resulting pointer addresses the requested offset from result

- **WHEN** the value is read
- **THEN** the block returns the value stored at data

- **GIVEN** a data pointer and an offset
- **WHEN** the pointer is advanced
- **THEN** the resulting pointer addresses the requested offset from data

> `source/exvius/raid/audit_source.py`

### Requirement: Audit

The system SHALL the service shall inspect a raid archive against the supplied mission and report whether the source data meets its audit expectations.

#### Scenario: ArchiveMeetsAuditExpectations
- **GIVEN** A raid archive whose contents are consistent with the supplied mission
- **WHEN** audit is called
- **THEN** The service returns an audit result for the archive

#### Scenario: ArchiveIsInvalidOrInconsistent
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A malformed archive or archive data that fails validation against the supplied mission
- **WHEN** audit is called
- **THEN** The service reports the failure with a validation error


## Sub-components

> `RaidSourceAudit` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: ValueError

> Implements: `ValueError`

Represents invalid input or raid archive data encountered during the audit.

#### Requirement: ReportInvalidData

The system SHALL the audit must represent invalid input or archive data with a valueerror.

#### Scenario: InvalidData
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The audit encounters invalid input or archive data
- **WHEN** It reports the failure
- **THEN** A ValueError is raised

### Sub-component: RecordSelection

> Implements: `select`

Selects records matching the supplied names, waves, campaign, and mission while supporting archive integrity checks.

#### Requirement: SelectMatchingRecords

The system SHALL the audit must select records according to the supplied names, waves, campaign, mission, and optional original data.

#### Scenario: MatchingRecords
- **GIVEN** Records include entries matching the supplied selection criteria
- **WHEN** The audit selects records
- **THEN** The matching records are returned

#### Scenario: NoMatchingRecords
- **GIVEN** No records match the supplied selection criteria
- **WHEN** The audit selects records
- **THEN** No records are selected

#### Requirement: RejectInvalidSelection

The system SHALL the audit must report invalid selection or archive data as a valueerror.

#### Scenario: InvalidSelectionData
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The selection encounters invalid data
- **WHEN** The audit processes the selection
- **THEN** A ValueError is raised

Retrieves the next item from an iterator during audit processing.

#### Requirement: RetrieveNextItem

The system SHALL the audit must retrieve the next available item from the supplied iterator.

#### Scenario: ItemAvailable
- **GIVEN** The iterator contains another item
- **WHEN** The audit requests the next item
- **THEN** That item is returned

The system SHALL the audit must obtain the names of the members contained in the archive.

#### Scenario: ArchiveHasMembers
- **GIVEN** The archive contains members
- **WHEN** The audit lists archive members
- **THEN** The member names are returned

### Sub-component: EndsWith

> Implements: `n.endswith`

Checks whether an archive member name has a required suffix.

#### Requirement: CheckMemberSuffix

The system SHALL the audit must determine whether the member name ends with the supplied suffix.

#### Scenario: MemberHasSuffix
- **GIVEN** A member name ends with the required suffix
- **WHEN** The audit checks the suffix
- **THEN** The result indicates a match

#### Scenario: MemberLacksSuffix
- **GIVEN** A member name does not end with the required suffix
- **WHEN** The audit checks the suffix
- **THEN** The result indicates no match

### Sub-component: ReadArchiveMember

> Implements: `z.read`

Reads the contents of a named member from the raid archive.

#### Requirement: ReadMemberContents

The system SHALL the audit must read and return the contents of the requested archive member.

#### Scenario: MemberExists
- **GIVEN** The requested member exists in the archive
- **WHEN** The audit reads the member
- **THEN** The member contents are returned

### Sub-component: JsonLoads

> Implements: `json.loads`

Parses JSON data from raid archive content.

#### Requirement: ParseJsonData

The system SHALL the audit must parse valid json input into its corresponding data value.

#### Scenario: ValidJson
- **GIVEN** The archive content contains valid JSON
- **WHEN** The audit parses the content
- **THEN** The corresponding data value is returned

### Sub-component: Sha256

> Implements: `hashlib.sha256`

Creates a SHA-256 digest context for checking raid data integrity.

#### Requirement: CreateSha256Digest

The system SHALL the audit must create a sha-256 digest context for the supplied content.

#### Scenario: DigestContent
- **GIVEN** Content is supplied for integrity checking
- **WHEN** The audit creates a SHA-256 digest
- **THEN** A SHA-256 digest context is returned

### Sub-component: ArchiveRead

> Implements: `read`

Reads a named archive member and validates that its name has an accepted suffix.

#### Requirement: ReadNamedMember

The system SHALL the audit must read the requested member when its name satisfies the accepted suffix requirement.

#### Scenario: AcceptedMemberName
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The requested name has an accepted suffix
- **WHEN** The audit reads the named member
- **THEN** The member contents are returned

#### Scenario: RejectedMemberName
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The requested name does not have an accepted suffix
- **WHEN** The audit validates the name
- **THEN** A ValueError is raised

### Sub-component: RecordPredicate

> Implements: `predicate`

Determines whether a code satisfies the audit's record-selection condition.

#### Requirement: EvaluateCode

The system SHALL the audit must evaluate whether the supplied code satisfies the record-selection condition.

#### Scenario: CodeSatisfiesCondition
- **GIVEN** A code satisfies the selection condition
- **WHEN** The audit evaluates the predicate
- **THEN** The result indicates that the condition is satisfied

#### Scenario: CodeFailsCondition
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A code does not satisfy the selection condition
- **WHEN** The audit evaluates the predicate
- **THEN** The result indicates that the condition is not satisfied

### Sub-component: ReadLine

> Implements: `raw.readline`

Reads the next line from the raw input stream during record processing.

#### Requirement: ReadNextLine

The system SHALL the audit must read and return the next line from the raw input stream.

#### Scenario: LineAvailable
- **GIVEN** The raw input stream has another line
- **WHEN** The audit reads a line
- **THEN** The next line is returned

### Sub-component: HashUpdate

> Implements: `h.update`

Adds input bytes to the active digest during integrity checking.

#### Requirement: UpdateDigest

The system SHALL the audit must incorporate the supplied bytes into the active digest.

#### Scenario: AddBytes
- **GIVEN** Bytes are available for integrity checking
- **WHEN** The audit updates the digest
- **THEN** The digest reflects the supplied bytes

### Sub-component: DecodeFirst

> Implements: `first.decode`

Decodes the first byte sequence into text for audit processing.

#### Requirement: DecodeFirstBytes

The system SHALL the audit must decode the first byte sequence into text.

#### Scenario: ValidFirstBytes
- **GIVEN** The first byte sequence is valid for the selected encoding
- **WHEN** The audit decodes it
- **THEN** The corresponding text is returned

### Sub-component: DecodeLine

> Implements: `line.decode`

Decodes a byte line into text for audit processing.

The system SHALL the audit must decode the byte line into text.

#### Scenario: ValidLineBytes
- **GIVEN** The byte line is valid for the selected encoding
- **WHEN** The audit decodes it
- **THEN** The corresponding text is returned

### Sub-component: AppendPicked

> Implements: `picked.append`

Adds a selected record to the collection of picked records.

The system SHALL the audit must add each selected record to the picked-record collection.

#### Scenario: SelectedRecord
- **GIVEN** A record satisfies the selection criteria
- **WHEN** The audit appends the record
- **THEN** The record is present in the picked collection

### Sub-component: DigestHex

> Implements: `h.hexdigest`

Returns the active digest as a hexadecimal string for integrity comparison.

- **WHEN** The audit requests the hexadecimal digest
- **THEN** The digest is returned as a hexadecimal string

> `source/exvius/raid/raid_model.py`

### Requirement: Simulate

The system SHALL the model shall replay each supplied turn's damage and optional break-point damage, reporting remaining hp, break state, clear status, and aggregate damage. it shall reject invalid hp, turn traces, damage values, or break-gauge settings.

- **Implementation**: `simulate::source/exvius/raid/raid_model.py`

#### Scenario: ReplayATraceWithoutABreakGauge
- **GIVEN** A positive starting HP and a non-empty list of valid damage turns
- **WHEN** simulate is called
- **THEN** It returns per-turn remaining HP, whether the trace cleared the target, turns used, total submitted
damage, and overkill

#### Scenario: ApplyBreakGaugeTiming
- **GIVEN** A valid break gauge and turns with BP damage sufficient to deplete it
- **WHEN** simulate replays the trace
- **THEN** It triggers a break when the gauge reaches zero and tracks the configured broken turns and
subsequent gauge value

#### Scenario: RejectInvalidTraceInput
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** HP, turn rows, damage, or break-gauge values violate the model's integer or range requirements, or
the trace is empty or exceeds 10000 turns
- **WHEN** simulate is called
- **THEN** It raises a validation error

### Requirement: SoloTarget

The system SHALL the model shall calculate a locally authored raid-pool recommendation as damage per attempt multiplied by desired attempts, requiring both inputs to be positive integers.

- **Implementation**: `solo_target::source/exvius/raid/raid_model.py`

#### Scenario: CalculateASoloTarget
- **GIVEN** Positive integer damage per attempt and desired attempt count
- **WHEN** solo_target is called
- **THEN** It returns their product

#### Scenario: RejectNonPositiveInputs
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Either input is not an integer or is less than one
- **WHEN** solo_target is called
- **THEN** It raises a validation error

> `source/exvius/restore_event_battles.py`

### Requirement: RunEventBattleReconstruction

The system SHALL reconstruct event battle content, wire the resulting bundles into the project document, and save the updated document.

#### Scenario: ReconstructionCompletes
- **GIVEN** Required source data and the project document are available
- **WHEN** main is called
- **THEN** Reconstructed battle bundles are wired into the document and the updated document is saved

### Requirement: RecoverSkills

The system SHALL the service shall recover skill records associated with a skill set and optionally restrict recovery to missing skills.

#### Scenario: RecoverSkillsForASet
- **GIVEN** A skill set identifier has associated recoverable skill data
- **WHEN** Skills are recovered for the set
- **THEN** The service SHALL return the associated skill records

#### Scenario: RecoverOnlyMissingSkills
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A skill set has both present and missing skills
- **WHEN** Recovery is requested with only_missing enabled
- **THEN** The service SHALL return only skills that are missing

### Requirement: SelectBattleRecords

The system SHALL the service shall select battle records that match the requested enemy names, waves, campaign, and mission context.

#### Scenario: SelectMatchingRecords
- **GIVEN** Battle records include entries matching requested names and waves
- **WHEN** Records are selected for a mission
- **THEN** The service SHALL return records matching the mission context

#### Scenario: NoMatchingRecords
- **GIVEN** No battle record matches the requested mission context
- **WHEN** Records are selected
- **THEN** The selection SHALL contain no unrelated battle records

### Requirement: BuildEnemyProfile

The system SHALL the service shall construct an enemy profile using matching battle data and any recovered boss specifications and skills.

#### Scenario: BuildProfileFromMatchingRecord
- **GIVEN** A matching battle record and enemy identity are available
- **WHEN** An enemy profile is built
- **THEN** The profile SHALL include applicable recovered combat data

#### Scenario: BuildProfileWithBossSpecifications
- **GIVEN** Boss specifications are available for the enemy
- **WHEN** The enemy profile is built
- **THEN** The profile SHALL incorporate the applicable boss specifications

### Requirement: BuildBattleBundle

The system SHALL the service shall assemble a mission battle bundle containing the mission phases, enemy profiles, and associated battle details.

#### Scenario: AssembleBundleFromMissionData
- **GIVEN** Mission data and selected battle records are available
- **WHEN** A battle bundle is built
- **THEN** The bundle SHALL include the applicable phases and enemy profiles

#### Scenario: IncludeRecoverableSkillData
- **GIVEN** The selected battle records reference recoverable skills
- **WHEN** A battle bundle is built
- **THEN** The bundle SHALL include the recovered skill data

### Requirement: PreserveFormationRecord

The system SHALL the service shall preserve a wave roster when its formation is known but its combat profiles are unavailable.

#### Scenario: PreserveKnownRosterWithProfileGaps
- **GIVEN** A wave roster is preserved and some combat profiles are absent
- **WHEN** A formation record is created
- **THEN** The record SHALL retain the preserved roster and represent the profile gap

### Requirement: CreateLegacyRecord

The system SHALL the service shall create a legacy battle record for a mission from the available legacy enemy names.

#### Scenario: CreateRecordFromLegacyNames
<!-- openlore-test: priority=low tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A mission identifier and legacy enemy names are available
- **WHEN** A legacy record is created
- **THEN** The record SHALL associate the names with the mission

### Requirement: ParseBattleRecords

The system SHALL the service shall extract structured battle records from a source page's content.

#### Scenario: ParseStructuredRecords
- **GIVEN** The source page contains battle record data
- **WHEN** The records are parsed
- **THEN** The service SHALL return the extracted structured records

#### Scenario: PageHasNoRecords
- **GIVEN** The source page contains no recognizable battle records
- **WHEN** The records are parsed
- **THEN** The service SHALL return an empty record collection

### Requirement: ParseBossSpecifications

The system SHALL the service shall extract structured boss specifications from supplied text.

#### Scenario: ParseAvailableBossSpecifications
- **GIVEN** Text contains recognizable boss specification data
- **WHEN** Boss specifications are parsed
- **THEN** The service SHALL return the extracted specifications

#### Scenario: NoBossSpecificationsPresent
- **GIVEN** Text contains no recognizable boss specifications
- **WHEN** Boss specifications are parsed
- **THEN** The service SHALL return no specifications

### Requirement: ReadTable

The system SHALL load and parse a named project data table.

- **Implementation**: `read_table::source/exvius/restore_event_battles.py`

#### Scenario: TableIsAvailable
- **GIVEN** A named table exists in the project data
- **WHEN** read_table is called with that name
- **THEN** The parsed table data is returned

### Requirement: LoadMorganaBattleData

The system SHALL reconstruct morgana's lgd battle data using the original global graphics and related battle records.

- **Implementation**: `morgana_lgd::source/exvius/restore_event_battles.py`

#### Scenario: MorganaDataIsReconstructed
- **GIVEN** The required battle tables and Global graphics archive are available
- **WHEN** morgana_lgd is called
- **THEN** Morgana's LGD battle data is assembled from the source records

### Requirement: LoadReconstructionTable

The system SHALL provide named table data to the battle reconstruction process.

#### Scenario: RequestedTableIsAvailable
- **GIVEN** The reconstruction source contains the requested table
- **WHEN** load is called with its name
- **THEN** The corresponding table data is returned

### Requirement: ReconstructStages

The system SHALL reconstruct event mission stages while preserving the established lottery, group, monster-part, and skill assembly chain.

- **Implementation**: `reconstructed_stages::source/exvius/restore_event_battles.py`

#### Scenario: StagesAreAssembledFromSourceRecords
- **GIVEN** LGD battle data and its related tables are available
- **WHEN** reconstructed_stages is called
- **THEN** Stages are derived with their lottery, group, monster-part, and skill data

### Requirement: ReconstructFollowers

The system SHALL reconstruct the recorded four event battle waves using the original global graphics.

- **Implementation**: `followers::source/exvius/restore_event_battles.py`

#### Scenario: FourWavesAreReconstructed
- **GIVEN** The event battle records and original Global graphics are available
- **WHEN** followers is called
- **THEN** The four recorded waves are reconstructed from the source data

### Requirement: PreserveKingMogCounterattack

The system SHALL reconstruct king mog's counterattack using original global boss stats while preserving its existing model, ai, and skill set.

- **Implementation**: `king_mog_counterattack::source/exvius/restore_event_battles.py`

#### Scenario: CounterattackRetainsOriginalBossData
- **GIVEN** The project document and original Global boss records are available
- **WHEN** king_mog_counterattack is called
- **THEN** The counterattack uses the original boss stats and retains its model, AI, and skills


## Sub-components

> `EventBattleReconstructionService` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: SourceIndex

> Implements: `IndexSource`

Provides indexed access to reconstruction source data.

- **WHEN** the index is used
- **THEN** the indexed data is available

- **WHEN** an absent key is accessed
- **THEN** the default value is supplied

Normalizes a value into a key suitable for matching.

- **WHEN** key is called
- **THEN** a normalized key is returned

- **WHEN** the item is requested
- **THEN** the next item is returned

- **THEN** first occurrences are retained in order

Removes leading and trailing whitespace from an entry.

- **THEN** leading whitespace is removed

> `source/exvius/recover_catalogue_content.py`

### Requirement: ValidateArchive

The system SHALL the service shall determine whether a package is a valid archive that can be inspected.

- **Implementation**: `valid::source/exvius/recover_catalogue_content.py`

#### Scenario: ReadableArchive
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A path points to a package that can be loaded and whose archive entries can be inspected
- **WHEN** The package is validated
- **THEN** The service reports it as valid

#### Scenario: InvalidArchive
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A path does not point to a loadable archive
- **WHEN** The package is validated
- **THEN** The service reports it as invalid

### Requirement: ImportJpArchive

The system SHALL the service shall retain original packages from the preserved jp archive when their checksums match the catalogue metadata.

- **Implementation**: `import_jp_archive::source/exvius/recover_catalogue_content.py`

#### Scenario: VerifiedPackage
- **GIVEN** Metadata identifies a package in the preserved archive and its checksums match
- **WHEN** The archive import runs
- **THEN** The original package is retained and the manifest is updated

#### Scenario: ChecksumMismatch
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A candidate package does not match the metadata checksums
- **WHEN** The archive import runs
- **THEN** The package is not accepted as a verified import

### Requirement: RecoverCatalogueContent

The system SHALL the service shall process catalogue metadata and downloaded files to recover, stage, and record package imports and aliases.

#### Scenario: CatalogueRecovery
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Catalogue metadata, a manifest, and candidate downloads are available
- **WHEN** The recovery command runs
- **THEN** Eligible packages are staged or imported, rejected candidates are recorded, and the manifest and log
are written

#### Scenario: MissingCandidateFiles
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Some catalogue entries have no available candidate file
- **WHEN** The recovery command runs
- **THEN** Those entries are excluded from successful imports and handled in the recovery results


## Sub-components

> `JP Archive Catalogue Recovery` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: Path

> Implements: `Path`

Resolves filesystem paths used during archive recovery.

#### Requirement: ResolvePath

The system SHALL the recovery process shall represent supplied locations as filesystem paths.

#### Scenario: PathInput
- **GIVEN** A filesystem location is supplied
- **WHEN** The recovery process constructs a Path
- **THEN** A path representing that location is available

### Sub-component: Valid

> Implements: `valid`

Determines whether a candidate package is a valid archive package.

#### Requirement: ValidatePackage

The system SHALL the validator shall determine package validity using the package loader and archive file information.

#### Scenario: ValidArchivePackage
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The path identifies a readable valid package
- **WHEN** valid(path) is called
- **THEN** The package is reported as valid

#### Scenario: InvalidArchivePackage
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The path does not identify a valid package
- **WHEN** valid(path) is called
- **THEN** The package is reported as invalid

### Sub-component: ValueError

> Implements: `ValueError`

Represents invalid values encountered while processing archive data.

#### Requirement: SignalInvalidValue

The system SHALL the recovery process shall treat a valueerror as an invalid-value condition during processing.

#### Scenario: InvalidValueEncountered
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Archive data cannot be interpreted as a permitted value
- **WHEN** The condition is raised
- **THEN** A ValueError is signaled

### Sub-component: JsonLoads

> Implements: `json.loads`

Parses JSON text used by archive metadata and manifests.

#### Requirement: ParseJson

The system SHALL the process shall parse valid json text into its corresponding data value.

#### Scenario: ValidJson
- **GIVEN** The input is valid JSON text
- **WHEN** json.loads is called
- **THEN** The corresponding JSON value is returned

#### Scenario: MalformedJson
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The input is malformed JSON text
- **WHEN** json.loads is called
- **THEN** A parsing error is raised
