## Sub-components

> `ClashBattleRestoreService` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: Reconstruction

> Implements: `Reconstruction`

Reconstructs battle data by coordinating field parsing, template matching, and value normalization.

#### Requirement: ReconstructBattleData

The system SHALL the reconstruction block shall coordinate the available parsing and normalization functions to produce reconstructed battle data from its inputs.

#### Scenario: ReconstructsFromValidInputs
- **GIVEN** Valid source data and reference templates are available
- **WHEN** The reconstruction block is invoked
- **THEN** It produces reconstructed battle data.

### Sub-component: Fields

> Implements: `fields`

Extracts field names and values from a block of text.

#### Requirement: ExtractFields

The system SHALL the fields function shall return parsed field names and values from the supplied block.

#### Scenario: ParsesWhitespacePaddedFields
- **GIVEN** A block contains field names and values with surrounding whitespace
- **WHEN** fields is called with the block
- **THEN** It returns the field names and values with surrounding whitespace removed.

### Sub-component: Templates

> Implements: `templates`

Finds template occurrences associated with a specified name in text.

#### Requirement: FindNamedTemplates

The system SHALL the templates function shall identify template occurrences in the supplied text that match the requested name.

#### Scenario: FindsMatchingTemplate
- **GIVEN** Text contains a template occurrence for the requested name
- **WHEN** templates is called with the text and name
- **THEN** It returns the matching template occurrence.

### Sub-component: Deepcopy

> Implements: `deepcopy`

Creates an independent copy of source data for safe reconstruction.

#### Requirement: CopySourceData

The system SHALL the deepcopy operation shall provide a copy whose mutations do not change the source data.

#### Scenario: CopyCanBeChangedIndependently
- **GIVEN** Source data is provided
- **WHEN** The returned copy is modified
- **THEN** The source data remains unchanged.

### Sub-component: Next

> Implements: `next`

Retrieves the next item from an iteration used during reconstruction.

#### Requirement: RetrieveNextItem

The system SHALL the next operation shall return the next available item from its iterator.

#### Scenario: ReturnsAvailableItem
- **GIVEN** The iterator contains another item
- **WHEN** next is invoked
- **THEN** It returns that item.

### Sub-component: Numeric

> Implements: `numeric`

Determines whether a value represents a plain numeric value.

#### Requirement: RecognizeNumericValue

The system SHALL the numeric function shall identify values that conform to the accepted plain numeric form.

#### Scenario: AcceptsNumericText
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The value is text in the accepted numeric form
- **WHEN** numeric is called with the value
- **THEN** It reports that the value is numeric.

### Sub-component: Family

> Implements: `family`

Determines the family associated with battle data during reconstruction.

#### Requirement: IdentifyDataFamily

The system SHALL the family operation shall return the family associated with the supplied battle data.

#### Scenario: IdentifiesKnownFamily
- **GIVEN** Battle data belongs to a recognized family
- **WHEN** The family operation is invoked
- **THEN** It returns the associated family.

### Sub-component: ValueError

> Implements: `ValueError`

Represents invalid values encountered while reconstructing battle data.

#### Requirement: SignalInvalidValue

The system SHALL the valueerror condition shall signal that a supplied value cannot be accepted for the operation.

#### Scenario: InvalidValueSignalsFailure
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** An operation receives a value it cannot accept
- **WHEN** The invalid value is processed
- **THEN** A ValueError condition is raised.

### Sub-component: Key

> Implements: `key`

Normalizes a value into a key suitable for matching battle data.

#### Requirement: NormalizeKey

The system SHALL the key function shall return the normalized key for the supplied value.

#### Scenario: NormalizesKeyInput
- **GIVEN** A value requiring normalization is supplied
- **WHEN** key is called with the value
- **THEN** It returns the normalized key.

### Sub-component: JsonLoads

> Implements: `json.loads`

Parses serialized JSON source data for battle reconstruction.

#### Requirement: ParseJsonSource

The system SHALL the json.loads operation shall parse valid json text into its corresponding data structure.

#### Scenario: ParsesValidJson
- **GIVEN** Valid JSON text is supplied
- **WHEN** json.loads is invoked
- **THEN** It returns the corresponding data structure.

### Sub-component: PGet

> Implements: `p.get`

Retrieves an optional value from the profile data.

#### Requirement: RetrieveProfileValue

The system SHALL the p.get operation shall retrieve the value associated with the requested key when present.

#### Scenario: ReturnsPresentProfileValue
- **GIVEN** The profile contains the requested key
- **WHEN** p.get is called for that key
- **THEN** It returns the associated value.

### Sub-component: ReadTargetText

> Implements: `target.read_text`

Reads source text from the target path for reconstruction.

#### Requirement: ReadTargetSource

The system SHALL the target.read_text operation shall return the text stored at the target path.

#### Scenario: ReadsAvailableTargetText
- **GIVEN** The target path contains readable text
- **WHEN** target.read_text is invoked
- **THEN** It returns the stored text.

### Sub-component: ReferenceGet

> Implements: `reference.get`

Retrieves a requested value from the reference data.

#### Requirement: RetrieveReferenceValue

The system SHALL the reference.get operation shall retrieve the value associated with the requested key when present.

#### Scenario: ReturnsPresentReferenceValue
- **GIVEN** The reference data contains the requested key
- **WHEN** reference.get is called for that key
- **THEN** It returns the associated value.

### Sub-component: Text

> Implements: `text`

Normalizes page content into text suitable for battle data parsing.

#### Requirement: ExtractPageText

The system SHALL the text function shall return normalized text derived from the supplied page.

#### Scenario: NormalizesPageMarkup
- **GIVEN** A page contains markup and readable content
- **WHEN** text is called with the page
- **THEN** It returns normalized text for parsing.

### Sub-component: SGet

> Implements: `s.get`

Retrieves an optional value from the source data.

#### Requirement: RetrieveSourceValue

The system SHALL the s.get operation shall retrieve the value associated with the requested key when present.

#### Scenario: ReturnsPresentSourceValue
- **GIVEN** The source data contains the requested key
- **WHEN** s.get is called for that key
- **THEN** It returns the associated value.

### Sub-component: RowGet

> Implements: `row.get`

Retrieves an optional value from a battle data row.

#### Requirement: RetrieveRowValue

The system SHALL the row.get operation shall retrieve the value associated with the requested key when present.

#### Scenario: ReturnsPresentRowValue
- **GIVEN** The row contains the requested key
- **WHEN** row.get is called for that key
- **THEN** It returns the associated value.

### Sub-component: RegexSearch

> Implements: `re.search`

Searches battle source text for a pattern used during reconstruction.

#### Requirement: SearchSourcePattern

The system SHALL the re.search operation shall report a match when the supplied pattern occurs in the source text.

#### Scenario: FindsPresentPattern
- **GIVEN** The source text contains a match for the pattern
- **WHEN** re.search is invoked
- **THEN** It returns a match result.

### Sub-component: SpecItems

> Implements: `spec.items`

Provides the specification entries used to guide battle data reconstruction.

#### Requirement: EnumerateSpecificationEntries

The system SHALL the spec.items operation shall provide the key and value pairs contained in the specification.

#### Scenario: EnumeratesAvailableEntries
- **GIVEN** A specification contains entries
- **WHEN** spec.items is invoked
- **THEN** It provides those entries as key and value pairs.

### Sub-component: ProfileUpdate

> Implements: `profile.update`

Applies reconstructed values to the profile data.

#### Requirement: UpdateProfile

The system SHALL the profile.update operation shall apply the supplied values to the profile.

#### Scenario: AppliesProfileValues
- **GIVEN** A profile and a set of values are supplied
- **WHEN** profile.update is invoked
- **THEN** The profile contains the supplied values.

### Sub-component: RegexSub

> Implements: `re.sub`

Replaces matching text in source values during normalization.

#### Requirement: ReplaceMatchingText

The system SHALL the re.sub operation shall replace text matching the supplied pattern according to the replacement value.

#### Scenario: ReplacesPatternMatches
- **GIVEN** Source text contains a match for the supplied pattern
- **WHEN** re.sub is invoked
- **THEN** It returns text with the matching portion replaced.

### Sub-component: Bundle

> Implements: `bundle`

Constructs a restored battle bundle from mission, record, names, and campaign data.

#### Requirement: ConstructBattleBundle

The system SHALL the bundle function shall produce a battle bundle from the supplied battle identifiers and data.

#### Scenario: ConstructsBundleFromInputs
- **GIVEN** Mission, record, names, and campaign data are supplied
- **WHEN** bundle is called
- **THEN** It returns a battle bundle containing reconstructed data.

### Sub-component: TitleReplace

> Implements: `title.replace`

Replaces a matched portion of a title while preparing reconstructed data.

#### Requirement: ReplaceTitleText

The system SHALL the title.replace operation shall return the title with the requested text replaced.

#### Scenario: ReplacesTitleContent
- **GIVEN** The title contains the text to replace
- **WHEN** title.replace is invoked
- **THEN** It returns the title with that text replaced.

### Sub-component: Sha256

> Implements: `hashlib.sha256`

Computes a SHA-256 digest for reconstructed battle data.

#### Requirement: ComputeDataDigest

The system SHALL the hashlib.sha256 operation shall produce a digest for the supplied data.

#### Scenario: ProducesDigest
- **GIVEN** Data bytes are supplied
- **WHEN** hashlib.sha256 is invoked
- **THEN** It returns a SHA-256 hash object for those bytes.

### Sub-component: RestoredAppend

> Implements: `restored.append`

Adds a reconstructed battle bundle to the restored results.

#### Requirement: AppendRestoredBundle

The system SHALL the restored.append operation shall add the supplied bundle to the restored results.

#### Scenario: AddsBundleToResults
- **GIVEN** A restored results collection and a battle bundle are available
- **WHEN** restored.append is invoked with the bundle
- **THEN** The collection includes the bundle.

### Sub-component: WriteTargetText

> Implements: `target.write_text`

Writes serialized reconstructed data to the target path.

#### Requirement: WriteReconstructedText

The system SHALL the target.write_text operation shall write the supplied text to the target path.

#### Scenario: WritesTextToTarget
- **GIVEN** The target path is writable and text is supplied
- **WHEN** target.write_text is invoked
- **THEN** The supplied text is stored at the target path.

### Sub-component: JsonDumps

> Implements: `json.dumps`

Serializes reconstructed data into JSON text.

#### Requirement: SerializeDataAsJson

The system SHALL the json.dumps operation shall serialize data into json text.

#### Scenario: SerializesData
- **GIVEN** Data suitable for JSON serialization is supplied
- **WHEN** json.dumps is invoked
- **THEN** It returns JSON text representing the data.

> `source/exvius/restore_exbonus.py`

### Requirement: RestoreExbonusData

The system SHALL the service shall reconstruct exbonus data by combining recovered and original tables, resolving references, and collecting related unit, skill, and item information.

#### Scenario: ReconstructDataFromAvailableSources
- **GIVEN** Recovered tables, original tables, and client assets are available
- **WHEN** The restoration operation runs
- **THEN** It combines the relevant records, translates references, and writes reconstructed output with
provenance

#### Scenario: ReportUnresolvedReferences
- **GIVEN** A reconstructed record contains a reference that cannot be resolved
- **WHEN** The restoration operation processes that record
- **THEN** The unresolved reference is recorded for review


## Sub-components

> `Exbonus Restoration` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: ClientManifest

> Implements: `client_manifest`

Extracts the client manifest from an APK and resolves its referenced assets.

#### Requirement: ExtractClientManifest

The system SHALL the function must locate and decode the client manifest from the supplied apk, raising a value error when the required manifest cannot be found or read.

#### Scenario: ManifestAvailable
- **GIVEN** An APK contains a readable client manifest
- **WHEN** The manifest is requested
- **THEN** The function returns decoded manifest data with referenced asset details.

#### Scenario: ManifestUnavailable
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The APK does not contain a usable client manifest
- **WHEN** The manifest is requested
- **THEN** The function raises a value error.

### Sub-component: RecoverTable

> Implements: `recovered`

Decodes recovered table data and records its provenance.

#### Requirement: DecodeRecoveredTable

The system SHALL the function must decode the supplied table and append provenance that identifies the recovered data.

#### Scenario: TableRecovered
- **GIVEN** A recoverable table is supplied
- **WHEN** The table is recovered
- **THEN** Decoded data is returned and provenance is appended.

### Sub-component: LoadOriginal

> Implements: `original`

Loads an original named asset from a supplied pack.

#### Requirement: RetrieveOriginalAsset

The system SHALL the function must retrieve the asset identified by name from the supplied pack.

#### Scenario: AssetExists
- **GIVEN** The pack contains the named asset
- **WHEN** The original asset is requested
- **THEN** The asset is returned.

### Sub-component: InitializeQueue

> Implements: `next`

Obtains the next item used by the orchestrator's processing sequence.

#### Requirement: GetNextItem

The system SHALL the operation must obtain the next item from the processing sequence when one is available.

#### Scenario: ItemAvailable
- **GIVEN** The processing sequence contains an item
- **WHEN** The next item is requested
- **THEN** The next item is provided.

### Sub-component: CreateQueue

> Implements: `deque`

Provides a double-ended queue for orchestrator work items.

#### Requirement: CreateWorkQueue

The system SHALL the operation must provide a queue capable of holding work items for subsequent processing.

#### Scenario: QueueCreated
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The orchestrator needs to schedule work
- **WHEN** A queue is created
- **THEN** A work queue is available.

### Sub-component: IterateManifest

> Implements: `manifest.items`

Enumerates the entries in the client manifest.

#### Requirement: EnumerateManifestEntries

The system SHALL the operation must provide each manifest entry for processing.

#### Scenario: ManifestHasEntries
- **GIVEN** The manifest contains entries
- **WHEN** Its items are iterated
- **THEN** Each entry is made available.

### Sub-component: GetRecordField

> Implements: `r.get`

Reads an optional field from a record.

#### Requirement: ReadOptionalField

The system SHALL the operation must return the requested field value when present and the designated default when absent.

#### Scenario: FieldMissing
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A record lacks the requested field
- **WHEN** The field is read with a default
- **THEN** The default value is returned.

### Sub-component: GetUnit

> Implements: `units.get`

Looks up a unit by its identifier.

#### Requirement: LookupUnit

The system SHALL the operation must return the unit associated with the requested identifier when present.

#### Scenario: UnitExists
- **GIVEN** The unit identifier is present in the unit collection
- **WHEN** The unit is looked up
- **THEN** The corresponding unit is returned.

### Sub-component: RecordAdjustment

> Implements: `adjustments.append`

Adds an adjustment to the accumulated adjustment list.

#### Requirement: AppendAdjustment

The system SHALL the operation must add the supplied adjustment to the adjustment collection.

#### Scenario: AdjustmentAdded
- **GIVEN** An adjustment is available
- **WHEN** It is appended
- **THEN** The adjustment is present in the collection.

### Sub-component: RecordProvenance

> Implements: `provenance.append`

Adds a provenance record to the accumulated provenance list.

#### Requirement: AppendProvenance

The system SHALL the operation must add the supplied provenance record to the provenance collection.

#### Scenario: ProvenanceAdded
- **GIVEN** A provenance record is available
- **WHEN** It is appended
- **THEN** The record is present in the collection.

### Sub-component: CollectNeededSkills

> Implements: `needed_skills.update`

Adds discovered skill identifiers to the required skill collection.

#### Requirement: UpdateNeededSkills

The system SHALL the operation must include all supplied skill identifiers in the required skill collection.

#### Scenario: SkillsDiscovered
- **GIVEN** New skill identifiers are discovered
- **WHEN** The required skills are updated
- **THEN** All discovered identifiers are included.

### Sub-component: RemovePendingItem

> Implements: `pending.popleft`

Removes and returns the next pending item.

#### Requirement: DequeuePendingItem

The system SHALL the operation must remove and return the earliest pending item when the queue is nonempty.

#### Scenario: PendingItemsExist
- **GIVEN** The pending queue contains items
- **WHEN** The next item is removed
- **THEN** The earliest item is returned and removed.

### Sub-component: MarkVisited

> Implements: `visited.add`

Marks an item as visited.

#### Requirement: AddVisitedItem

The system SHALL the operation must record the supplied item in the visited collection.

#### Scenario: ItemVisited
- **GIVEN** An item has been processed
- **WHEN** It is added to visited
- **THEN** The visited collection contains the item.

### Sub-component: EnumerateRowValues

> Implements: `row.values`

Enumerates the values contained in a row.

#### Requirement: GetRowValues

The system SHALL the operation must provide the values present in the supplied row.

#### Scenario: RowHasValues
- **GIVEN** A row contains values
- **WHEN** Its values are enumerated
- **THEN** Each row value is made available.

### Sub-component: FindReferences

> Implements: `re.findall`

Finds all occurrences of a pattern in text.

#### Requirement: FindAllMatches

The system SHALL the operation must return every non-overlapping match for the supplied pattern and text.

#### Scenario: MultipleMatches
- **GIVEN** The text contains multiple pattern matches
- **WHEN** All matches are found
- **THEN** Each match is returned.

### Sub-component: EnqueueItem

> Implements: `pending.append`

Adds an item to the end of the pending work queue.

#### Requirement: AppendPendingItem

The system SHALL the operation must place the supplied item at the end of the pending queue.

#### Scenario: ItemQueued
- **GIVEN** A new item requires processing
- **WHEN** It is appended to pending
- **THEN** It is available at the queue's end.

### Sub-component: UpdateUnitCaptions

> Implements: `unit_captions.update`

Adds unit caption mappings to the accumulated caption collection.

#### Requirement: MergeUnitCaptions

The system SHALL the operation must include the supplied unit caption mappings in the caption collection.

#### Scenario: CaptionsDiscovered
- **GIVEN** Unit caption mappings are discovered
- **WHEN** The caption collection is updated
- **THEN** The mappings are included.

### Sub-component: UpdateEnglish

Adds English text mappings to the accumulated translations.

The system SHALL the operation must include the supplied english mappings in the translation collection.

#### Scenario: TranslationsDiscovered
- **GIVEN** English mappings are discovered
- **WHEN** The translation collection is updated
- **THEN** The mappings are included.

### Sub-component: IterateAdditions

> Implements: `additions.items`

Enumerates entries in the additions collection.

The system SHALL the operation must provide every key and value pair in the additions collection.

#### Scenario: AdditionsExist
- **GIVEN** The additions collection contains entries
- **WHEN** Its items are iterated
- **THEN** Each key and value pair is provided.

### Sub-component: TrimQualifier

Removes trailing whitespace from a qualifier.

The system SHALL the operation must return the qualifier without trailing whitespace.

#### Scenario: QualifierHasTrailingWhitespace
- **GIVEN** A qualifier ends in whitespace
- **WHEN** Trailing whitespace is removed
- **THEN** The result has no trailing whitespace.

### Sub-component: MakeRelativePath

> Implements: `datamine.relative_to`

Resolves a datamine path relative to a base path.

The system SHALL the operation must return the datamine path relative to the supplied base path when it is contained beneath that base.

#### Scenario: PathUnderBase
- **GIVEN** The datamine path is beneath the base path
- **WHEN** A relative path is requested
- **THEN** The path relative to the base is returned.

### Sub-component: HashData

> Implements: `hashlib.sha256`

Computes a SHA-256 digest for data.

#### Requirement: ComputeSha256

The system SHALL the operation must produce a sha-256 digest for the supplied data.

#### Scenario: DataSupplied
- **GIVEN** Data is available
- **WHEN** Its SHA-256 digest is computed
- **THEN** A digest identifying the data is produced.

### Sub-component: ReadDatamine

> Implements: `datamine.read_bytes`

Reads the datamine file as bytes.

#### Requirement: ReadDatamineBytes

The system SHALL the operation must return the contents of the datamine file as bytes.

#### Scenario: DatamineReadable
- **GIVEN** The datamine file exists and is readable
- **WHEN** Its bytes are read
- **THEN** The file contents are returned as bytes.

### Sub-component: OpenArchive

Opens a ZIP archive for inspection.

- **WHEN** The archive is opened
- **THEN** An archive handle is available.

Lists the member names in an archive.

- **WHEN** Member names are listed
- **THEN** All member names are returned.

Checks whether a name ends with a specified suffix.

The system SHALL the operation must indicate whether the supplied name ends with the requested suffix.

- **WHEN** The suffix is checked
- **THEN** The result indicates a match.

### Sub-component: MergeMinedData

Adds mined data to the accumulated mined collection.

- **GIVEN** Mined entries are available
- **WHEN** The mined collection is updated
- **THEN** The entries are included.

Parses JSON text into a data value.

- **THEN** The corresponding data value is returned.

- **THEN** The substitutions are included.

#### Scenario: NewFilenameSupplied

#### Scenario: UnsupportedSuffix
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The requested name ends with an unsupported suffix
- **WHEN** The file is read
- **THEN** A value error is raised.

#### Scenario: SupportedName
- **GIVEN** The requested name has a supported suffix
- **WHEN** The file is read
- **THEN** Its content is returned.

> `source/exvius/restore_kira.py`

### Requirement: RestoreEvent

The system SHALL the service shall derive the event mission and battle data from source tables and update the event encounter and catalogue records.

- **Implementation**: `restore_event::source/exvius/restore_kira.py`

#### Scenario: RestoreEventBattleData
- **GIVEN** The event source tables and existing event records are available
- **WHEN** restore_event is called
- **THEN** The event mission, phases, monsters, parts, drops, and skill data are derived and the encounter and
catalogue records are updated


## Sub-components

> `Event Data Restoration Service` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: ClientManifest

> Implements: `client_manifest`

Extracts client manifest data and related identifiers from an APK.

#### Requirement: ExtractManifest

The system SHALL the block must read the apk manifest and derive the required manifest identifiers.

#### Scenario: ValidApk
- **GIVEN** A readable APK containing a valid manifest
- **WHEN** client_manifest is called
- **THEN** It returns the manifest-derived data.

#### Scenario: InvalidApk
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** An APK that cannot be read or has an invalid manifest
- **WHEN** client_manifest is called
- **THEN** It raises an error indicating the failure.

### Sub-component: Next

> Implements: `next`

Selects the next available item from an iterable.

#### Requirement: SelectNext

The system SHALL the block must return the next item from the provided iterator.

#### Scenario: NonemptyIterator
- **GIVEN** An iterator containing at least one item
- **WHEN** next is called
- **THEN** The first available item is returned.

#### Scenario: EmptyIterator
- **GIVEN** An exhausted iterator
- **WHEN** next is called
- **THEN** The iterator's exhaustion behavior is observed.

### Sub-component: Decode

> Implements: `decode`

Decodes encrypted or encoded records into structured data, optionally handling localized records.

#### Requirement: DecodeRecords

The system SHALL the block must decode supported input records and return valid structured records, preserving multiple records when present.

#### Scenario: MultipleRecords
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Raw input containing multiple valid encoded records
- **WHEN** decode is called
- **THEN** All valid records are returned in order.

#### Scenario: InvalidInput
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Raw input that cannot be decoded with the supplied key
- **WHEN** decode is called
- **THEN** The documented validation error is raised.

### Sub-component: Path

> Implements: `Path`

Constructs a filesystem path from the supplied path value.

#### Requirement: ConstructPath

The system SHALL the block should represent the supplied path as a path object.

#### Scenario: PathValueProvided
- **GIVEN** A path-like value
- **WHEN** Path is called
- **THEN** A path object representing that value is produced.

### Sub-component: LoadingText

> Implements: `loading_text`

Restores loading text data and applies requested text replacements.

#### Requirement: RestoreLoadingText

The system SHALL the block must produce target loading text using source data, key, and requested replacements, optionally appending missing entries.

#### Scenario: ReplacementRequested
- **GIVEN** Source loading text and a replacement mapping
- **WHEN** loading_text is called
- **THEN** The target contains the requested replacement text.

#### Scenario: AppendMissingEnabled
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A replacement key missing from the source and append_missing is true
- **WHEN** loading_text is called
- **THEN** The missing entry is included in the target.

### Sub-component: Ndjson

> Implements: `ndjson`

Serializes rows as newline-delimited JSON.

#### Requirement: SerializeRows

The system SHALL the block must serialize each input row as one json value on its own line.

#### Scenario: MultipleRows
- **GIVEN** A sequence containing multiple rows
- **WHEN** ndjson is called
- **THEN** The output contains one JSON record per row.

#### Scenario: EmptyRows
- **GIVEN** An empty sequence
- **WHEN** ndjson is called
- **THEN** The output contains no records.

### Sub-component: LocalizedTextVersions

> Implements: `localized_text_versions`

Restores localized text versions using the localized-text index to select the source file.

#### Requirement: RestoreLocalizedVersions

The system SHALL the block must select the localized text file through its localized-text index and restore the requested versions.

#### Scenario: LocalizedIndexAvailable
- **GIVEN** A localized-text index and matching source data
- **WHEN** localized_text_versions is called
- **THEN** The indexed localized source is restored to the target.

#### Scenario: GeneralVersionTableDiffers
- **GIVEN** A general version table that points elsewhere than the localized-text index
- **WHEN** localized_text_versions is called
- **THEN** The localized-text index determines file selection.

### Sub-component: ExbonusMaster

> Implements: `exbonus_master`

Restores the EX bonus master data using the supplied source, table, and optional restored data.

#### Requirement: RestoreExbonusMaster

The system SHALL the block must produce target ex bonus master data from the supplied inputs.

#### Scenario: SourceAndTableProvided
- **GIVEN** A readable source and an EX bonus table
- **WHEN** exbonus_master is called
- **THEN** The target contains the restored master data.

#### Scenario: RestoredDataProvided
- **GIVEN** Previously restored data is supplied
- **WHEN** exbonus_master is called
- **THEN** The supplied data is accounted for in the target output.

### Sub-component: MemorialReadBytes

> Implements: `memorial.read_bytes`

Reads the memorial data file as bytes.

#### Requirement: ReadMemorialBytes

The system SHALL the block must return the bytes stored in the memorial file.

#### Scenario: MemorialFileExists
- **GIVEN** A readable memorial file
- **WHEN** read_bytes is called
- **THEN** Its bytes are returned.

#### Scenario: MemorialFileMissing
<!-- openlore-test: priority=high tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The memorial file does not exist
- **WHEN** read_bytes is called
- **THEN** A file access error is raised.

### Sub-component: ChallengeItems

> Implements: `challenges.items`

Provides the challenge mapping's key-value entries.

#### Requirement: EnumerateChallenges

The system SHALL the block must expose the challenge mapping entries for iteration.

#### Scenario: ChallengesPresent
- **GIVEN** A mapping with challenge entries
- **WHEN** items is called
- **THEN** Its key-value entries are available.

### Sub-component: JsonLoads

> Implements: `json.loads`

Parses JSON text into a structured value.

#### Requirement: ParseJson

The system SHALL the block must parse valid json text into its corresponding value.

#### Scenario: ValidJson
- **GIVEN** Valid JSON text
- **WHEN** loads is called
- **THEN** The corresponding structured value is returned.

#### Scenario: MalformedJson
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Malformed JSON text
- **WHEN** loads is called
- **THEN** A JSON parsing error is raised.

- **GIVEN** An abilities mapping with entries
- **WHEN** items is called
- **THEN** Its key-value entries are available.

Provides the limit-break descriptions mapping's key-value entries.

- **THEN** Its key-value entries are available.

Reads the current data file as bytes.

Creates a temporary directory context for intermediate work.

- **GIVEN** A request to create a temporary directory
- **WHEN** TemporaryDirectory is invoked
- **THEN** A usable temporary directory is provided.

### Sub-component: ZipFile

> Implements: `zipfile.ZipFile`

Opens or creates a ZIP archive for data access.

The system SHALL the block must provide archive access for the requested file and mode.

#### Scenario: ValidArchive
- **GIVEN** A valid archive path and supported mode
- **WHEN** ZipFile is invoked
- **THEN** An archive object is returned.

#### Scenario: InvalidArchive
<!-- openlore-test: priority=high tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** An unreadable or malformed archive
- **WHEN** ZipFile is invoked
- **THEN** An archive access error is raised.

### Sub-component: ArchiveWrite

> Implements: `archive.write`

Adds a filesystem file to an archive.

#### Requirement: AddFileToArchive

The system SHALL the block must add the specified file to the archive under the requested archive name.

#### Scenario: ReadableSource
- **GIVEN** A readable source file and open archive
- **WHEN** write is called
- **THEN** The file is present in the archive.

- **THEN** Its key-value entries are available.

Checks whether the source path identifies a regular file.

- **GIVEN** The source path identifies a regular file
- **WHEN** is_file is called
- **THEN** True is returned.

#### Scenario: NonFile
- **GIVEN** The source path identifies no regular file
- **WHEN** is_file is called
- **THEN** False is returned.

- **WHEN** as_posix is called
- **THEN** A slash-separated path string is returned.

- **THEN** The archive contains the named entry with that data.

- **GIVEN** The native location contains paths matching the pattern
- **WHEN** glob is called
- **THEN** The matching paths are returned.

#### Scenario: NoMatches
- **GIVEN** No paths match the pattern
- **WHEN** glob is called
- **THEN** An empty result is returned.

- **WHEN** read_bytes is called
- **THEN** Its bytes are returned.

### Sub-component: RowsGet

> Implements: `rows.get`

Retrieves a value from the rows mapping by key with optional fallback.

#### Requirement: GetRowValue

The system SHALL the block must return the value associated with the requested key or the supplied default when absent.

#### Scenario: KeyPresent
- **GIVEN** The key exists in rows
- **WHEN** get is called
- **THEN** Its associated value is returned.

#### Scenario: KeyAbsent
- **GIVEN** The key is absent from rows
- **WHEN** get is called
- **THEN** The default value is returned.

- **WHEN** stat is called
- **THEN** Its filesystem metadata is returned.

### Sub-component: Original

> Implements: `original`

Loads a named original file from a packed archive.

#### Scenario: MemberExists
- **GIVEN** The pack contains the requested member
- **WHEN** original is called
- **THEN** A stream of that member's bytes is returned.

#### Scenario: MemberMissing
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The pack does not contain the requested member
- **WHEN** original is called
- **THEN** The missing-member behavior is reported.

> `source/exvius/restore_kira.py`

### Requirement: Build

The system SHALL the service shall assemble the required event data, assets, and metadata into the requested output package.

#### Scenario: BuildEventPackage
- **GIVEN** Required source data and client assets are available
- **WHEN** build is called with an output path
- **THEN** The service assembles the event artifacts and writes the package to the output path

### Requirement: BuildBanner

The system SHALL the service shall create the event banner from original artwork and save it with updated metadata.

- **Implementation**: `build_banner::source/exvius/restore_kira.py`

#### Scenario: CreateEventBanner
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The original banner artwork is available
- **WHEN** build_banner is called
- **THEN** The event banner is composed, saved, and its metadata is updated

### Requirement: SerializeNdjson

The system SHALL the service shall serialize rows as newline-delimited json for inclusion in generated event data.

- **Implementation**: `ndjson::source/exvius/restore_kira.py`

#### Scenario: SerializeRows
- **GIVEN** A collection of data rows is provided
- **WHEN** ndjson is called
- **THEN** Each row is serialized as a JSON record on its own line


## Sub-components

> `Event Artifact Build Service` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: ClientManifest

> Implements: `client_manifest`

Extracts and validates the client manifest from an APK.

#### Requirement: ExtractClientManifest

The system SHALL the service shall read the manifest data from the supplied apk and return the extracted client manifest.

#### Scenario: ManifestAvailable
- **GIVEN** A readable APK contains a manifest matching the expected format
- **WHEN** client_manifest processes the APK
- **THEN** The extracted manifest data is returned

#### Scenario: InvalidApk
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The supplied APK cannot be read as a valid archive
- **WHEN** client_manifest processes the APK
- **THEN** The operation reports a validation or read error

### Sub-component: Next

> Implements: `next`

Selects the next available item from an iterable.

#### Requirement: SelectNextItem

The system SHALL the operation shall return the next item from the supplied iterator.

#### Scenario: ItemAvailable
- **GIVEN** The iterator contains at least one item
- **WHEN** next is called
- **THEN** The first available item is returned

#### Scenario: IteratorExhausted
- **GIVEN** The iterator contains no further items
- **WHEN** next is called
- **THEN** The operation reports iterator exhaustion

### Sub-component: Decode

> Implements: `decode`

Decodes encrypted or encoded records into usable data records.

#### Requirement: DecodeRecords

The system SHALL the decoder shall convert supported raw input into records and preserve all valid records, including records from supported padded multi-record input.

#### Scenario: MultipleRecords
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Raw input contains multiple valid padded CBC records
- **WHEN** decode processes the input
- **THEN** All valid records are returned

#### Scenario: InvalidInput
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Raw input cannot be decoded using the supplied key
- **WHEN** decode processes the input
- **THEN** The operation reports a decoding error

#### Scenario: LocalizedRecords
- **GIVEN** Localized mode is enabled and the input contains valid localized records
- **WHEN** decode processes the input
- **THEN** The localized records are returned

### Sub-component: Path

> Implements: `Path`

Represents a filesystem path for path-based operations.

#### Requirement: ConstructPath

The system SHALL the operation shall provide a path value representing the supplied path input.

#### Scenario: ValidPath
- **GIVEN** A path string is supplied
- **WHEN** Path is constructed
- **THEN** A path value representing that string is available

### Sub-component: LoadingText

> Implements: `loading_text`

Creates or updates loading text using decoded source data and optional replacements.

#### Requirement: BuildLoadingText

The system SHALL the operation shall produce target loading text from the source and key, apply supplied replacements, and append missing entries when requested.

#### Scenario: ApplyReplacements
- **GIVEN** The source contains valid loading text and replacements are supplied
- **WHEN** loading_text builds the target
- **THEN** The target reflects the replacements

#### Scenario: AppendMissingEntries
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Append-missing is enabled and the source contains entries absent from the target
- **WHEN** loading_text builds the target
- **THEN** The missing entries are included

### Sub-component: Ndjson

> Implements: `ndjson`

Serializes rows as newline-delimited JSON.

#### Requirement: SerializeRows

The system SHALL the operation shall serialize each supplied row as a json value in newline-delimited form.

#### Scenario: MultipleRows
- **GIVEN** A collection contains multiple rows
- **WHEN** ndjson serializes the rows
- **THEN** The output contains one JSON representation per row on separate lines

#### Scenario: EmptyRows
- **GIVEN** The collection contains no rows
- **WHEN** ndjson serializes the rows
- **THEN** The output contains no row entries

### Sub-component: LocalizedTextVersions

> Implements: `localized_text_versions`

Builds the localized-text version file selected by the localized-text index.

#### Requirement: BuildLocalizedTextVersions

The system SHALL the operation shall build the localized-text version output from the supplied source, target, key, and replacements; the localized-text index shall determine which file is selected.

#### Scenario: LocalizedIndexSelection
- **GIVEN** The localized-text index selects a file and replacement data is available
- **WHEN** localized_text_versions builds the output
- **THEN** The selected file receives the localized-text version data

### Sub-component: ExbonusMaster

> Implements: `exbonus_master`

Builds the EX bonus master output from source data and a table.

#### Requirement: BuildExbonusMaster

The system SHALL the operation shall create the target ex bonus master using the supplied source, key, table, and optional restored data.

#### Scenario: BuildWithRestoredData
- **GIVEN** Source data and a table are available, with restored data optionally supplied
- **WHEN** exbonus_master builds the target
- **THEN** The target contains the resulting EX bonus master data

### Sub-component: MemorialReadBytes

> Implements: `memorial.read_bytes`

Reads the memorial file as bytes.

#### Requirement: ReadMemorialBytes

The system SHALL the operation shall return the byte contents of the memorial file.

#### Scenario: ReadableMemorial
- **GIVEN** The memorial file exists and is readable
- **WHEN** memorial.read_bytes is called
- **THEN** The file bytes are returned

### Sub-component: ChallengesItems

> Implements: `challenges.items`

Provides the challenge collection's items.

#### Requirement: GetChallengeItems

The system SHALL the operation shall provide the challenge collection's entries.

#### Scenario: ChallengeEntriesAvailable
- **GIVEN** The challenges collection contains entries
- **WHEN** challenges.items is accessed
- **THEN** The entries are available for processing

### Sub-component: JsonLoads

Parses JSON text into a data value.

#### Requirement: ParseJson

The system SHALL the operation shall parse valid json text into its corresponding data value.

#### Scenario: ValidJson
- **GIVEN** The input is valid JSON text
- **WHEN** json.loads parses the input
- **THEN** The corresponding data value is returned

#### Scenario: InvalidJson
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The input is malformed JSON text
- **WHEN** json.loads parses the input
- **THEN** The operation reports a parsing error

> `source/exvius/restore_vision_battles.py`

### Requirement: SplitPageSections

The system SHALL the utility shall split a source page into sections that can be processed independently.

- **Implementation**: `sections::source/exvius/restore_vision_battles.py`

#### Scenario: PageWithSectionHeaders
- **GIVEN** A page contains multiple section headers and their content
- **WHEN** sections is called
- **THEN** It returns the page content divided into corresponding sections

### Requirement: BuildBattleRecord

The system SHALL the utility shall derive a battle record for a named page and optional stage from reconstruction data.

- **Implementation**: `record_for::source/exvius/restore_vision_battles.py`

#### Scenario: MatchingPageAndStage
- **GIVEN** Reconstruction data contains content matching the requested page, name, and stage
- **WHEN** record_for is called
- **THEN** It returns the reconstructed battle record

#### Scenario: NoMatchingContent
- **GIVEN** Reconstruction data contains no content matching the requested page and name
- **WHEN** record_for is called
- **THEN** It returns no battle record

### Requirement: RestoreVisionBattles

The system SHALL the utility shall load source and reference data, identify recoverable dark visions battles, and persist the reconstructed results.

#### Scenario: RecoverBattleRecords
- **GIVEN** Source pages and supporting reconstruction data are available
- **WHEN** main is called
- **THEN** It reconstructs eligible battle records and writes the results

#### Scenario: NoEligibleRecords
- **GIVEN** Source data contains no eligible battle records
- **WHEN** main is called
- **THEN** It writes the resulting empty or unchanged restoration output


## Sub-components

> `Vision Battle Restoration Utility` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: Sections

> Implements: `sections`

Extracts named text sections from a source page.

#### Requirement: ExtractSections

The system SHALL the function shall identify section headers and return the corresponding section text from the page.

#### Scenario: PageWithSections
- **GIVEN** A page contains recognizable section headers and text
- **WHEN** sections is called with the page
- **THEN** The returned sections contain the text associated with each recognized header.

### Sub-component: Fields

> Implements: `fields`

Parses field names and values from a text block.

#### Requirement: ParseFields

The system SHALL the function shall extract field names and values from the supplied block, trimming surrounding whitespace.

#### Scenario: BlockWithFields
- **GIVEN** A block contains field names and values with optional surrounding whitespace
- **WHEN** fields is called with the block
- **THEN** The returned fields contain the parsed names and values without surrounding whitespace.

### Sub-component: Templates

> Implements: `templates`

Finds template-like spans associated with a supplied name in text.

#### Requirement: FindTemplates

The system SHALL the function shall locate and return template spans matching the supplied name in the text.

#### Scenario: TextWithMatchingTemplate
- **GIVEN** Text contains a template matching the supplied name
- **WHEN** templates is called
- **THEN** The matching template span is included in the result.

### Sub-component: Key

> Implements: `key`

Normalizes a value into a lookup key.

#### Requirement: NormalizeKey

The system SHALL the function shall convert the supplied value into its normalized key representation.

#### Scenario: ValueWithFormatting
- **GIVEN** A value contains characters or formatting handled by key normalization
- **WHEN** key is called
- **THEN** The result is the normalized lookup key for that value.

### Requirement: HitTestPoint

The system SHALL the service shall determine whether a point lies within a supplied box.

- **Implementation**: `containsPoint::source/exvius/summon_menu.js`

#### Scenario: PointIsInsideTheBox
- **GIVEN** A point within the box bounds
- **WHEN** containsPoint is called
- **THEN** The result indicates a hit

#### Scenario: PointIsOutsideTheBox
- **GIVEN** A point outside the box bounds
- **WHEN** containsPoint is called
- **THEN** The result indicates no hit

### Requirement: FindFirstHit

The system SHALL the service shall return the first box hit by any supplied point.

- **Implementation**: `firstHit::source/exvius/summon_menu.js`

#### Scenario: APointHitsABox
- **GIVEN** Boxes and points with at least one intersection
- **WHEN** firstHit is called
- **THEN** The first matching box is returned

#### Scenario: NoPointsHitABox
- **GIVEN** Boxes and points with no intersections
- **WHEN** firstHit is called
- **THEN** No matching box is returned

### Requirement: LayoutDropdown

The system SHALL the service shall calculate dropdown menu item layout from a header, groups, and row height.

- **Implementation**: `dropdownLayout::source/exvius/summon_menu.js`

#### Scenario: DropdownHasGroupedItems
- **GIVEN** A header and one or more item groups
- **WHEN** dropdownLayout is called
- **THEN** A layout is returned for the header and grouped items

#### Scenario: DropdownUsesDefaultRowHeight
- **GIVEN** A header and groups without an explicit row height
- **WHEN** dropdownLayout is called
- **THEN** The layout uses a row height of 28

### Requirement: InstallSummonMenu

The system SHALL the service shall install the summon menu behavior and connect it to the game module, bridge, retained resources, and optional home carousel.

- **Implementation**: `installSummonMenu::source/exvius/summon_menu.js`

#### Scenario: InstallMenuIntegration
- **GIVEN** A game module, bridge, and retained resource collection
- **WHEN** installSummonMenu is called
- **THEN** Menu hooks and behavior are installed and required resources are retained

#### Scenario: InstallWithHomeCarousel
- **GIVEN** A game module and an available home carousel
- **WHEN** installSummonMenu is called
- **THEN** Touch navigation can coordinate with the home carousel

> `source/exvius/title_menu.js`

### Requirement: InstallTitleMenu

The system SHALL the title menu customization shall shorten publisher-logo transitions, relabel info and restore as new game and continue, hide online-only controls, and suppress the account-binding tutorial when the relevant hooks are available.

- **Implementation**: `installTitleMenu::source/exvius/title_menu.js`

#### Scenario: TitleHooksInstall
- **GIVEN** All required title exports are available
- **WHEN** installTitleMenu is called
- **THEN** The title controls are relabeled, online-only controls are suppressed, and LOCAL_TITLE_READY is
reported

#### Scenario: RequiredTitleExportMissing
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A required title export cannot be found
- **WHEN** installTitleMenu is called
- **THEN** Title customization stops and a LOCAL_TITLE_DISABLED note identifies the missing export

#### Scenario: NewGameSelection
- **GIVEN** The Info button is hit
- **WHEN** The title touch handler processes the selection
- **THEN** The new-game bridge action is called and the original button hit is suppressed

#### Scenario: NewGameBridgeFailure
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The Info button is hit and the bridge action fails
- **WHEN** The title touch handler processes the selection
- **THEN** The save remains unchanged and the original Info press is retained

#### Scenario: ContinueSelection
- **GIVEN** The Restore button is hit
- **WHEN** The title touch handler processes the selection
- **THEN** LOCAL_TITLE_CONTINUE is reported and the original full-screen start flow proceeds

#### Scenario: OnlineOnlyControls
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The title or options layout requests login options, support, or delete-account controls
- **WHEN** The menu hooks process the requests
- **THEN** Those controls are not created

#### Scenario: AccountTutorial
- **GIVEN** The options menu requests the account-binding tutorial record
- **WHEN** The picture-story lookup runs
- **THEN** The tutorial record is suppressed and LOCAL_OPTIONS_ACCOUNT_TUTORIAL_SUPPRESSED is reported

> `source/exvius/verify_content.py`

### Requirement: FindDevelopmentPackages

The system SHALL the service shall identify original editor packages that contain no mission or map-introduction reference.

- **Implementation**: `development_packages::source/exvius/verify_content.py`

#### Scenario: PackageHasNoMissionOrMapIntroductionReference
- **GIVEN** A package is an original editor package with neither reference
- **WHEN** development_packages is called
- **THEN** The package is included in the returned collection

### Requirement: ExtractTextIds

The system SHALL the service shall extract text identifiers from supplied text data.

- **Implementation**: `text_ids::source/exvius/verify_content.py`

#### Scenario: TextDataContainsIdentifiers
- **GIVEN** The input contains text identifier rows
- **WHEN** text_ids is called
- **THEN** The identifiers are returned

### Requirement: LoadCommonTellers

The system SHALL the service shall load localized rows for common teller captions.

- **Implementation**: `common_tellers::source/exvius/verify_content.py`

#### Scenario: CommonTellerCaptionsAreAvailable
- **GIVEN** The relevant content package is readable
- **WHEN** common_tellers is called
- **THEN** Localized teller rows are returned

### Requirement: LoadCommonNpcNames

The system SHALL the service shall provide english native npc captions, including the generated localized extension.

- **Implementation**: `common_npc_names::source/exvius/verify_content.py`

#### Scenario: NPCCaptionsIncludeLocalizedExtension
- **GIVEN** The caption data and generated extension are available
- **WHEN** common_npc_names is called
- **THEN** English native NPC captions and localized extension labels are returned

### Requirement: VerifyContent

The system SHALL the service shall inspect content packages and report detected content or integrity issues.

#### Scenario: ContentPackagesPassVerification
- **GIVEN** The configured packages contain valid content
- **WHEN** main is called
- **THEN** A verification report is written with issue totals

#### Scenario: ContentIssuesAreDetected
- **GIVEN** A package contains invalid or missing content
- **WHEN** main is called
- **THEN** The issues are included in the report and summarized in the totals


## Sub-components

> `ContentVerificationService` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: ImmutableSetCreation

> Implements: `frozenset`

Creates an immutable set of values for membership checks.

#### Requirement: CreateImmutableSet

The system SHALL the block must provide an immutable set containing the supplied values.

#### Scenario: ValuesAreFrozen
- **GIVEN** A collection of distinct values
- **WHEN** The set is created
- **THEN** The result contains each supplied value and does not permit mutation.

### Sub-component: AnyMatch

> Implements: `any`

Determines whether at least one value in an iterable satisfies a condition.

#### Requirement: CheckForMatchingValue

The system SHALL the block must report whether any value in the iterable is truthy.

#### Scenario: MatchingValueExists
- **GIVEN** An iterable containing at least one truthy value
- **WHEN** The iterable is checked
- **THEN** The result is true.

### Sub-component: TextRows

> Implements: `text_rows`

Reads identifier and text rows from a Memorial id,text table.

#### Requirement: ParseTextTable

The system SHALL the block shall decode table data and return parsed identifier and text rows.

#### Scenario: ValidRowsAreParsed
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Encoded table data with id,text lines
- **WHEN** The table is read
- **THEN** Each valid line yields its identifier and text.

### Sub-component: LocalizedRows

> Implements: `localized_rows`

Reads English and other-language cells while preserving empty cells.

#### Requirement: ParseLocalizedTable

The system SHALL the block shall return localized cells without dropping empty values.

#### Scenario: EmptyCellIsPreserved
- **GIVEN** A row containing an empty language cell
- **WHEN** The localized table is read
- **THEN** The returned row retains that empty cell.

### Sub-component: SceneScoped

> Implements: `scene_scoped`

Determines whether a speaker name belongs to a scene-scoped category.

#### Requirement: CheckSceneSpeaker

The system SHALL the block must identify speakers whose names begin with the scene prefix.

#### Scenario: ScenePrefixMatches
- **GIVEN** A speaker name beginning with the scene prefix
- **WHEN** The speaker is checked
- **THEN** The result indicates that the speaker is scene-scoped.

### Sub-component: MovieRead

> Implements: `movie.read`

Reads movie resource data for content verification.

#### Requirement: ReadMovieResource

The system SHALL the block shall provide the requested movie resource data for inspection.

#### Scenario: MovieResourceAvailable
- **GIVEN** A readable movie resource
- **WHEN** The resource is read
- **THEN** Its data is returned for verification.

### Sub-component: PathReadBytes

> Implements: `path.read_bytes`

Reads a file's contents as bytes.

#### Requirement: ReadFileBytes

The system SHALL the block shall return the bytes stored at the requested path.

#### Scenario: ExistingFileRead
- **GIVEN** A path to an existing readable file
- **WHEN** Its bytes are read
- **THEN** The file contents are returned.

### Sub-component: CpkLoad

> Implements: `cpk.load`

Loads a CPK package for inspection.

#### Requirement: LoadPackage

The system SHALL the block shall make the contents of a cpk package available for verification.

#### Scenario: ValidPackageLoaded
- **GIVEN** Valid CPK package data
- **WHEN** The package is loaded
- **THEN** An archive suitable for inspection is returned.

### Sub-component: ArchiveFileInfos

> Implements: `archive.file_infos`

Retrieves metadata for files contained in an archive.

#### Requirement: ListArchiveFiles

The system SHALL the block shall expose file metadata for archive members.

#### Scenario: ArchiveHasMembers
- **GIVEN** An archive containing files
- **WHEN** Its file information is requested
- **THEN** Metadata for the contained files is returned.

### Sub-component: MemberItems

> Implements: `members.items`

Provides the key and value pairs in the member mapping.

#### Requirement: IterateMembers

The system SHALL the block shall expose each member name and its associated value.

#### Scenario: MembersAreEnumerated
- **GIVEN** A mapping with multiple members
- **WHEN** Its items are requested
- **THEN** Each member pair is available.

### Sub-component: NameEndsWith

> Implements: `name.endswith`

Checks whether a name has a specified suffix.

#### Requirement: CheckNameSuffix

The system SHALL the block must determine whether the name ends with the requested suffix.

#### Scenario: MatchingSuffix
- **GIVEN** A name ending in the requested suffix
- **WHEN** The suffix is checked
- **THEN** The result is true.

### Sub-component: AppendIssue

> Implements: `issues.append`

Adds a validation issue to the issue collection.

#### Requirement: RecordIssue

The system SHALL the block shall add the specified issue to the collection.

#### Scenario: IssueIsRecorded
- **GIVEN** An issue and an issue collection
- **WHEN** The issue is appended
- **THEN** The collection includes the issue.

### Sub-component: ArchiveFileBytes

> Implements: `archive.file_bytes`

Retrieves the bytes for a named archive member.

#### Requirement: ReadArchiveMember

The system SHALL the block shall return the contents of the requested archive member.

#### Scenario: MemberExists
- **GIVEN** An archive containing the requested member
- **WHEN** Its bytes are requested
- **THEN** The member contents are returned.

### Sub-component: DigitCheck

> Implements: `i.isdigit`

Checks whether a string consists only of digits.

#### Requirement: CheckDigits

The system SHALL the block must identify strings composed entirely of digits.

#### Scenario: NumericString
- **GIVEN** A nonempty string containing only digits
- **WHEN** The string is checked
- **THEN** The result is true.

### Sub-component: BaseKeys

> Implements: `base.keys`

Provides the keys in the base mapping.

#### Requirement: ListBaseKeys

The system SHALL the block shall expose the keys present in the base mapping.

#### Scenario: MappingHasKeys
- **GIVEN** A base mapping with keys
- **WHEN** Its keys are requested
- **THEN** The mapping's keys are returned.

### Sub-component: SgKeys

> Implements: `sg.keys`

Provides the keys in the sg mapping.

#### Requirement: ListSgKeys

The system SHALL the block shall expose the keys present in the sg mapping.

#### Scenario: MappingHasKeys
- **GIVEN** An sg mapping with keys
- **WHEN** Its keys are requested
- **THEN** The mapping's keys are returned.

### Sub-component: BaseItems

> Implements: `base.items`

Provides key and value pairs from the base mapping.

#### Requirement: IterateBaseEntries

The system SHALL the block shall expose each key and value in the base mapping.

#### Scenario: BaseEntriesAreEnumerated
- **GIVEN** A base mapping with entries
- **WHEN** Its items are requested
- **THEN** Each key and value pair is available.

### Sub-component: StripText

> Implements: `text.strip`

Removes leading and trailing whitespace from text.

#### Requirement: TrimText

The system SHALL the block shall return text without leading or trailing whitespace.

#### Scenario: SurroundingWhitespaceRemoved
- **GIVEN** Text with surrounding whitespace
- **WHEN** The text is stripped
- **THEN** The result has no leading or trailing whitespace.

### Sub-component: SgItems

> Implements: `sg.items`

Provides key and value pairs from the sg mapping.

#### Requirement: IterateSgEntries

The system SHALL the block shall expose each key and value in the sg mapping.

#### Scenario: SgEntriesAreEnumerated
- **GIVEN** An sg mapping with entries
- **WHEN** Its items are requested
- **THEN** Each key and value pair is available.

### Sub-component: RegexSearch

> Implements: `re.search`

Finds the first match of a pattern in text.

#### Requirement: SearchPattern

The system SHALL the block must determine whether the pattern matches the supplied text.

#### Scenario: PatternFound
- **GIVEN** Text containing the requested pattern
- **WHEN** The text is searched
- **THEN** A matching result is returned.

### Sub-component: RegexSubstitute

> Implements: `re.sub`

Replaces pattern matches in text.

#### Requirement: ReplacePattern

The system SHALL the block shall return text with pattern matches replaced by the replacement value.

#### Scenario: MatchingTextReplaced
- **GIVEN** Text containing a pattern match
- **WHEN** The substitution is applied
- **THEN** The match is replaced with the requested value.

### Sub-component: SgValues

> Implements: `sg.values`

Provides the values in the sg mapping.

#### Requirement: ListSgValues

The system SHALL the block shall expose all values in the sg mapping.

#### Scenario: SgValuesAreAvailable
- **GIVEN** An sg mapping with values
- **WHEN** Its values are requested
- **THEN** The mapping's values are returned.

### Sub-component: RegexFindAll

> Implements: `re.findall`

Finds all occurrences of a pattern in text.

#### Requirement: FindAllMatches

The system SHALL the block shall return all matches of the pattern in the supplied text.

#### Scenario: MultipleMatches
- **GIVEN** Text containing multiple pattern matches
- **WHEN** All matches are requested
- **THEN** Each matching occurrence is returned.

### Sub-component: MemberGet

> Implements: `members.get`

Retrieves a member value by key from the members mapping.

#### Requirement: GetMember

The system SHALL the block shall return the value for a key when present and the default when absent.

#### Scenario: MissingMemberUsesDefault
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A key absent from the members mapping
- **WHEN** Its value is requested with a default
- **THEN** The default value is returned.

### Sub-component: NameReplace

> Implements: `name.replace`

Replaces occurrences of a substring in a name.

#### Requirement: ReplaceNameText

The system SHALL the block shall return the name with occurrences of the requested substring replaced.

#### Scenario: SubstringIsReplaced
- **GIVEN** A name containing the old substring
- **WHEN** The replacement is applied
- **THEN** The name contains the new substring in its place.

### Sub-component: SceneItems

> Implements: `scene.items`

Provides key and value pairs from the scene mapping.

#### Requirement: IterateSceneEntries

The system SHALL the block shall expose each key and value in the scene mapping.

#### Scenario: SceneEntriesAreEnumerated
- **GIVEN** A scene mapping with entries
- **WHEN** Its items are requested
- **THEN** Each key and value pair is available.

### Sub-component: RejectBlankRowsAndCheckEnglishSpeakers

> Implements: `test_reject_blank_rows_and_check_english_speaker_names`

Verifies rejection of blank rows and validation of English speaker names.

#### Requirement: VerifyBlankRowsAndSpeakerNames

The system SHALL the block shall verify that blank rows are rejected and english speaker names are checked.

#### Scenario: BlankRowsRejected
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Input containing a blank row
- **WHEN** The validation test runs
- **THEN** The blank row is rejected.

#### Scenario: EnglishSpeakerChecked
- **GIVEN** An English speaker name
- **WHEN** The validation test runs
- **THEN** The speaker name is checked.

### Sub-component: Read

> Implements: `read`

Reads a named resource for content verification.

#### Requirement: ReadNamedResource

The system SHALL the block shall return the resource associated with the supplied name.

#### Scenario: ResourceIsRead
- **GIVEN** A valid resource name
- **WHEN** The resource is read
- **THEN** Its data is returned.

### Sub-component: CommonTellers

> Implements: `common_tellers`

Collects shared teller data from package content.

#### Requirement: CollectCommonTellers

The system SHALL the block shall provide common teller data from localized package resources.

#### Scenario: TellerDataAvailable
- **GIVEN** Packages containing localized teller data
- **WHEN** Common tellers are collected
- **THEN** The shared teller data is returned.

### Sub-component: CommonNpcNames

> Implements: `common_npc_names`

Collects English native NPC captions, including generated localized extensions.

#### Requirement: CollectCommonNpcNames

The system SHALL the block shall return english native npc captions and applicable generated localized captions.

#### Scenario: NativeAndLocalizedCaptionsIncluded
- **GIVEN** NPC label data with native and generated localized captions
- **WHEN** Common NPC names are collected
- **THEN** Both caption forms are included.

### Sub-component: WantedFiles

> Implements: `wanted_files`

Lists package directory, file, and version entries referenced by client resource tables.

#### Requirement: ListWantedPackages

The system SHALL the block shall return directory, file, and version tuples for packages listed by client resource tables.

#### Scenario: ListedPackageReturned
- **GIVEN** A client resource table referencing a package and version
- **WHEN** Wanted files are enumerated
- **THEN** The package directory, file, and version are returned.

### Sub-component: DeviceDir

> Implements: `device_dir`

Determines the app-files subfolder for a package directory.

#### Requirement: ResolveDeviceDirectory

The system SHALL the block shall return the folder beneath the app files directory, accounting for android sound banks.

#### Scenario: AndroidSoundBankDirectory
- **GIVEN** A directory for a CRI sound bank
- **WHEN** Its device directory is resolved
- **THEN** The android subfolder is identified.

### Sub-component: Next

> Implements: `next`

Retrieves the next item from an iterator.

#### Requirement: RetrieveNextItem

The system SHALL the block shall return the next available item from the iterator.

#### Scenario: IteratorHasItem
- **GIVEN** An iterator with an available item
- **WHEN** The next item is requested
- **THEN** That item is returned.

### Sub-component: Inspect

> Implements: `inspect`

Validates package contents, references, localized text, NPC captions, and package integrity.

#### Requirement: InspectContentPackage

The system SHALL the block must inspect package resources and report detected validation issues.

#### Scenario: InvalidPackageIssueReported
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A package containing an invalid reference or malformed content
- **WHEN** The package is inspected
- **THEN** At least one corresponding validation issue is reported.

### Sub-component: DevelopmentPackages

> Implements: `development_packages`

Lists original editor packages that lack mission or map-introduction references.

#### Requirement: ListDevelopmentPackages

The system SHALL the block shall identify original editor packages without mission or map-introduction references.

#### Scenario: UnreferencedEditorPackageIncluded
- **GIVEN** An original editor package with no mission or map-introduction reference
- **WHEN** Development packages are enumerated
- **THEN** The package is included.

### Sub-component: All

> Implements: `all`

Determines whether every value in an iterable satisfies a condition.

#### Requirement: CheckAllValues

The system SHALL the block must report whether all values in the iterable are truthy.

#### Scenario: AllValuesTruthy
- **GIVEN** An iterable containing only truthy values
- **WHEN** The iterable is checked
- **THEN** The result is true.

### Sub-component: Counter

> Implements: `collections.Counter`

Counts occurrences of values in an iterable.

#### Requirement: CountValues

The system SHALL the block shall return occurrence counts for values in the input iterable.

#### Scenario: RepeatedValueCounted
- **GIVEN** An iterable with a repeated value
- **WHEN** Occurrences are counted
- **THEN** The value's count equals its number of occurrences.

### Sub-component: ExtendCandidates

> Implements: `candidates.extend`

Adds multiple values to the candidates collection.

#### Requirement: AddCandidates

The system SHALL the block shall append each supplied value to the candidates collection.

#### Scenario: MultipleCandidatesAdded
- **GIVEN** A candidates collection and multiple values
- **WHEN** The values are extended into the collection
- **THEN** All supplied values appear in the collection.

### Sub-component: DirectoryStartsWith

> Implements: `directory.startswith`

Checks whether a directory name begins with a specified prefix.

#### Requirement: CheckDirectoryPrefix

The system SHALL the block must determine whether the directory begins with the requested prefix.

#### Scenario: MatchingPrefix
- **GIVEN** A directory beginning with the requested prefix
- **WHEN** The prefix is checked
- **THEN** The result is true.

### Sub-component: AppendCandidate

> Implements: `candidates.append`

Adds one value to the candidates collection.

#### Requirement: AddCandidate

The system SHALL the block shall append the supplied value to the candidates collection.

#### Scenario: CandidateAdded
- **GIVEN** A candidates collection and one candidate
- **WHEN** The candidate is appended
- **THEN** The collection includes the candidate.

### Sub-component: DirectorySplit

> Implements: `directory.split`

Splits a directory string into components.

#### Requirement: SplitDirectory

The system SHALL the block shall return directory components separated by the requested delimiter.

#### Scenario: DirectoryHasComponents
- **GIVEN** A directory containing the delimiter
- **WHEN** The directory is split
- **THEN** Its components are returned.

### Sub-component: IsFile

> Implements: `p.is_file`

Checks whether a path refers to a regular file.

#### Requirement: CheckRegularFile

The system SHALL the block must indicate whether the path refers to a regular file.

#### Scenario: RegularFileExists
- **GIVEN** A path referring to a regular file
- **WHEN** The file status is checked
- **THEN** The result is true.

### Sub-component: DictFromKeys

> Implements: `dict.fromkeys`

Creates a dictionary using supplied keys and a shared value.

#### Requirement: CreateDictionaryFromKeys

The system SHALL the block shall return a dictionary with each supplied key associated with the shared value.

#### Scenario: KeysMappedToSharedValue
- **GIVEN** A set of keys and a shared value
- **WHEN** The dictionary is created
- **THEN** Each key maps to the shared value.

### Sub-component: WriteOutput

> Implements: `out.write`

Writes supplied text to an output destination.

#### Requirement: WriteText

The system SHALL the block shall write the supplied text to the output destination.

#### Scenario: TextIsWritten
- **GIVEN** An output destination and text
- **WHEN** The text is written
- **THEN** The destination receives the text.

### Sub-component: JsonDumps

> Implements: `json.dumps`

Serializes a value as JSON text.

#### Requirement: SerializeJson

The system SHALL the block shall return a json representation of the supplied value.

#### Scenario: ValueSerialized
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A JSON-serializable value
- **WHEN** It is serialized
- **THEN** Valid JSON text is returned.

### Sub-component: TotalsValues

> Implements: `totals.values`

Provides the values in the totals mapping.

#### Requirement: ListTotalValues

The system SHALL the block shall expose all values in the totals mapping.

#### Scenario: TotalsAreAvailable
- **GIVEN** A totals mapping containing values
- **WHEN** Its values are requested
- **THEN** The mapping's values are returned.

## Mac Emulator Browser Integration

### Requirement: SupportPersistentHeadlessBoot

The macOS launcher SHALL preserve its visible boot default and SHALL support a
headless browser boot that reuses the existing named AVD and persistent userdata.
It SHALL NOT restart an already-running AVD to change display modes.

- **Implementation**: `Emulator::source/exvius/macos_emulator.py`

#### Scenario: BrowserBootPreservesUserdata
- **GIVEN** the configured Exvius AVD is stopped
- **WHEN** browser mode requests a headless boot
- **THEN** the launcher adds -no-window without wiping or recreating the AVD.

### Requirement: StopBrowserBeforeAndroid

The existing stop command SHALL stop the owned browser bridge before shutting
down the verified Exvius emulator. Other devices SHALL remain untouched.
Browser capture and authentication behavior are specified in browser-streaming.

- **Implementation**: `Emulator::source/exvius/macos_emulator.py`

#### Scenario: StopOwnedBridgeAndDevice
- **GIVEN** the browser bridge and named emulator are running
- **WHEN** the existing stop command is invoked
- **THEN** bridge shutdown precedes the emulator shutdown command.
